# Phase 27 donor-development behavioral projection

> Generated from the canonical Phase 27 story registry and normative volumes. Regenerate with its render-spec tool; do not edit independently. Complete REQ27 contracts and operating controls remain binding alongside these observable scenarios.

## ADDED Requirements

### Requirement: US27-P01 — Continue partnership work across successive requests and years

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to continue partnership work across successive requests and years, so that I can keep relationship history without starting over. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P01, REQ27-P02.

#### Scenario: US27-P01 AC27-P01-01

- **GIVEN** a Party and eligible ministry with no participant, Support Binding, gift, email address or public Page,
- **WHEN** authorized staff deliberately start ordinary work,
- **THEN** the identified relationship can be recorded without inventing any missing financial, public or participation fact.

#### Scenario: US27-P01 AC27-P01-02

- **GIVEN** one enrollment for a ministry with successive asks and annual renewal,
- **WHEN** the year, responsible worker, ministry display name or stage list changes,
- **THEN** the ordinary enrollment identity and earlier work remain; no new enrollment or fresh supporter assent is inferred.

#### Scenario: US27-P01 AC27-P01-03

- **GIVEN** a worker participates in two ministries and a church has a named committee contact,
- **WHEN** staff select a support relationship,
- **THEN** the actual Party and exact ministry are distinguishable; neither the worker login nor church contact silently substitutes for the selected subject.

#### Scenario: US27-P01 AC27-P01-04

- **GIVEN** a relationship is not yet attributable to an identified ministry,
- **WHEN** staff record an introduction or authorized task,
- **THEN** the useful Party/referral/task record is accepted through its owner without creating a cultivation enrollment or dummy ministry.

#### Scenario: US27-P01 AC27-P01-05

- **GIVEN** two Tenants or two Legal Entities have similarly named ministries,
- **WHEN** a command supplies a mismatched or hidden reference,
- **THEN** the command produces no cross-scope relationship or restricted identifying result; legitimate explicitly authorized multi-entity work uses each exact admitted scope.

#### Scenario: US27-P01 AC27-P02-01

- **GIVEN** an ordinary relationship includes prayer and monthly financial support,
- **WHEN** an equipment ask is declined while an annual increase remains pending,
- **THEN** the same ordinary enrollment retains the independent responses, commitments and nonfinancial relationship without an automatic closure or stage change.

#### Scenario: US27-P01 AC27-P02-02

- **GIVEN** a proposed additional enrollment differs only by staff, deadline, label, year, currency, confidentiality or stage list,
- **WHEN** the proposal is evaluated,
- **THEN** it cannot create an independent journey on that basis; the permitted request/task/responsibility or existing-work path is explained.

#### Scenario: US27-P01 AC27-P02-03

- **GIVEN** an actual intended-user case requires a distinct continuing outcome that simpler source records cannot represent,
- **WHEN** authorized staff deliberately admit that exception,
- **THEN** its separate purpose and concise reason are recorded without constructing a general purpose hierarchy or routine approval queue.

#### Scenario: US27-P01 AC27-P02-04

- **GIVEN** two commands use different labels or command IDs for the same ordinary meaning,
- **WHEN** they race with one another or with a pause,
- **THEN** one authoritative ordinary meaning remains and permitted reuse does not reset the existing work.

#### Scenario: US27-P01 AC27-P02-05

- **GIVEN** a Party correction would cause two ordinary enrollments to collide,
- **WHEN** the existing source owner processes the correction,
- **THEN** no history, grants or accepted effects are silently unioned and no fake purpose is created; unresolved meaning remains explicit for qualified correction.

### Requirement: US27-P02 — Identify the actual supporter and ministry

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to identify the actual supporter and ministry, so that I can avoid confusing a church contact or current worker with the relationship subject. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P01.

#### Scenario: US27-P02 AC27-P01-01

### Requirement: US27-P03 — Record useful early introductions before a ministry is known

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to record useful early introductions before a ministry is known, so that I can work without invented source data or premature enrollment. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P01, REQ27-P03.

#### Scenario: US27-P03 AC27-P01-01

- **GIVEN** a Party and eligible ministry with no participant, Support Binding, gift, email address or public Page,
- **WHEN** authorized staff deliberately start ordinary work,
- **THEN** the identified relationship can be recorded without inventing any missing financial, public or participation fact.

#### Scenario: US27-P03 AC27-P01-02

- **GIVEN** one enrollment for a ministry with successive asks and annual renewal,
- **WHEN** the year, responsible worker, ministry display name or stage list changes,
- **THEN** the ordinary enrollment identity and earlier work remain; no new enrollment or fresh supporter assent is inferred.

#### Scenario: US27-P03 AC27-P01-03

- **GIVEN** a worker participates in two ministries and a church has a named committee contact,
- **WHEN** staff select a support relationship,
- **THEN** the actual Party and exact ministry are distinguishable; neither the worker login nor church contact silently substitutes for the selected subject.

#### Scenario: US27-P03 AC27-P01-04

- **GIVEN** a relationship is not yet attributable to an identified ministry,
- **WHEN** staff record an introduction or authorized task,
- **THEN** the useful Party/referral/task record is accepted through its owner without creating a cultivation enrollment or dummy ministry.

#### Scenario: US27-P03 AC27-P01-05

- **GIVEN** two Tenants or two Legal Entities have similarly named ministries,
- **WHEN** a command supplies a mismatched or hidden reference,
- **THEN** the command produces no cross-scope relationship or restricted identifying result; legitimate explicitly authorized multi-entity work uses each exact admitted scope.

#### Scenario: US27-P03 AC27-P03-01

- **GIVEN** known authorized Party/ministry/purpose/work context exists,
- **WHEN** staff expressly start cultivation while recording the included referral or ask,
- **THEN** one understandable action accepts the specifically included source effects without asking for known information again.

#### Scenario: US27-P03 AC27-P03-02

- **GIVEN** a gift, score change, imported contact or introduction-only note arrives,
- **WHEN** the source fact is processed,
- **THEN** no new enrollment is admitted; separately qualified financial/service facts and existing Party/task work remain usable.

#### Scenario: US27-P03 AC27-P03-03

- **GIVEN** an ordinary enrollment already contains stages, owner and several open asks,
- **WHEN** a new start operation resolves to permitted reuse,
- **THEN** those existing facts remain unchanged and only separately requested authorized work is added.

#### Scenario: US27-P03 AC27-P03-04

- **GIVEN** paired fixtures differ only in whether a forbidden ordinary enrollment exists,
- **WHEN** a caller lacking minimum admission-result authority attempts entry,
- **THEN** observable availability, bounded response, counts and errors do not expose the hidden difference or admit a workaround duplicate.

#### Scenario: US27-P03 AC27-P03-05

- **GIVEN** a caller has limited reuse metadata authority but not history/edit authority,
- **WHEN** the command resolves existing work,
- **THEN** only that admitted metadata is returned and no edit, history, missionary or finance access is created.

### Requirement: US27-P04 — Manage prayer and financial participation together

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to manage prayer and financial participation together, so that I can respect the complete relationship without treating prayer as a fundraising stage. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P02, REQ27-P31.

#### Scenario: US27-P04 AC27-P02-01

- **GIVEN** an ordinary relationship includes prayer and monthly financial support,
- **WHEN** an equipment ask is declined while an annual increase remains pending,
- **THEN** the same ordinary enrollment retains the independent responses, commitments and nonfinancial relationship without an automatic closure or stage change.

#### Scenario: US27-P04 AC27-P02-02

- **GIVEN** a proposed additional enrollment differs only by staff, deadline, label, year, currency, confidentiality or stage list,
- **WHEN** the proposal is evaluated,
- **THEN** it cannot create an independent journey on that basis; the permitted request/task/responsibility or existing-work path is explained.

#### Scenario: US27-P04 AC27-P02-03

- **GIVEN** an actual intended-user case requires a distinct continuing outcome that simpler source records cannot represent,
- **WHEN** authorized staff deliberately admit that exception,
- **THEN** its separate purpose and concise reason are recorded without constructing a general purpose hierarchy or routine approval queue.

#### Scenario: US27-P04 AC27-P02-04

- **GIVEN** two commands use different labels or command IDs for the same ordinary meaning,
- **WHEN** they race with one another or with a pause,
- **THEN** one authoritative ordinary meaning remains and permitted reuse does not reset the existing work.

#### Scenario: US27-P04 AC27-P02-05

- **GIVEN** a Party correction would cause two ordinary enrollments to collide,
- **WHEN** the existing source owner processes the correction,
- **THEN** no history, grants or accepted effects are silently unioned and no fake purpose is created; unresolved meaning remains explicit for qualified correction.

#### Scenario: US27-P04 AC27-P31-01

- **GIVEN** the latest qualifying source fact or relevant obligation lies beyond all current display caps,
- **WHEN** the complete projection is computed,
- **THEN** the admitted total or latest fact includes that evidence or explicitly reports incomplete coverage, never a falsely complete capped result.

#### Scenario: US27-P04 AC27-P31-02

- **GIVEN** finance source coverage is absent or amounts use different currencies/periods and recognition meanings,
- **WHEN** a cultivation summary is displayed,
- **THEN** unknown is not zero and incomparable values are not summed, converted or represented as received cash.

#### Scenario: US27-P04 AC27-P31-03

- **GIVEN** one source gift or encounter appears in multiple allowed views,
- **WHEN** organization totals and source identity are reconciled,
- **THEN** the source fact is counted once for its admitted aggregate meaning and remains a reference rather than another financial or encounter record.

#### Scenario: US27-P04 AC27-P31-04

- **GIVEN** the newest source fact is corrected, restricted, merged or erased,
- **WHEN** derived views rebuild,
- **THEN** the result may move backward or become unknown; stale cached maxima and forbidden derivatives do not remain authoritative.

#### Scenario: US27-P04 AC27-P31-05

- **GIVEN** a protected fact differs between otherwise equivalent fixtures,
- **WHEN** authorized summaries, ranks, filters and export contents are compared,
- **THEN** the restricted fact does not change admitted output unless an independently qualified aggregate disclosure exists.

#### Scenario: US27-P04 AC27-P31-06

- **GIVEN** staff change a ministry target, stage, lifecycle or relationship purpose,
- **WHEN** source owners are reconciled,
- **THEN** gifts, commitments, Designations, consent, recurring support and publishing authority do not change by implication.

#### Scenario: US27-P04 AC27-P31-07

- **GIVEN** a reader fails or a claimed-complete projection disagrees with source evidence,
- **WHEN** the authorized user opens the affected view,
- **THEN** it exposes incomplete/unavailable or pending reconciliation truth and a permitted owner resolution route instead of false zero or healthy status.

### Requirement: US27-P06 — Start or reuse work in its existing context

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to start or reuse work in its existing context, so that I can record deliberate intent once without entering known details again. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P03.

#### Scenario: US27-P06 AC27-P03-01

- **WHEN** staff expressly start cultivation while recording the included referral or ask,

#### Scenario: US27-P06 AC27-P03-02

- **THEN** no new enrollment is admitted; separately qualified financial/service facts and existing Party/task work remain usable.

#### Scenario: US27-P06 AC27-P03-03

- **WHEN** a new start operation resolves to permitted reuse,
- **THEN** those existing facts remain unchanged and only separately requested authorized work is added.

#### Scenario: US27-P06 AC27-P03-04

### Requirement: US27-P07 — Reuse existing work within my actual permissions

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to reuse existing work within my actual permissions, so that I can avoid duplicate enrollments without receiving private history. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P03, REQ27-P13.

#### Scenario: US27-P07 AC27-P03-01

- **GIVEN** known authorized Party/ministry/purpose/work context exists,
- **WHEN** staff expressly start cultivation while recording the included referral or ask,
- **THEN** one understandable action accepts the specifically included source effects without asking for known information again.

#### Scenario: US27-P07 AC27-P03-02

- **GIVEN** a gift, score change, imported contact or introduction-only note arrives,
- **WHEN** the source fact is processed,
- **THEN** no new enrollment is admitted; separately qualified financial/service facts and existing Party/task work remain usable.

#### Scenario: US27-P07 AC27-P03-03

- **GIVEN** an ordinary enrollment already contains stages, owner and several open asks,
- **WHEN** a new start operation resolves to permitted reuse,
- **THEN** those existing facts remain unchanged and only separately requested authorized work is added.

#### Scenario: US27-P07 AC27-P03-04

- **GIVEN** paired fixtures differ only in whether a forbidden ordinary enrollment exists,
- **WHEN** a caller lacking minimum admission-result authority attempts entry,
- **THEN** observable availability, bounded response, counts and errors do not expose the hidden difference or admit a workaround duplicate.

#### Scenario: US27-P07 AC27-P03-05

- **GIVEN** a caller has limited reuse metadata authority but not history/edit authority,
- **WHEN** the command resolves existing work,
- **THEN** only that admitted metadata is returned and no edit, history, missionary or finance access is created.

#### Scenario: US27-P07 AC27-P13-01

- **GIVEN** valid old rows are paired with hostile proposed Tenant, entity, Party, ministry, owner, actor or purpose fields,
- **WHEN** each admitted insert/update/upsert/delete/returning/RPC/service/import/repair path is exercised under real effective roles,
- **THEN** all independently forbidden resulting states and spoofed attribution fail without leaking hidden constraint/source details.

#### Scenario: US27-P07 AC27-P13-02

- **GIVEN** source links are null, partially-null composite references, cross-Tenant or mismatched entity references,
- **WHEN** the command and applicable database constraints are exercised,
- **THEN** required identity cannot be fabricated and optional references are wholly absent or valid against their owners.

#### Scenario: US27-P07 AC27-P13-03

- **GIVEN** member-without-grant, revoked-member, former-member-with-independent-grant and admitted service contexts exist,
- **WHEN** read/action/result commands are evaluated,
- **THEN** current exact authority determines outcomes; role labels, enrollment and membership neither add nor remove independent valid grants by inference.

#### Scenario: US27-P07 AC27-P13-04

- **GIVEN** faults occur at every local acceptance/history/receipt/work/intent boundary,
- **WHEN** the command executes,
- **THEN** pre-commit failure leaves no partially accepted business change; post-commit secondary failure retains one source receipt and visible repairable pending/failed state.

#### Scenario: US27-P07 AC27-P13-05

- **GIVEN** same intent is submitted through multiple transports or after a lost response,
- **WHEN** duplicates, changed payload and stale out-of-order events arrive,
- **THEN** one intended effect remains; changed meaning conflicts and old events/receipts do not overwrite newer state or acquire new disclosure authority.

#### Scenario: US27-P07 AC27-P13-06

- **GIVEN** permission revocation and acceptance contend in independent transactions,
- **WHEN** each valid ordering is forced,
- **THEN** stale authority cannot create a new effect after the revocation boundary; accepted earlier history stays true without preserving future forbidden access.

#### Scenario: US27-P07 AC27-P13-07

- **GIVEN** hidden source facts differ in paired fixtures while permitted inputs are identical,
- **WHEN** counts, ranking, filters, selection, cached responses, errors and exports are observed,
- **THEN** unauthorized facts do not alter observable permitted output; stale responses after Tenant/role change are rejected.

#### Scenario: US27-P07 AC27-P13-08

- **GIVEN** sensitive source material is redacted, expires or is lawfully erased,
- **WHEN** history, task previews, notifications, reports, logs, exports, generated files and retained derivatives are reconciled,
- **THEN** admitted structural evidence remains only where permitted and protected bodies/identities are not restored or copied to evade source restrictions.

#### Scenario: US27-P07 AC27-P13-09

- **GIVEN** an external owner returns an ambiguous timeout after possible success,
- **WHEN** recovery runs,
- **THEN** the source/provider receipt is reconciled before retry and no blind duplicate effect or false success is reported.

### Requirement: US27-P08 — Review a finite group and understand page versus all-results selection

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to review a finite group and understand page versus all-results selection, so that I can know exactly which people or organizations the action covers. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P04.

#### Scenario: US27-P08 AC27-P04-01

- **GIVEN** staff review A and B while C is outside the selection,
- **WHEN** C later matches or staff edit the saved filter and pages are evicted,
- **THEN** only the finite reviewed A/B intent can execute; source eligibility is rechecked without substituting a different subject.

#### Scenario: US27-P08 AC27-P04-02

- **GIVEN** a supported page selection and all-results selection have different sizes,
- **WHEN** staff review either action,
- **THEN** the scope and count are honest and inspectable; loaded rows are never represented as all matches.

#### Scenario: US27-P08 AC27-P04-03

- **GIVEN** one member commits and another member fails before source acceptance,
- **WHEN** the group completes or is resumed,
- **THEN** the first success remains and the failed member has no partial accepted source state; remaining permitted outcomes can be recovered individually.

#### Scenario: US27-P08 AC27-P04-04

- **GIVEN** a worker commits source acceptance but loses its progress write or first client response,
- **WHEN** the same intent is retried,
- **THEN** source receipt reconciliation recovers one accepted effect and does not duplicate its ask/task or manufacture failure.

#### Scenario: US27-P08 AC27-P04-05

- **GIVEN** cancellation and acceptance contend for the same unadmitted member,
- **WHEN** each valid serialization order is exercised,
- **THEN** accepted work survives if acceptance wins; cancellation blocks later admission if cancellation wins; no accepted history is rolled back.

#### Scenario: US27-P08 AC27-P04-06

- **GIVEN** a Tenant switch, permission revocation, source retirement or material source change occurs after review,
- **WHEN** a deferred member executes or its result arrives,
- **THEN** original context remains fixed and current constraints decide the member outcome; stale output cannot populate the new context.

#### Scenario: US27-P08 AC27-P04-07

- **GIVEN** a group exceeds a qualified numeric capacity boundary,
- **WHEN** staff submit it,
- **THEN** the system explains a rejection or explicit partition; it neither truncates membership nor starts an unreviewed replacement operation.

### Requirement: US27-P09 — Recover individual group results and stop remaining work

- **THEN** pre-commit failure leaves no partially accepted business change; post-commit secondary failure retains one source receipt and visible repairable pending/failed state.

#### Scenario: US27-P10 AC27-P13-05

- **GIVEN** same intent is submitted through multiple transports or after a lost response,
- **WHEN** duplicates, changed payload and stale out-of-order events arrive,
- **THEN** one intended effect remains; changed meaning conflicts and old events/receipts do not overwrite newer state or acquire new disclosure authority.

#### Scenario: US27-P10 AC27-P13-06

- **GIVEN** permission revocation and acceptance contend in independent transactions,
- **WHEN** each valid ordering is forced,
- **THEN** stale authority cannot create a new effect after the revocation boundary; accepted earlier history stays true without preserving future forbidden access.

#### Scenario: US27-P10 AC27-P13-07

- **GIVEN** hidden source facts differ in paired fixtures while permitted inputs are identical,
- **WHEN** counts, ranking, filters, selection, cached responses, errors and exports are observed,
- **THEN** unauthorized facts do not alter observable permitted output; stale responses after Tenant/role change are rejected.

#### Scenario: US27-P10 AC27-P13-08

- **GIVEN** sensitive source material is redacted, expires or is lawfully erased,
- **WHEN** history, task previews, notifications, reports, logs, exports, generated files and retained derivatives are reconciled,
- **THEN** admitted structural evidence remains only where permitted and protected bodies/identities are not restored or copied to evade source restrictions.

#### Scenario: US27-P10 AC27-P13-09

- **GIVEN** an external owner returns an ambiguous timeout after possible success,
- **WHEN** recovery runs,
- **THEN** the source/provider receipt is reconciled before retry and no blind duplicate effect or false success is reported.

#### Scenario: US27-P10 AC27-P30-01

- **GIVEN** ordinary start, pause, close, reopen and stage/list flows contain all admitted context,
- **WHEN** representative staff and missionaries complete relevant tasks without coaching,
- **THEN** they identify the supporter, ministry, responsibility and next action and distinguish internal intent from supporter agreement.

#### Scenario: US27-P10 AC27-P30-02

- **GIVEN** the same context uses long Thai, Arabic, Latin, combining-character names, RTL and restricted aliases,
- **WHEN** the flow is rendered in supported locales and mobile widths,
- **THEN** full safe context is understandable without Western splitting, US-address assumptions, clipped critical labels or legal-name/location fallback.

#### Scenario: US27-P10 AC27-P30-03

- **GIVEN** the flow contains dragging, dialogs, removed rows, validation and status changes,
- **WHEN** keyboard-only, screen-reader and single-pointer users complete it,
- **THEN** actions have working alternatives, focus is visible/returned correctly, errors are associated and status changes are announced.

#### Scenario: US27-P10 AC27-P30-04

- **GIVEN** the user operates at 320 CSS pixels, 400% zoom, light/dark modes and mobile keyboard conditions,
- **WHEN** each ordinary action is completed,
- **THEN** meaning, required controls and safe source links remain usable with measured contrast and target compliance.

#### Scenario: US27-P10 AC27-P30-05

- **GIVEN** a save response is delayed, dropped or rejected after permitted input is entered,
- **WHEN** the user navigates, retries or returns,
- **THEN** the UI preserves allowed drafts and recovers the same intent while distinguishing accepted, pending, rejected and unconfirmed outcomes.

#### Scenario: US27-P10 AC27-P30-06

- **GIVEN** current permissions or active Tenant change while old requests are pending,
- **WHEN** late responses arrive,
- **THEN** denied draft content is removed and old-context responses cannot overwrite the current context or claim a forbidden success.

#### Scenario: US27-P10 AC27-P30-07

- **GIVEN** a confirmed source mutation removes a row from the current list,
- **WHEN** the result appears,
- **THEN** focus moves predictably, the durable result and next action remain understandable beyond a toast, and no unsafe generic Undo erases accepted or reused work.

### Requirement: US27-P11 — See the established eligible partnership lead as a changeable starting choice

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to see the established eligible partnership lead as a changeable starting choice, so that I can avoid both extra entry and accidental assignment. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P05.

#### Scenario: US27-P11 AC27-P05-01

- **GIVEN** a partnership has one established currently eligible lead,
- **WHEN** staff start or review responsibility,
- **THEN** that lead is visibly prefilled and changeable without a separate confirmation; the unrelated creator/Party representative is not substituted.

#### Scenario: US27-P11 AC27-P05-02

- **GIVEN** the partnership lead is unknown or several candidates are equally plausible,
- **WHEN** staff start work,
- **THEN** the UI requests an authorized choice and does not use first result, administrator status or ministry membership as proof.

#### Scenario: US27-P11 AC27-P05-03

- **GIVEN** the Party representative changes while a partnership and requests have different owners,
- **WHEN** that assignment change is accepted,
- **THEN** partnership and child responsibility do not silently transfer; separately qualified handoff remains possible.

#### Scenario: US27-P11 AC27-P05-04

- **GIVEN** the current lead departs and no existing eligible assignee or queue remains,
- **WHEN** access is revoked,
- **THEN** revocation applies immediately and unresolved work is visible to its qualified repair owner; no private payload reaches an invented or former recipient.

#### Scenario: US27-P11 AC27-P05-05

- **GIVEN** a user becomes responsible for partnership or task work,
- **WHEN** the source assignment is accepted,
- **THEN** no independent read, financial, consent, publication or missionary grant is created.

### Requirement: US27-P12 — Repair responsibility after a departure

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized responsibility manager, I want to repair responsibility after a departure, so that I can keep follow-through accountable while denying revoked access immediately. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P05, REQ27-P07, REQ27-P10.

#### Scenario: US27-P12 AC27-P05-01

- **GIVEN** a partnership has one established currently eligible lead,
- **WHEN** staff start or review responsibility,
- **THEN** that lead is visibly prefilled and changeable without a separate confirmation; the unrelated creator/Party representative is not substituted.

#### Scenario: US27-P12 AC27-P05-02

- **GIVEN** the partnership lead is unknown or several candidates are equally plausible,
- **WHEN** staff start work,
- **THEN** the UI requests an authorized choice and does not use first result, administrator status or ministry membership as proof.

#### Scenario: US27-P12 AC27-P05-03

- **GIVEN** the Party representative changes while a partnership and requests have different owners,
- **WHEN** that assignment change is accepted,
- **THEN** partnership and child responsibility do not silently transfer; separately qualified handoff remains possible.

#### Scenario: US27-P12 AC27-P05-04

- **GIVEN** the current lead departs and no existing eligible assignee or queue remains,
- **WHEN** access is revoked,
- **THEN** revocation applies immediately and unresolved work is visible to its qualified repair owner; no private payload reaches an invented or former recipient.

#### Scenario: US27-P12 AC27-P05-05

- **GIVEN** a user becomes responsible for partnership or task work,
- **WHEN** the source assignment is accepted,
- **THEN** no independent read, financial, consent, publication or missionary grant is created.

#### Scenario: US27-P12 AC27-P07-01

- **GIVEN** appropriate authorized existing work already addresses the enrollment need,
- **WHEN** staff start or continue cultivation,
- **THEN** the existing source task can satisfy next action and no duplicate task is created merely for enrollment.

#### Scenario: US27-P12 AC27-P07-02

- **GIVEN** several promises and requests are due while one next action is selected,
- **WHEN** the partnership summary is displayed,
- **THEN** other applicable obligations remain discoverable and are not implicitly completed or hidden by that choice.

#### Scenario: US27-P12 AC27-P07-03

- **GIVEN** one meeting addresses two of three source obligations,
- **WHEN** authorized staff record the source outcomes,
- **THEN** only the evidenced obligations change; one encounter is not copied into several actual encounters and the third obligation survives.

#### Scenario: US27-P12 AC27-P07-04

- **GIVEN** a task projects a source-owned paused-work review,
- **WHEN** a generic complete/delete/dismiss/bulk/AI operation is attempted,
- **THEN** it cannot resume or end cultivation or satisfy the review outside the qualified source action.

#### Scenario: US27-P12 AC27-P07-05

- **GIVEN** a shared task has an independent valid reason after a cultivation-specific reason ends,
- **WHEN** the source disposition commits,
- **THEN** the surviving reason, current authority, accountability and retrieval route remain.

#### Scenario: US27-P12 AC27-P07-06

- **GIVEN** a local projection fails after source acceptance,
- **WHEN** an authorized operator opens existing work/recovery context,
- **THEN** the accepted source and unresolved secondary state are findable with their durable identity and safe repair action, without SQL repair as routine UX.

#### Scenario: US27-P12 AC27-P10-01

- **GIVEN** the lead, creator, Party representative and finance owner are different people,
- **WHEN** review responsibility and due attention resolve,
- **THEN** only the qualified current lead or authorized explicit review assignee receives the appropriate source-backed work; other identities receive nothing without independent qualification.

#### Scenario: US27-P12 AC27-P10-02

- **GIVEN** a currently eligible explicit review assignee exists,
- **WHEN** the partnership lead changes,
- **THEN** the explicit review responsibility remains until a deliberate qualified handoff changes it; no duplicate recipient is inferred.

#### Scenario: US27-P12 AC27-P10-03

- **GIVEN** an enabled staff or missionary recipient profile is used,
- **WHEN** the user opens the task and attention,
- **THEN** each route resolves the same source review and allowed action with a working role-safe authenticated link; incomplete or reserved profiles cannot emit substitute attention.

#### Scenario: US27-P12 AC27-P10-04

- **GIVEN** a user reads an actionable review notification,
- **WHEN** they revisit work or try to archive it,
- **THEN** the unresolved work remains actionable and archive is unavailable; after source ending it is visible only within the authorized 90-day Recent ceiling.

#### Scenario: US27-P12 AC27-P10-05

- **GIVEN** zero eligible recipients, an indeterminate resolver or revocation immediately before release occurs,
- **WHEN** the worker processes the review,
- **THEN** no unqualified recipient receives attention and an authorized source repair exception remains; restored access does not revive an ended engagement.

#### Scenario: US27-P12 AC27-P10-06

- **GIVEN** two due workers and a reschedule/resume/end contend,
- **WHEN** both serialization orders and response-loss retries are exercised,
- **THEN** there is one intended admitted attention, no stale active attention or successor overwrite, and no old prepared envelope is sent to a newly chosen recipient.

#### Scenario: US27-P12 AC27-P10-07

- **GIVEN** a protected reason, restricted identity or worker location is associated with the source,
- **WHEN** task previews, notification payloads, logs, counts, exports and links are examined under each admitted role,
- **THEN** only independently allowed minimal metadata appears; public and donor surfaces receive no review context.

#### Scenario: US27-P12 AC27-P10-08

- **GIVEN** the optional email provider or Studio is unavailable,
- **WHEN** a qualified internal-only review becomes due,
- **THEN** native source work and permitted in-product attention remain functional without a substitute email or duplicate Studio effect.

### Requirement: US27-P13 — Review only future work actually affected by ministry changes

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to review only future work actually affected by ministry changes, so that I can preserve continuity without implying loyalty transfers to replacement people. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P06.

#### Scenario: US27-P13 AC27-P06-01

- **GIVEN** ministry name or participant metadata changes without changing source eligibility or represented future work,
- **WHEN** source updates propagate,
- **THEN** history remains and no blanket supporter review, new journey, task or consent inference occurs.

#### Scenario: US27-P13 AC27-P06-02

- **GIVEN** an open invitation names a person who leaves the ministry,
- **WHEN** the authoritative source change is accepted,
- **THEN** only the affected future invitation/follow-through is presented for permitted review; replacement-person supporter allegiance is not inferred.

#### Scenario: US27-P13 AC27-P06-03

- **GIVEN** a draft relationship points to the wrong source and authorized correction is qualified,
- **WHEN** staff correct it with the expected revision,
- **THEN** the current association is corrected with original reference, trusted actor and reason preserved.

#### Scenario: US27-P13 AC27-P06-04

- **GIVEN** an ask has already been presented or an encounter/gift accepted,
- **WHEN** staff correct the enrollment reference,
- **THEN** those accepted source facts remain attributable to their original context; required downstream corrections use their owners.

#### Scenario: US27-P13 AC27-P06-05

- **GIVEN** two distinct same-name ministries or different Tenant/Legal Entity scopes are involved,
- **WHEN** a move or source repair is proposed,
- **THEN** it cannot silently merge or retarget history; genuinely different future work uses explicit permitted disposition.

#### Scenario: US27-P13 AC27-P06-06

- **GIVEN** current restrictions conflict with future work while a review remains pending,
- **WHEN** a consequential source action is attempted,
- **THEN** the restriction applies immediately rather than waiting for staff to complete the review.

### Requirement: US27-P14 — Correct a mistaken relationship reference through its owner

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to correct a mistaken relationship reference through its owner, so that I can repair honest errors while preserving accepted evidence. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P06.

#### Scenario: US27-P14 AC27-P06-01

- **GIVEN** ministry name or participant metadata changes without changing source eligibility or represented future work,
- **WHEN** source updates propagate,
- **THEN** history remains and no blanket supporter review, new journey, task or consent inference occurs.

#### Scenario: US27-P14 AC27-P06-02

- **GIVEN** an open invitation names a person who leaves the ministry,
- **WHEN** the authoritative source change is accepted,
- **THEN** only the affected future invitation/follow-through is presented for permitted review; replacement-person supporter allegiance is not inferred.

#### Scenario: US27-P14 AC27-P06-03

- **GIVEN** a draft relationship points to the wrong source and authorized correction is qualified,
- **WHEN** staff correct it with the expected revision,
- **THEN** the current association is corrected with original reference, trusted actor and reason preserved.

#### Scenario: US27-P14 AC27-P06-04

- **GIVEN** an ask has already been presented or an encounter/gift accepted,
- **WHEN** staff correct the enrollment reference,
- **THEN** those accepted source facts remain attributable to their original context; required downstream corrections use their owners.

#### Scenario: US27-P14 AC27-P06-05

- **GIVEN** two distinct same-name ministries or different Tenant/Legal Entity scopes are involved,
- **WHEN** a move or source repair is proposed,
- **THEN** it cannot silently merge or retarget history; genuinely different future work uses explicit permitted disposition.

#### Scenario: US27-P14 AC27-P06-06

- **GIVEN** current restrictions conflict with future work while a review remains pending,
- **WHEN** a consequential source action is attempted,
- **THEN** the restriction applies immediately rather than waiting for staff to complete the review.

### Requirement: US27-P15 — Reuse appropriate existing next work

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to reuse appropriate existing next work, so that I can avoid a duplicate task merely because cultivation is recorded. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P07.

#### Scenario: US27-P15 AC27-P07-01

- **GIVEN** appropriate authorized existing work already addresses the enrollment need,
- **WHEN** staff start or continue cultivation,
- **THEN** the existing source task can satisfy next action and no duplicate task is created merely for enrollment.

#### Scenario: US27-P15 AC27-P07-02

- **GIVEN** several promises and requests are due while one next action is selected,
- **WHEN** the partnership summary is displayed,
- **THEN** other applicable obligations remain discoverable and are not implicitly completed or hidden by that choice.

#### Scenario: US27-P15 AC27-P07-03

- **GIVEN** one meeting addresses two of three source obligations,
- **WHEN** authorized staff record the source outcomes,
- **THEN** only the evidenced obligations change; one encounter is not copied into several actual encounters and the third obligation survives.

#### Scenario: US27-P15 AC27-P07-04

- **GIVEN** a task projects a source-owned paused-work review,
- **WHEN** a generic complete/delete/dismiss/bulk/AI operation is attempted,
- **THEN** it cannot resume or end cultivation or satisfy the review outside the qualified source action.

#### Scenario: US27-P15 AC27-P07-05

- **GIVEN** a shared task has an independent valid reason after a cultivation-specific reason ends,
- **WHEN** the source disposition commits,
- **THEN** the surviving reason, current authority, accountability and retrieval route remain.

#### Scenario: US27-P15 AC27-P07-06

- **GIVEN** a local projection fails after source acceptance,
- **WHEN** an authorized operator opens existing work/recovery context,
- **THEN** the accepted source and unresolved secondary state are findable with their durable identity and safe repair action, without SQL repair as routine UX.

### Requirement: US27-P16 — See independent promises and requests alongside the next action

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to see independent promises and requests alongside the next action, so that I can avoid losing follow-through when several matters are underway. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P07.

#### Scenario: US27-P16 AC27-P07-01

- **GIVEN** appropriate authorized existing work already addresses the enrollment need,
- **WHEN** staff start or continue cultivation,
- **THEN** the existing source task can satisfy next action and no duplicate task is created merely for enrollment.

#### Scenario: US27-P16 AC27-P07-02

- **GIVEN** several promises and requests are due while one next action is selected,
- **WHEN** the partnership summary is displayed,
- **THEN** other applicable obligations remain discoverable and are not implicitly completed or hidden by that choice.

#### Scenario: US27-P16 AC27-P07-03

- **GIVEN** one meeting addresses two of three source obligations,
- **WHEN** authorized staff record the source outcomes,
- **THEN** only the evidenced obligations change; one encounter is not copied into several actual encounters and the third obligation survives.

#### Scenario: US27-P16 AC27-P07-04

- **GIVEN** a task projects a source-owned paused-work review,
- **WHEN** a generic complete/delete/dismiss/bulk/AI operation is attempted,
- **THEN** it cannot resume or end cultivation or satisfy the review outside the qualified source action.

#### Scenario: US27-P16 AC27-P07-05

- **GIVEN** a shared task has an independent valid reason after a cultivation-specific reason ends,
- **WHEN** the source disposition commits,
- **THEN** the surviving reason, current authority, accountability and retrieval route remain.

#### Scenario: US27-P16 AC27-P07-06

- **GIVEN** a local projection fails after source acceptance,
- **WHEN** an authorized operator opens existing work/recovery context,
- **THEN** the accepted source and unresolved secondary state are findable with their durable identity and safe repair action, without SQL repair as routine UX.

### Requirement: US27-P17 — Pause work until a deliberately chosen internal review

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to pause work until a deliberately chosen internal review, so that I can retain intention without implying the supporter owes a response. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P08.

#### Scenario: US27-P17 AC27-P08-01

- **GIVEN** authorized staff choose a date and pause current work,
- **WHEN** the same save is repeated,
- **THEN** one current review occurrence and at most one source task identity are retained; changed meaning under the same key conflicts.

#### Scenario: US27-P17 AC27-P08-02

- **GIVEN** an actual review concludes that work should remain paused,
- **WHEN** staff choose Keep on hold and a successor date,
- **THEN** the old review is Completed and a new current occurrence with the chosen date exists atomically.

#### Scenario: US27-P17 AC27-P08-03

- **GIVEN** staff need a different date without conducting the review,
- **WHEN** they reschedule the current occurrence,
- **THEN** the schedule changes with attributable history and no completed-review fact is created.

#### Scenario: US27-P17 AC27-P08-04

- **GIVEN** the due date arrives or a notification is read/archived or a gift is received,
- **WHEN** the external or engagement event is processed,
- **THEN** cultivation remains paused, the review remains unresolved, and no automatic donor contact or source completion occurs.

#### Scenario: US27-P17 AC27-P08-05

- **GIVEN** a review action resumes or closes work,
- **WHEN** the qualified source command commits,
- **THEN** affected obligations are explicitly accounted for; unrelated tasks, gifts, commitments, consent and public content remain unchanged.

#### Scenario: US27-P17 AC27-P08-06

- **GIVEN** staff intend meaningful future work but do not choose a date,
- **WHEN** they attempt the dated-pause action,
- **THEN** a clear date requirement is shown without fabricating a cadence, donor action or completed review.

### Requirement: US27-P18 — Review paused work and choose keep on hold, resume or close

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized review assignee, I want to review paused work and choose keep on hold, resume or close, so that I can record the actual decision with a clear next step. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P08, REQ27-P11.

#### Scenario: US27-P18 AC27-P08-01

- **GIVEN** authorized staff choose a date and pause current work,
- **WHEN** the same save is repeated,
- **THEN** one current review occurrence and at most one source task identity are retained; changed meaning under the same key conflicts.

#### Scenario: US27-P18 AC27-P08-02

- **GIVEN** an actual review concludes that work should remain paused,
- **WHEN** staff choose Keep on hold and a successor date,
- **THEN** the old review is Completed and a new current occurrence with the chosen date exists atomically.

#### Scenario: US27-P18 AC27-P08-03

- **GIVEN** staff need a different date without conducting the review,
- **WHEN** they reschedule the current occurrence,
- **THEN** the schedule changes with attributable history and no completed-review fact is created.

#### Scenario: US27-P18 AC27-P08-04

- **GIVEN** the due date arrives or a notification is read/archived or a gift is received,
- **WHEN** the external or engagement event is processed,
- **THEN** cultivation remains paused, the review remains unresolved, and no automatic donor contact or source completion occurs.

#### Scenario: US27-P18 AC27-P08-05

- **GIVEN** a review action resumes or closes work,
- **WHEN** the qualified source command commits,
- **THEN** affected obligations are explicitly accounted for; unrelated tasks, gifts, commitments, consent and public content remain unchanged.

#### Scenario: US27-P18 AC27-P08-06

- **GIVEN** staff intend meaningful future work but do not choose a date,
- **WHEN** they attempt the dated-pause action,
- **THEN** a clear date requirement is shown without fabricating a cadence, donor action or completed review.

#### Scenario: US27-P18 AC27-P11-01

- **GIVEN** staff inspect cases of declined ask, ongoing promised prayer follow-through, continuing giving without further development work and a deliberate temporary pause,
- **WHEN** they choose a source action,
- **THEN** the UI distinguishes these meanings and prevents treating missing date or visible emptiness alone as completed work.

#### Scenario: US27-P18 AC27-P11-02

- **GIVEN** a relevant promise exists outside the first page or is protected from the actor,
- **WHEN** closure readiness is evaluated,
- **THEN** complete owner evidence determines readiness; permitted minimal outcomes disclose only authorized information and unknown checks cannot pass as empty.

#### Scenario: US27-P18 AC27-P11-03

- **GIVEN** an independent obligation has valid continuing authority and responsibility,
- **WHEN** closure disposes the development-specific work,
- **THEN** the independent obligation stays findable and actionable; reassigning the same continuing development work does not falsely satisfy readiness.

#### Scenario: US27-P18 AC27-P11-04

- **GIVEN** close races new work, review changes or authority revocation through any admitted writer,
- **WHEN** real transactions exercise both orders,
- **THEN** no accepted combination strands required work or permits stale authority; closure and the incompatible new work cannot both pass separate stale prechecks.

#### Scenario: US27-P18 AC27-P11-05

- **GIVEN** a review is actually performed before End, or a future review is made unnecessary by direct Close,
- **WHEN** the action is accepted,
- **THEN** the first outcome is Completed and the second No longer required; unrelated task reasons survive and queued obsolete attention cannot newly release.

#### Scenario: US27-P18 AC27-P11-06

- **GIVEN** staff close one of several scoped relationships,
- **WHEN** current views, counts and financial/contact sources are reconciled,
- **THEN** only that enrollment leaves current-work scope; the preserved stage, requests, assignments, gifts, consent and other enrollments are unchanged and no last-contact/successful-ask increment occurs.

#### Scenario: US27-P18 AC27-P11-07

- **GIVEN** close response is lost and a retry occurs after a later transition or revoked access,
- **WHEN** the original intent is recovered,
- **THEN** one accepted effect retains original actor/time; current authority governs result disclosure and the stale intent cannot close or reopen newer work.

#### Scenario: US27-P18 AC27-P11-08

- **GIVEN** closed work is viewed under current permissions after redaction or lawful content deletion,
- **WHEN** history and derived reports are opened,
- **THEN** the admitted structural history remains attributable without restoring forbidden content, changing closed-period interpretation or fabricating successful outcomes.

### Requirement: US27-P19 — Reschedule without claiming that a review happened

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized review assignee, I want to reschedule without claiming that a review happened, so that I can keep trustworthy working history. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P08, REQ27-P09.

#### Scenario: US27-P19 AC27-P08-01

- **GIVEN** authorized staff choose a date and pause current work,
- **WHEN** the same save is repeated,
- **THEN** one current review occurrence and at most one source task identity are retained; changed meaning under the same key conflicts.

#### Scenario: US27-P19 AC27-P08-02

- **GIVEN** an actual review concludes that work should remain paused,
- **WHEN** staff choose Keep on hold and a successor date,
- **THEN** the old review is Completed and a new current occurrence with the chosen date exists atomically.

#### Scenario: US27-P19 AC27-P08-03

- **GIVEN** staff need a different date without conducting the review,
- **WHEN** they reschedule the current occurrence,
- **THEN** the schedule changes with attributable history and no completed-review fact is created.

#### Scenario: US27-P19 AC27-P08-04

- **GIVEN** the due date arrives or a notification is read/archived or a gift is received,
- **WHEN** the external or engagement event is processed,
- **THEN** cultivation remains paused, the review remains unresolved, and no automatic donor contact or source completion occurs.

#### Scenario: US27-P19 AC27-P08-05

- **GIVEN** a review action resumes or closes work,
- **WHEN** the qualified source command commits,
- **THEN** affected obligations are explicitly accounted for; unrelated tasks, gifts, commitments, consent and public content remain unchanged.

#### Scenario: US27-P19 AC27-P08-06

- **GIVEN** staff intend meaningful future work but do not choose a date,
- **WHEN** they attempt the dated-pause action,
- **THEN** a clear date requirement is shown without fabricating a cadence, donor action or completed review.

#### Scenario: US27-P19 AC27-P09-01

- **GIVEN** the Tenant has a valid authoritative work timezone and today is selected,
- **WHEN** staff save a pause,
- **THEN** the visible chosen date/zone and stored due interpretation agree and today is accepted.

#### Scenario: US27-P19 AC27-P09-02

- **GIVEN** a new ordinary review date is in the past or the selected local date does not exist,
- **WHEN** staff save,
- **THEN** a linked understandable error retains permitted input and no substitute date or review is accepted.

#### Scenario: US27-P19 AC27-P09-03

- **GIVEN** the organization zone is missing or invalid,
- **WHEN** staff choose a review date,
- **THEN** the flow requires the qualified setup/repair path and never silently uses browser timezone.

#### Scenario: US27-P19 AC27-P09-04

- **GIVEN** a review spans a twenty-three-hour or twenty-five-hour day or crosses hemispheres,
- **WHEN** due and overdue views are evaluated,
- **THEN** due begins at the first valid instant of the saved local date and overdue at the following local date, without UTC-midnight or one-hour drift.

#### Scenario: US27-P19 AC27-P09-05

- **GIVEN** staff travel, the assignee changes or the Tenant changes its work timezone after acceptance,
- **WHEN** the review is viewed and processed,
- **THEN** its saved date/zone meaning remains unchanged unless a deliberate qualified schedule correction occurs.

#### Scenario: US27-P19 AC27-P09-06

- **GIVEN** timezone rules affecting an accepted future date change,
- **WHEN** the controlled reconciliation runs,
- **THEN** the authoritative interpretation and provenance are reconciled consistently and reader disagreement cannot silently trigger duplicate attention.

### Requirement: US27-P20 — See my review date retain its organization-calendar meaning during travel

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized review assignee, I want to see my review date retain its organization-calendar meaning during travel, so that I can avoid missed or unexpectedly shifted internal work. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P09.

#### Scenario: US27-P20 AC27-P09-01

- **GIVEN** the Tenant has a valid authoritative work timezone and today is selected,
- **WHEN** staff save a pause,
- **THEN** the visible chosen date/zone and stored due interpretation agree and today is accepted.

#### Scenario: US27-P20 AC27-P09-02

- **GIVEN** a new ordinary review date is in the past or the selected local date does not exist,
- **WHEN** staff save,
- **THEN** a linked understandable error retains permitted input and no substitute date or review is accepted.

#### Scenario: US27-P20 AC27-P09-03

- **GIVEN** the organization zone is missing or invalid,
- **WHEN** staff choose a review date,
- **THEN** the flow requires the qualified setup/repair path and never silently uses browser timezone.

#### Scenario: US27-P20 AC27-P09-04

- **GIVEN** a review spans a twenty-three-hour or twenty-five-hour day or crosses hemispheres,
- **WHEN** due and overdue views are evaluated,
- **THEN** due begins at the first valid instant of the saved local date and overdue at the following local date, without UTC-midnight or one-hour drift.

#### Scenario: US27-P20 AC27-P09-05

- **GIVEN** staff travel, the assignee changes or the Tenant changes its work timezone after acceptance,
- **WHEN** the review is viewed and processed,
- **THEN** its saved date/zone meaning remains unchanged unless a deliberate qualified schedule correction occurs.

#### Scenario: US27-P20 AC27-P09-06

- **GIVEN** timezone rules affecting an accepted future date change,
- **WHEN** the controlled reconciliation runs,
- **THEN** the authoritative interpretation and provenance are reconciled consistently and reader disagreement cannot silently trigger duplicate attention.

### Requirement: US27-P21 — Find the same assigned review in my permitted task context

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized staff member or missionary with a qualified review profile, I want to find the same assigned review in my permitted task context, so that I can act through a safe working link without copied tasks or excess disclosure. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P10.

#### Scenario: US27-P21 AC27-P10-01

- **GIVEN** the lead, creator, Party representative and finance owner are different people,
- **WHEN** review responsibility and due attention resolve,
- **THEN** only the qualified current lead or authorized explicit review assignee receives the appropriate source-backed work; other identities receive nothing without independent qualification.

#### Scenario: US27-P21 AC27-P10-02

- **GIVEN** a currently eligible explicit review assignee exists,
- **WHEN** the partnership lead changes,
- **THEN** the explicit review responsibility remains until a deliberate qualified handoff changes it; no duplicate recipient is inferred.

#### Scenario: US27-P21 AC27-P10-03

- **GIVEN** an enabled staff or missionary recipient profile is used,
- **WHEN** the user opens the task and attention,
- **THEN** each route resolves the same source review and allowed action with a working role-safe authenticated link; incomplete or reserved profiles cannot emit substitute attention.

#### Scenario: US27-P21 AC27-P10-04

- **GIVEN** a user reads an actionable review notification,
- **WHEN** they revisit work or try to archive it,
- **THEN** the unresolved work remains actionable and archive is unavailable; after source ending it is visible only within the authorized 90-day Recent ceiling.

#### Scenario: US27-P21 AC27-P10-05

- **GIVEN** zero eligible recipients, an indeterminate resolver or revocation immediately before release occurs,
- **WHEN** the worker processes the review,
- **THEN** no unqualified recipient receives attention and an authorized source repair exception remains; restored access does not revive an ended engagement.

#### Scenario: US27-P21 AC27-P10-06

- **GIVEN** two due workers and a reschedule/resume/end contend,
- **WHEN** both serialization orders and response-loss retries are exercised,
- **THEN** there is one intended admitted attention, no stale active attention or successor overwrite, and no old prepared envelope is sent to a newly chosen recipient.

#### Scenario: US27-P21 AC27-P10-07

- **GIVEN** a protected reason, restricted identity or worker location is associated with the source,
- **WHEN** task previews, notification payloads, logs, counts, exports and links are examined under each admitted role,
- **THEN** only independently allowed minimal metadata appears; public and donor surfaces receive no review context.

#### Scenario: US27-P21 AC27-P10-08

- **GIVEN** the optional email provider or Studio is unavailable,
- **WHEN** a qualified internal-only review becomes due,
- **THEN** native source work and permitted in-product attention remain functional without a substitute email or duplicate Studio effect.

### Requirement: US27-P22 — Read reminders without hiding unresolved work

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized review assignee, I want to read reminders without hiding unresolved work, so that I can retain an accurate actionable work list until the source is resolved. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P10.

#### Scenario: US27-P22 AC27-P10-01

- **GIVEN** the lead, creator, Party representative and finance owner are different people,
- **WHEN** review responsibility and due attention resolve,
- **THEN** only the qualified current lead or authorized explicit review assignee receives the appropriate source-backed work; other identities receive nothing without independent qualification.

#### Scenario: US27-P22 AC27-P10-02

- **GIVEN** a currently eligible explicit review assignee exists,
- **WHEN** the partnership lead changes,
- **THEN** the explicit review responsibility remains until a deliberate qualified handoff changes it; no duplicate recipient is inferred.

#### Scenario: US27-P22 AC27-P10-03

- **GIVEN** an enabled staff or missionary recipient profile is used,
- **WHEN** the user opens the task and attention,
- **THEN** each route resolves the same source review and allowed action with a working role-safe authenticated link; incomplete or reserved profiles cannot emit substitute attention.

#### Scenario: US27-P22 AC27-P10-04

- **GIVEN** a user reads an actionable review notification,
- **WHEN** they revisit work or try to archive it,
- **THEN** the unresolved work remains actionable and archive is unavailable; after source ending it is visible only within the authorized 90-day Recent ceiling.

#### Scenario: US27-P22 AC27-P10-05

- **GIVEN** zero eligible recipients, an indeterminate resolver or revocation immediately before release occurs,
- **WHEN** the worker processes the review,
- **THEN** no unqualified recipient receives attention and an authorized source repair exception remains; restored access does not revive an ended engagement.

#### Scenario: US27-P22 AC27-P10-06

- **GIVEN** two due workers and a reschedule/resume/end contend,
- **WHEN** both serialization orders and response-loss retries are exercised,
- **THEN** there is one intended admitted attention, no stale active attention or successor overwrite, and no old prepared envelope is sent to a newly chosen recipient.

#### Scenario: US27-P22 AC27-P10-07

- **GIVEN** a protected reason, restricted identity or worker location is associated with the source,
- **WHEN** task previews, notification payloads, logs, counts, exports and links are examined under each admitted role,
- **THEN** only independently allowed minimal metadata appears; public and donor surfaces receive no review context.

#### Scenario: US27-P22 AC27-P10-08

- **GIVEN** the optional email provider or Studio is unavailable,
- **WHEN** a qualified internal-only review becomes due,
- **THEN** native source work and permitted in-product attention remain functional without a substitute email or duplicate Studio effect.

### Requirement: US27-P23 — Close finished work after understanding relevant obligations

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to close finished work after understanding relevant obligations, so that I can preserve promised follow-through and independent work. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P11.

#### Scenario: US27-P23 AC27-P11-01

- **GIVEN** staff inspect cases of declined ask, ongoing promised prayer follow-through, continuing giving without further development work and a deliberate temporary pause,
- **WHEN** they choose a source action,
- **THEN** the UI distinguishes these meanings and prevents treating missing date or visible emptiness alone as completed work.

#### Scenario: US27-P23 AC27-P11-02

- **GIVEN** a relevant promise exists outside the first page or is protected from the actor,
- **WHEN** closure readiness is evaluated,
- **THEN** complete owner evidence determines readiness; permitted minimal outcomes disclose only authorized information and unknown checks cannot pass as empty.

#### Scenario: US27-P23 AC27-P11-03

- **GIVEN** an independent obligation has valid continuing authority and responsibility,
- **WHEN** closure disposes the development-specific work,
- **THEN** the independent obligation stays findable and actionable; reassigning the same continuing development work does not falsely satisfy readiness.

#### Scenario: US27-P23 AC27-P11-04

- **GIVEN** close races new work, review changes or authority revocation through any admitted writer,
- **WHEN** real transactions exercise both orders,
- **THEN** no accepted combination strands required work or permits stale authority; closure and the incompatible new work cannot both pass separate stale prechecks.

#### Scenario: US27-P23 AC27-P11-05

- **GIVEN** a review is actually performed before End, or a future review is made unnecessary by direct Close,
- **WHEN** the action is accepted,
- **THEN** the first outcome is Completed and the second No longer required; unrelated task reasons survive and queued obsolete attention cannot newly release.

#### Scenario: US27-P23 AC27-P11-06

- **GIVEN** staff close one of several scoped relationships,
- **WHEN** current views, counts and financial/contact sources are reconciled,
- **THEN** only that enrollment leaves current-work scope; the preserved stage, requests, assignments, gifts, consent and other enrollments are unchanged and no last-contact/successful-ask increment occurs.

#### Scenario: US27-P23 AC27-P11-07

- **GIVEN** close response is lost and a retry occurs after a later transition or revoked access,
- **WHEN** the original intent is recovered,
- **THEN** one accepted effect retains original actor/time; current authority governs result disclosure and the stale intent cannot close or reopen newer work.

#### Scenario: US27-P23 AC27-P11-08

- **GIVEN** closed work is viewed under current permissions after redaction or lawful content deletion,
- **WHEN** history and derived reports are opened,
- **THEN** the admitted structural history remains attributable without restoring forbidden content, changing closed-period interpretation or fabricating successful outcomes.

### Requirement: US27-P24 — Find permitted closed-work history

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to find permitted closed-work history, so that I can understand what ended without confusing closure with a failed relationship. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P11, REQ27-P31.

#### Scenario: US27-P24 AC27-P11-01

- **GIVEN** staff inspect cases of declined ask, ongoing promised prayer follow-through, continuing giving without further development work and a deliberate temporary pause,
- **WHEN** they choose a source action,
- **THEN** the UI distinguishes these meanings and prevents treating missing date or visible emptiness alone as completed work.

#### Scenario: US27-P24 AC27-P11-02

- **GIVEN** a relevant promise exists outside the first page or is protected from the actor,
- **WHEN** closure readiness is evaluated,
- **THEN** complete owner evidence determines readiness; permitted minimal outcomes disclose only authorized information and unknown checks cannot pass as empty.

#### Scenario: US27-P24 AC27-P11-03

- **GIVEN** an independent obligation has valid continuing authority and responsibility,
- **WHEN** closure disposes the development-specific work,
- **THEN** the independent obligation stays findable and actionable; reassigning the same continuing development work does not falsely satisfy readiness.

#### Scenario: US27-P24 AC27-P11-04

- **GIVEN** close races new work, review changes or authority revocation through any admitted writer,
- **WHEN** real transactions exercise both orders,
- **THEN** no accepted combination strands required work or permits stale authority; closure and the incompatible new work cannot both pass separate stale prechecks.

#### Scenario: US27-P24 AC27-P11-05

- **GIVEN** a review is actually performed before End, or a future review is made unnecessary by direct Close,
- **WHEN** the action is accepted,
- **THEN** the first outcome is Completed and the second No longer required; unrelated task reasons survive and queued obsolete attention cannot newly release.

#### Scenario: US27-P24 AC27-P11-06

- **GIVEN** staff close one of several scoped relationships,
- **WHEN** current views, counts and financial/contact sources are reconciled,
- **THEN** only that enrollment leaves current-work scope; the preserved stage, requests, assignments, gifts, consent and other enrollments are unchanged and no last-contact/successful-ask increment occurs.

#### Scenario: US27-P24 AC27-P11-07

- **GIVEN** close response is lost and a retry occurs after a later transition or revoked access,
- **WHEN** the original intent is recovered,
- **THEN** one accepted effect retains original actor/time; current authority governs result disclosure and the stale intent cannot close or reopen newer work.

#### Scenario: US27-P24 AC27-P11-08

- **GIVEN** closed work is viewed under current permissions after redaction or lawful content deletion,
- **WHEN** history and derived reports are opened,
- **THEN** the admitted structural history remains attributable without restoring forbidden content, changing closed-period interpretation or fabricating successful outcomes.

#### Scenario: US27-P24 AC27-P31-01

- **GIVEN** the latest qualifying source fact or relevant obligation lies beyond all current display caps,
- **WHEN** the complete projection is computed,
- **THEN** the admitted total or latest fact includes that evidence or explicitly reports incomplete coverage, never a falsely complete capped result.

#### Scenario: US27-P24 AC27-P31-02

- **GIVEN** finance source coverage is absent or amounts use different currencies/periods and recognition meanings,
- **WHEN** a cultivation summary is displayed,
- **THEN** unknown is not zero and incomparable values are not summed, converted or represented as received cash.

#### Scenario: US27-P24 AC27-P31-03

- **GIVEN** one source gift or encounter appears in multiple allowed views,
- **WHEN** organization totals and source identity are reconciled,
- **THEN** the source fact is counted once for its admitted aggregate meaning and remains a reference rather than another financial or encounter record.

#### Scenario: US27-P24 AC27-P31-04

- **GIVEN** the newest source fact is corrected, restricted, merged or erased,
- **WHEN** derived views rebuild,
- **THEN** the result may move backward or become unknown; stale cached maxima and forbidden derivatives do not remain authoritative.

#### Scenario: US27-P24 AC27-P31-05

- **GIVEN** a protected fact differs between otherwise equivalent fixtures,
- **WHEN** authorized summaries, ranks, filters and export contents are compared,
- **THEN** the restricted fact does not change admitted output unless an independently qualified aggregate disclosure exists.

#### Scenario: US27-P24 AC27-P31-06

- **GIVEN** staff change a ministry target, stage, lifecycle or relationship purpose,
- **WHEN** source owners are reconciled,
- **THEN** gifts, commitments, Designations, consent, recurring support and publishing authority do not change by implication.

#### Scenario: US27-P24 AC27-P31-07

- **GIVEN** a reader fails or a claimed-complete projection disagrees with source evidence,
- **WHEN** the authorized user opens the affected view,
- **THEN** it exposes incomplete/unavailable or pending reconciliation truth and a permitted owner resolution route instead of false zero or healthy status.

### Requirement: US27-P25 — Reopen the same relationship when genuine work returns

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to reopen the same relationship when genuine work returns, so that I can retain every prior cycle without reviving completed tasks or revoked access. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P12.

#### Scenario: US27-P25 AC27-P12-01

- **GIVEN** closed ordinary work returns for the same Party/ministry/purpose/scope,
- **WHEN** authorized staff deliberately reopen,
- **THEN** the same enrollment preserves original start and closure history and records the new return cycle.

#### Scenario: US27-P25 AC27-P12-02

- **GIVEN** a relationship has changed people, restrictions and consumed child effects since closure,
- **WHEN** it is reopened,
- **THEN** current eligibility and responsibility are revalidated and old permissions, tasks, notifications, redacted bodies and consumed effects are not revived.

#### Scenario: US27-P25 AC27-P12-03

- **GIVEN** the current stage was explicitly cleared before closure,
- **WHEN** staff reopen,
- **THEN** Not yet assessed remains the authoritative absence; the last historical non-null stage is not reinstated.

#### Scenario: US27-P25 AC27-P12-04

- **GIVEN** the current recorded stage is historical or retired,
- **WHEN** staff reopen,
- **THEN** it remains understandable as previously recorded with genuine provenance, without fabricated new assessment/contact time or forced mapping.

#### Scenario: US27-P25 AC27-P12-05

- **GIVEN** a concurrent authorized stage update or a later close wins before a stale reopen/retry,
- **WHEN** the older request arrives,
- **THEN** it cannot overwrite the newer stage or reopen the work again; one authorized cycle outcome remains.

#### Scenario: US27-P25 AC27-P12-06

- **GIVEN** gift, message, referral, import or elapsed-time events arrive for paused or closed work,
- **WHEN** the sources process them,
- **THEN** qualified independent service/financial facts proceed and cultivation lifecycle remains unchanged.

#### Scenario: US27-P25 AC27-P12-07

- **GIVEN** a proposed return actually changes purpose, ministry or scope, or attempts mistaken-close undo/collision repair,
- **WHEN** staff choose an action,
- **THEN** ordinary reopen does not silently retarget or erase history; the qualified correction/disposition path is required.

### Requirement: US27-P26 — Keep the last recorded stage or absence when work reopens

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to keep the last recorded stage or absence when work reopens, so that I can avoid unnecessary reassessment and fabricated freshness. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P12.

#### Scenario: US27-P26 AC27-P12-01

- **GIVEN** closed ordinary work returns for the same Party/ministry/purpose/scope,
- **WHEN** authorized staff deliberately reopen,
- **THEN** the same enrollment preserves original start and closure history and records the new return cycle.

#### Scenario: US27-P26 AC27-P12-02

- **GIVEN** a relationship has changed people, restrictions and consumed child effects since closure,
- **WHEN** it is reopened,
- **THEN** current eligibility and responsibility are revalidated and old permissions, tasks, notifications, redacted bodies and consumed effects are not revived.

#### Scenario: US27-P26 AC27-P12-03

- **GIVEN** the current stage was explicitly cleared before closure,
- **WHEN** staff reopen,
- **THEN** Not yet assessed remains the authoritative absence; the last historical non-null stage is not reinstated.

#### Scenario: US27-P26 AC27-P12-04

- **GIVEN** the current recorded stage is historical or retired,
- **WHEN** staff reopen,
- **THEN** it remains understandable as previously recorded with genuine provenance, without fabricated new assessment/contact time or forced mapping.

#### Scenario: US27-P26 AC27-P12-05

- **GIVEN** a concurrent authorized stage update or a later close wins before a stale reopen/retry,
- **WHEN** the older request arrives,
- **THEN** it cannot overwrite the newer stage or reopen the work again; one authorized cycle outcome remains.

#### Scenario: US27-P26 AC27-P12-06

- **GIVEN** gift, message, referral, import or elapsed-time events arrive for paused or closed work,
- **WHEN** the sources process them,
- **THEN** qualified independent service/financial facts proceed and cultivation lifecycle remains unchanged.

#### Scenario: US27-P26 AC27-P12-07

- **GIVEN** a proposed return actually changes purpose, ministry or scope, or attempts mistaken-close undo/collision repair,
- **WHEN** staff choose an action,
- **THEN** ordinary reopen does not silently retarget or erase history; the qualified correction/disposition path is required.

### Requirement: US27-P27 — Adapt approved stage names, guidance and order

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized organization configurator, I want to adapt approved stage names, guidance and order, so that I can fit the organization process while preserving historical meaning. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P14.

#### Scenario: US27-P27 AC27-P14-01

- **GIVEN** An organization catalog, supplied definitions and existing partnership work with stable identities.

- **WHEN** the specified configuration, template, display or identity operation is performed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

An appropriately authorized tenant can add a genuinely additional stage, edit same-meaning wording, retire/restore and reorder without code/schema deployment; an untouched tenant can use its starter preset. No mandatory universal fixed-stage mapping, personal pipeline or workflow builder is needed.

#### Scenario: US27-P27 AC27-P14-02

- **GIVEN** An organization catalog, supplied definitions and existing partnership work with stable identities.

- **WHEN** the specified configuration, template, display or identity operation is performed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Repeat rename/reorder/restore and a separate changed-meaning addition. Existing journey IDs, accepted stage identity, assessment/entry/contact timestamps and effects remain unchanged. Authorized history reconstructs the relevant earlier meaning; unknown imported dates remain unknown.

#### Scenario: US27-P27 AC27-P14-03

- **GIVEN** a Tenant declines a template update,
- **WHEN** a required platform security or authorization change takes effect,
- **THEN** the platform change remains required independently of voluntary template adoption.

### Requirement: US27-P28 — Retire and restore stages safely

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized organization configurator, I want to retire and restore stages safely, so that I can stop new use without breaking current work or losing history. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P15.

#### Scenario: US27-P28 AC27-P15-01

- **GIVEN** A list with the specified active or retired definitions and existing references.

- **WHEN** retirement, restoration, concurrent last-stage edits or retained-reference use is attempted.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Retire an in-use stage across active/paused/closed journeys. New selections fail; existing authorized detail, board, filters and counts retain the records. Task/owner edits and D9 reopening still work without stage reassessment. Explicit restore preserves identity/history and revives no ended work.

#### Scenario: US27-P28 AC27-P15-02

- **GIVEN** A list with the specified active or retired definitions and existing references.

- **WHEN** retirement, restoration, concurrent last-stage edits or retained-reference use is attempted.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Retiring the last selectable stage fails atomically with an actionable message. Retiring a separately configured default cannot silently change it; a valid explicitly supplied same-change treatment succeeds. Reorder alone changes neither default nor eligibility.

#### Scenario: US27-P28 AC27-P15-03

- **GIVEN** A list with the specified active or retired definitions and existing references.

- **WHEN** retirement, restoration, concurrent last-stage edits or retained-reference use is attempted.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Reopen with an unchanged retired stage and unknown provenance date; retain both without a new assessment. Fresh retired selection fails. Same-value no-op, renamed guidance and locale changes do not create transitions or reset dates. Qualified historical corrections/imports retain their separate treatment.

#### Scenario: US27-P28 AC27-P15-04

- **GIVEN** A list with the specified active or retired definitions and existing references.

- **WHEN** retirement, restoration, concurrent last-stage edits or retained-reference use is attempted.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Interleave target retirement/restoration, rename and reorder with selection. An accepted-before-retirement reference is retained; retirement-first rejects fresh admission. A same-meaning rename preserves identity. No fallback selection, cross-preset target or implicit history rewrite occurs.

#### Scenario: US27-P28 AC27-P15-05

- **GIVEN** A list with the specified active or retired definitions and existing references.
