- **WHEN** retirement, restoration, concurrent last-stage edits or retained-reference use is attempted.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Reject removal of the last selectable stage on a retained usable list, including withdrawn/no-visible-use cases and two concurrent removals. An explicit atomic replacement succeeds; no hidden journey count is required or disclosed.

### Requirement: US27-P29 — Create an additional usable approved list

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized organization configurator, I want to create an additional usable approved list, so that I can support a genuinely different organization process without a workflow builder. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P16.

#### Scenario: US27-P29 AC27-P16-01

- **GIVEN** The stated organization configuration, naming scope, permissions and creation input.

- **WHEN** creation, collision, cancellation or creator-departure handling is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

A tenant can continue with supplied lists, customize one, or deliberately create an additional usable list under current authority. No individual/team/location/view requires another list automatically; same ordinary work is not duplicated. Existing configuration remains intact.

#### Scenario: US27-P29 AC27-P16-02

- **GIVEN** The stated organization configuration, naming scope, permissions and creation input.

- **WHEN** creation, collision, cancellation or creator-departure handling is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Test blank/whitespace names, exact canonical conflicts, simultaneous name collision, similar names, Unicode and translated display collisions under the documented policy. For an actor lacking minimal availability/conflict disclosure, hidden-present and absent names yield the same permitted unavailable outcome without attempting an existence-revealing create. Feedback is permitted and field-linked; no identity/intent merge by name, arbitrary ASCII restriction or forced legacy renaming. Same stage label in two lists retains different scoped identities and understandable context in offered combined views.

#### Scenario: US27-P29 AC27-P16-03

- **GIVEN** The stated organization configuration, naming scope, permissions and creation input.

- **WHEN** creation, collision, cancellation or creator-departure handling is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Fail before each required stage, configuration-history and receipt write and before commit. No partially accepted list is selectable or visible as success. A concurrent ordinary observer sees no uncommitted shell. A successful new list records actual definitions, trusted actor/time and durable result once; all existing configuration/work stays intact.

#### Scenario: US27-P29 AC27-P16-04

- **GIVEN** a separately qualified configuration-copy operation is offered,
- **WHEN** permitted configuration is copied,
- **THEN** new scoped identities are independent and no source journeys, visits, grants, defaults, applicability or consumed effects are implicitly copied.

### Requirement: US27-P30 — Withdraw a list from new use while retaining its existing work

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized organization configurator, I want to withdraw a list from new use while retaining its existing work, so that I can change the organization process without forced migration. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P17.

#### Scenario: US27-P30 AC27-P17-01

- **GIVEN** Available or withdrawn lists and the stated existing, returning or proposed work.

- **WHEN** new-use availability changes or admission/continued-use operations are exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Before releasing creation, enforce the settled whole-list lifecycle, shared approved-list choice, optional usual-list initializer, deliberate same-work switching and optional destination assessment. Real scenarios cover existing/unassessed/paused/closed journeys under their current qualified lifecycle policy, historical references, concurrent admission/edit, last usable stage, zero new-use lists, applicable defaults and recovery. Stage-level retirement or hard deletion does not substitute for the whole-list lifecycle contract.

#### Scenario: US27-P30 AC27-P17-02

- **GIVEN** Available or withdrawn lists and the stated existing, returning or proposed work.

- **WHEN** new-use availability changes or admission/continued-use operations are exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Withdraw an available list: new admission cannot use it, while previously attached assessed work can select available stages forward, backward or by skipping. One normal stage event records an actual change; withdrawal creates none.

#### Scenario: US27-P30 AC27-P17-03

- **GIVEN** Available or withdrawn lists and the stated existing, returning or proposed work.

- **WHEN** new-use availability changes or admission/continued-use operations are exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Existing work remains in authorized staff/missionary lists, boards, search and reports with the same journey, list, stage, lead and due work. No gifts, consent, task or contact effects occur.

#### Scenario: US27-P30 AC27-P17-04

- **GIVEN** Available or withdrawn lists and the stated existing, returning or proposed work.

- **WHEN** new-use availability changes or admission/continued-use operations are exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Existing unassessed work can make its first later assessment on the withdrawn list; no fabricated initial stage/date or compulsory review is added.

#### Scenario: US27-P30 AC27-P17-05

- **GIVEN** Available or withdrawn lists and the stated existing, returning or proposed work.

- **WHEN** new-use availability changes or admission/continued-use operations are exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Resume/reopen the same eligible journey on its withdrawn list: current source binding and stage/absence survive; later stage choice works. Prior ended tasks remain ended. Current permission/return requirements still apply.

#### Scenario: US27-P30 AC27-P17-06

- **GIVEN** Available or withdrawn lists and the stated existing, returning or proposed work.

- **WHEN** new-use availability changes or admission/continued-use operations are exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Restore availability on the same list ID; one configuration event records the real change. Individually retired stages/defaults/grants/tasks remain unchanged. New admission becomes possible only when current applicability and other checks pass. An applicable current naming collision requires permitted explicit resolution or rejection, without silent rename, merge or disclosure.

#### Scenario: US27-P30 AC27-P17-07

- **GIVEN** Available or withdrawn lists and the stated existing, returning or proposed work.

- **WHEN** new-use availability changes or admission/continued-use operations are exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Retiring an unused mistaken list removes it from new-work choices while keeping authorized settings/history and reversal available. Cancel before creation commits leaves no persisted list. No normal hard-delete path erases retained facts.

#### Scenario: US27-P30 AC27-P17-08

- **GIVEN** Available or withdrawn lists and the stated existing, returning or proposed work.

- **WHEN** new-use availability changes or admission/continued-use operations are exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Deliberately withdraw the last admission-eligible list in a valid scope with explicit valid dependency treatment. Existing work remains usable; new start explains missing configuration and preserves input. Required unresolved dependency prevents acceptance without exposing hidden rows.

#### Scenario: US27-P30 AC27-P17-09

- **GIVEN** withdrawal depends on a different owner disposition and optionally the matching usual-list reference,
- **WHEN** staff perform withdrawal while dependency state changes,
- **THEN** acceptance revalidates completed owner treatment or rejects; the supported matching-default removal is atomic and no partial cross-owner success is claimed.

### Requirement: US27-P31 — Choose an eligible approved list from the shared catalog

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to choose an eligible approved list from the shared catalog, so that I can use the appropriate process with clear options. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P18.

#### Scenario: US27-P31 AC27-P18-01

- **GIVEN** The stated authorized choice set, new-work draft or existing ordinary partnership.

- **WHEN** choice, admission, reuse or a reviewed-group member is processed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Concurrent known-stage and unassessed starts for the same ordinary journey produce the permitted single identity and accurate accepted/reused outcomes. An unassessed replay cannot erase the winning stage; different member outcomes remain independently recoverable.

#### Scenario: US27-P31 AC27-P18-02

- **GIVEN** The stated authorized choice set, new-work draft or existing ordinary partnership.

- **WHEN** choice, admission, reuse or a reviewed-group member is processed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

All offered staff/missionary views of one journey show its source list/stage consistently under permission. Viewing settings or changing a view filter changes no source list. An established list does not cause a repeated chooser; unresolved applicability never uses first list/order as authority. Loading/filtered empty/true empty/denied/failure are distinct.

#### Scenario: US27-P31 AC27-P18-03

- **GIVEN** The stated authorized choice set, new-work draft or existing ordinary partnership.

- **WHEN** choice, admission, reuse or a reviewed-group member is processed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Given the same valid source/permission scope, approved new-use lists are not further filtered by viewer app, Party type, ministry/team label or an unselected process rule. Unauthorized/withdrawn choices remain unavailable.

#### Scenario: US27-P31 AC27-P18-04

- **GIVEN** The stated authorized choice set, new-work draft or existing ordinary partnership.

- **WHEN** choice, admission, reuse or a reviewed-group member is processed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

A church supporting a missionary can use the appropriate approved process without a hidden church-versus-missionary gate. Names and permitted guidance/details help distinguish purpose; missing optional guidance does not invalidate a list.

#### Scenario: US27-P31 AC27-P18-05

- **GIVEN** The stated authorized choice set, new-work draft or existing ordinary partnership.

- **WHEN** choice, admission, reuse or a reviewed-group member is processed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

An already established valid choice is clearly shown without repeated selection. A missing choice is understandable; opening, searching and previewing do not admit work or modify a source record. The qualified initializer governs genuine unresolved choices.

#### Scenario: US27-P31 AC27-P18-06

- **GIVEN** The stated authorized choice set, new-work draft or existing ordinary partnership.

- **WHEN** choice, admission, reuse or a reviewed-group member is processed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

In an unsent form, change from list A with proposed stage A1 to list B containing a same-label B1. Preserve other inputs, visibly resolve the invalid stage and admit only the explicitly final valid B/stage-or-absence intent. No name/rank mapping occurs.

#### Scenario: US27-P31 AC27-P18-07

- **GIVEN** The stated authorized choice set, new-work draft or existing ordinary partnership.

- **WHEN** choice, admission, reuse or a reviewed-group member is processed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

With paged/partial choices or concurrent catalog edits, no sole/default inference arises from a loaded subset. An unrelated new list or same-meaning rename does not require re-entering unaffected valid information.

#### Scenario: US27-P31 AC27-P18-08

- **GIVEN** The stated authorized choice set, new-work draft or existing ordinary partnership.

- **WHEN** choice, admission, reuse or a reviewed-group member is processed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Start intent discovers an existing ordinary journey with another or withdrawn retained list: authorized reuse preserves its binding/stage/lead and returns a truthful result, without creating a second journey or switching it. Hidden reuse remains protected.

#### Scenario: US27-P31 AC27-P18-09

- **GIVEN** The stated authorized choice set, new-work draft or existing ordinary partnership.

- **WHEN** choice, admission, reuse or a reviewed-group member is processed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

A fixed reviewed group mixes accepted, permitted reused, newly ineligible and unconfirmed members. Preserve accepted work, independently reject later invalid members, reconcile unknown results and never expand or retarget the reviewed group.

#### Scenario: US27-P31 AC27-P18-10

- **GIVEN** The stated authorized choice set, new-work draft or existing ordinary partnership.

- **WHEN** choice, admission, reuse or a reviewed-group member is processed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Change unsent A/stage-A to B with a same-label stage-B: visibly resolve the incompatible stage, preserve other input and submit only B plus its explicitly final valid stage or allowed absence. The server rejects an invalid submitted pair instead of silently nulling/mapping it.

#### Scenario: US27-P31 AC27-P18-11

- **GIVEN** The stated authorized choice set, new-work draft or existing ordinary partnership.

- **WHEN** choice, admission, reuse or a reviewed-group member is processed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Prefilling, changing, clearing or restoring configuration leaves every existing journey's list/stage/history unchanged. Starting intent that resolves to existing work reuses its actual binding under D3/D4/D15, without an implicit saved switch or duplicate ordinary journey.

#### Scenario: US27-P31 AC27-P18-12

- **GIVEN** The stated authorized choice set, new-work draft or existing ordinary partnership.

- **WHEN** choice, admission, reuse or a reviewed-group member is processed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

At acceptance, withdrawn/unauthorized/wrong-scope list, absent required list and wrong-list stage reject safely. A still-eligible chosen list succeeds even if no longer default/sole. Stage absence never permits an invalid parent scope.

#### Scenario: US27-P31 AC27-P18-13

- **GIVEN** The stated authorized choice set, new-work draft or existing ordinary partnership.

- **WHEN** choice, admission, reuse or a reviewed-group member is processed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Submit concrete A and lose the response, then change organization default to B: retry/group execution preserves original normalized A intent and reconciles one result. Omitted list is not “use latest default.” Each reviewed member retains current authorization and its own truthful acceptance/reuse outcome.

### Requirement: US27-P32 — Begin with a visible appropriate starting list

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to begin with a visible appropriate starting list, so that I can save effort while retaining control over the choice. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P19.

#### Scenario: US27-P32 AC27-P19-01

- **GIVEN** The stated catalog coverage, usual-list setting, established intent and authorization.

- **WHEN** initialization, configuration, refetch, withdrawal or a dependent start occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Fresh context with several eligible lists and a permitted valid usual reference visibly prefills that list; other approved choices remain available. With no usable default, ask; do not infer one by rank, app, role, Party type or prior personal use.

#### Scenario: US27-P32 AC27-P19-02

- **GIVEN** The stated catalog coverage, usual-list setting, established intent and authorization.

- **WHEN** initialization, configuration, refetch, withdrawal or a dependent start occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Authorized configuration sets, replaces and clears the optional usual reference without requiring admission rights. A starter consumes only permitted initializer facts without needing settings-management powers. No default is required for an otherwise usable catalog.

#### Scenario: US27-P32 AC27-P19-03

- **GIVEN** The stated catalog coverage, usual-list setting, established intent and authorization.

- **WHEN** initialization, configuration, refetch, withdrawal or a dependent start occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Cover complete authorized eligible counts 0, 1, 2 and more than 2, plus a first page/search containing one of several matches, hidden lists and failed/partial reads. Prefill a sole list only from authoritative eligible coverage; filtered count is never proof.

#### Scenario: US27-P32 AC27-P19-04

- **GIVEN** The stated catalog coverage, usual-list setting, established intent and authorization.

- **WHEN** initialization, configuration, refetch, withdrawal or a dependent start occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Prefill A, then change/unset the default or add B while A stays eligible: the draft remains A while any now-false “usual” or “only choice” explanation updates or disappears. Explicitly choose B before a late A-default response: B remains. No refresh or untouched-form reset changes either established choice.

#### Scenario: US27-P32 AC27-P19-05

- **GIVEN** The stated catalog coverage, usual-list setting, established intent and authorization.

- **WHEN** initialization, configuration, refetch, withdrawal or a dependent start occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

If clearing is offered, deliberately clear the proposed list and refetch: it remains unresolved until valid user action. Edit an unrelated field while defaults load: that edit is preserved and does not wrongly prevent legitimate initialization of the untouched list field.

#### Scenario: US27-P32 AC27-P19-06

- **GIVEN** The stated catalog coverage, usual-list setting, established intent and authorization.

- **WHEN** initialization, configuration, refetch, withdrawal or a dependent start occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Default helper failure is shown truthfully and supports retry; independently authorized explicit or proved sole choice still proceeds through normal validation. Failure is neither configured absence nor permission to reveal/use a concealed default.

#### Scenario: US27-P32 AC27-P19-07

- **GIVEN** The stated catalog coverage, usual-list setting, established intent and authorization.

- **WHEN** initialization, configuration, refetch, withdrawal or a dependent start occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Race Set A with withdrawal in both orders; exercise stale expected revision, pre-replaced B and prior clearing. No accepted default points outside its qualified scope or to a list unavailable for new work; starter-specific inability to discover/use it prevents application without clearing the organization setting; withdrawal clears only expressly included matching A. Failure of required history/result rolls back the combined source mutation.

#### Scenario: US27-P32 AC27-P19-08

- **GIVEN** The stated catalog coverage, usual-list setting, established intent and authorization.

- **WHEN** initialization, configuration, refetch, withdrawal or a dependent start occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Set/replace/clear with same-value new intent, exact retry and same-key changed payload yield distinct truthful outcomes; no duplicate configuration history, stale overwrite or old result presented as current state.

#### Scenario: US27-P32 AC27-P19-09

- **GIVEN** The stated catalog coverage, usual-list setting, established intent and authorization.

- **WHEN** initialization, configuration, refetch, withdrawal or a dependent start occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

After withdrawal and same-ID restoration, the former default remains unset unless deliberately configured again. Replaying an old accepted Set A, old tab or import cannot reinstate it.

### Requirement: US27-P34 — Choose any appropriate eligible stage directly

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to choose any appropriate eligible stage directly, so that I can use judgment without artificial sequential clicks. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P20.

#### Scenario: US27-P34 AC27-P20-01

- **GIVEN** The stated recorded stage, source obligations and approved stage definitions.

- **WHEN** an ordinary stage choice, skip, return, no-op or advisory-guidance interaction occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Given valid current authority, eligible target and satisfied source obligations, save an ordinary stage change with no explanation, acknowledgment or tenant-required field. No new note/checklist/approval/task is demanded merely for the change. Unsafe optional content, if separately supplied, follows its own validation and may be removed.

#### Scenario: US27-P34 AC27-P20-02

- **GIVEN** The stated recorded stage, source obligations and approved stage definitions.

- **WHEN** an ordinary stage choice, skip, return, no-op or advisory-guidance interaction occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

An untouched missing newly required Party/custom field does not block the stage-only action and is absent from its payload. An actually requested governed field edit still receives its owner's validation. No filler, default or private data is added to bypass a form.

#### Scenario: US27-P34 AC27-P20-03

- **GIVEN** The stated recorded stage, source obligations and approved stage definitions.

- **WHEN** an ordinary stage choice, skip, return, no-op or advisory-guidance interaction occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

An authorized otherwise-valid direct skip/backward update succeeds with one deliberate commit, no intermediate clicks, compulsory note, acknowledgment, approval or unrelated profile field. Ineligible/unauthorized targets still fail. Display reorder does not change allowed ordinary moves.

#### Scenario: US27-P34 AC27-P20-04

- **GIVEN** The stated recorded stage, source obligations and approved stage definitions.

- **WHEN** an ordinary stage choice, skip, return, no-op or advisory-guidance interaction occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Starting at X, accept X→Z: exactly that change exists, with no Y visit. Then Z→X: both changes remain with distinct source revisions/occurrences. No new supporter, gift, consent, closure, contact or completed task is inferred.

#### Scenario: US27-P34 AC27-P20-05

- **GIVEN** The stated recorded stage, source obligations and approved stage definitions.

- **WHEN** an ordinary stage choice, skip, return, no-op or advisory-guidance interaction occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

A same-value save creates no new stage history, entry/assessment time or effect. Retry an earlier accepted mutation and an earlier no-op after intervening changes: each returns only its currently disclosable historical result and cannot mutate or overwrite the current value.

#### Scenario: US27-P34 AC27-P20-06

- **GIVEN** an ordinary skip or return meets actual platform and source safeguards,
- **WHEN** the user selects and saves the destination,
- **THEN** tenant guidance is advisory and the flow adds no rank-derived regression warning, completion styling, compulsory information or extra ceremony.

- **GIVEN** The stated recorded stage, source obligations and approved stage definitions.

### Requirement: US27-P36 — Start valid work without a current assessment

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to start valid work without a current assessment, so that I can avoid making up a stage when I am not ready to assess. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P21.

#### Scenario: US27-P36 AC27-P21-01

- **GIVEN** The specified assessed, unassessed, retired, unavailable or restricted source facts.

- **WHEN** admission, assessment, read, grouping or serialization is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

From each offered native/API/reviewed-group admission path, valid known-stage and valid no-stage intent both succeed under D4. One deliberate commit suffices; no mandatory placeholder, note, extra confirmation or invented stage event. Required work/accountability and usable catalog remain.

#### Scenario: US27-P36 AC27-P21-02

- **GIVEN** The specified assessed, unassessed, retired, unavailable or restricted source facts.

- **WHEN** admission, assessment, read, grouping or serialization is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Add, rename, reorder and retire organization stages under D10. Omission remains understandable/available, known assessment remains selectable, and rank supplies no default. A real stage labeled like the absence label is unambiguously distinct. Zero-selectable catalog remains invalid.

#### Scenario: US27-P36 AC27-P21-03

- **GIVEN** The specified assessed, unassessed, retired, unavailable or restricted source facts.

- **WHEN** admission, assessment, read, grouping or serialization is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Verify real absence, known/retired stage, hidden field, options loading/failure, bad reference, partial source and legacy uncertainty. Independently verified stage knowledge survives an options-only failure; an empty filtered list is not proof of invalid catalog. None is silently converted to another; a rejected selected stage does not become an unassessed success. No private existence leak through explanation/counts.

#### Scenario: US27-P36 AC27-P21-04

- **GIVEN** The specified assessed, unassessed, retired, unavailable or restricted source facts.

- **WHEN** admission, assessment, read, grouping or serialization is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

New-start omission is allowed. Updating another field does not clear a stage. Generic clear/delete/upsert or incidental explicit null cannot substitute for the deliberate same-list clearing command required by REQ27-P28. Known-stage intent from one reviewed member is not replaced by group fallback/default.

#### Scenario: US27-P36 AC27-P21-05

- **GIVEN** The specified assessed, unassessed, retired, unavailable or restricted source facts.

- **WHEN** admission, assessment, read, grouping or serialization is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Compare pagination, list, offered board, filters, counts and exports for the same declared scope with unassessed, assessed, retired, denied and failed-coverage fixtures. No implicit stage join drops work; unknown/partial totals are not zero. After first assessment, a filtered row's removal has truthful success and usable focus. A board shall not clear a stage merely by applying a null patch; any offered clearing interaction shall express the deliberate reviewed consequence and use REQ27-P28's qualified source command.

#### Scenario: US27-P36 AC27-P21-06

- **GIVEN** The specified assessed, unassessed, retired, unavailable or restricted source facts.

- **WHEN** admission, assessment, read, grouping or serialization is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

A truthful no-stage display is distinguishable from loading, denied data, unavailable labels, retired actual stages, failed read and incomplete option coverage or a real same-named stage; no unseen stale value is submitted. An independent stage-options failure does not prevent an otherwise validated intentional no-stage change.

#### Scenario: US27-P36 AC27-P21-07

- **GIVEN** The specified assessed, unassessed, retired, unavailable or restricted source facts.

- **WHEN** admission, assessment, read, grouping or serialization is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Authorized mixed assessed/unassessed portfolio fixtures reconcile counts and pagination at exact boundaries; restricted/incomplete coverage is truthfully represented, and supported measures exclude fabricated stage duration.

#### Scenario: US27-P36 AC27-P21-08

- **GIVEN** optional assessment is stored or projected,
- **WHEN** the same authoritative stage reference is absent or present,
- **THEN** absence and assessment derive from that source with history, with no independently writable assessedness state or fake configured stage.

### Requirement: US27-P37 — Distinguish no assessment from unavailable or protected data

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to distinguish no assessment from unavailable or protected data, so that I can trust what the record currently means. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P21, REQ27-P26.

#### Scenario: US27-P37 AC27-P21-01

- **GIVEN** The specified assessed, unassessed, retired, unavailable or restricted source facts.

- **WHEN** admission, assessment, read, grouping or serialization is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

From each offered native/API/reviewed-group admission path, valid known-stage and valid no-stage intent both succeed under D4. One deliberate commit suffices; no mandatory placeholder, note, extra confirmation or invented stage event. Required work/accountability and usable catalog remain.

#### Scenario: US27-P37 AC27-P21-02

- **GIVEN** The specified assessed, unassessed, retired, unavailable or restricted source facts.

- **WHEN** admission, assessment, read, grouping or serialization is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Add, rename, reorder and retire organization stages under D10. Omission remains understandable/available, known assessment remains selectable, and rank supplies no default. A real stage labeled like the absence label is unambiguously distinct. Zero-selectable catalog remains invalid.

#### Scenario: US27-P37 AC27-P21-03

- **GIVEN** The specified assessed, unassessed, retired, unavailable or restricted source facts.

- **WHEN** admission, assessment, read, grouping or serialization is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Verify real absence, known/retired stage, hidden field, options loading/failure, bad reference, partial source and legacy uncertainty. Independently verified stage knowledge survives an options-only failure; an empty filtered list is not proof of invalid catalog. None is silently converted to another; a rejected selected stage does not become an unassessed success. No private existence leak through explanation/counts.

#### Scenario: US27-P37 AC27-P21-04

- **GIVEN** The specified assessed, unassessed, retired, unavailable or restricted source facts.

- **WHEN** admission, assessment, read, grouping or serialization is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

New-start omission is allowed. Updating another field does not clear a stage. Generic clear/delete/upsert or incidental explicit null cannot substitute for the deliberate same-list clearing command required by REQ27-P28. Known-stage intent from one reviewed member is not replaced by group fallback/default.

#### Scenario: US27-P37 AC27-P21-05

- **GIVEN** The specified assessed, unassessed, retired, unavailable or restricted source facts.

- **WHEN** admission, assessment, read, grouping or serialization is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Compare pagination, list, offered board, filters, counts and exports for the same declared scope with unassessed, assessed, retired, denied and failed-coverage fixtures. No implicit stage join drops work; unknown/partial totals are not zero. After first assessment, a filtered row's removal has truthful success and usable focus. A board shall not clear a stage merely by applying a null patch; any offered clearing interaction shall express the deliberate reviewed consequence and use REQ27-P28's qualified source command.

#### Scenario: US27-P37 AC27-P21-06

- **GIVEN** The specified assessed, unassessed, retired, unavailable or restricted source facts.

- **WHEN** admission, assessment, read, grouping or serialization is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

A truthful no-stage display is distinguishable from loading, denied data, unavailable labels, retired actual stages, failed read and incomplete option coverage or a real same-named stage; no unseen stale value is submitted. An independent stage-options failure does not prevent an otherwise validated intentional no-stage change.

#### Scenario: US27-P37 AC27-P21-07

- **GIVEN** The specified assessed, unassessed, retired, unavailable or restricted source facts.

- **WHEN** admission, assessment, read, grouping or serialization is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Authorized mixed assessed/unassessed portfolio fixtures reconcile counts and pagination at exact boundaries; restricted/incomplete coverage is truthfully represented, and supported measures exclude fabricated stage duration.

#### Scenario: US27-P37 AC27-P21-08

- **GIVEN** optional assessment is stored or projected,
- **WHEN** the same authoritative stage reference is absent or present,
- **THEN** absence and assessment derive from that source with history, with no independently writable assessedness state or fake configured stage.

#### Scenario: US27-P37 AC27-P26-01

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Add/select/retire/reorder stages with lifecycle-, finance-, consent- and task-like names. No money, pledge, participation, consent, closure, task or donor-message effect occurs merely from those labels/configuration. Existing D6 follow-up and D7/D8 lifecycle remain independently correct; Studio may be off.

#### Scenario: US27-P37 AC27-P26-02

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

A real stage change records actual actor/time/from/to provenance without requiring a narrative. It changes no contact/giving/consent/closure fact. Preserve valid existing work and expose genuine missing accountability; no duplicate task or false completion appears. Optional context is not copied into unrestricted logs. Where an existing qualified flow submits text with a stage, inject partial success/response loss: report each actual accepted outcome without discarding or duplicating text or certifying an unsaved note.

#### Scenario: US27-P37 AC27-P26-03

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Native updates work with Studio and unrelated providers unavailable. DON-14 cannot gate or reverse the accepted stage; duplicate/out-of-order downstream delivery preserves one source effect and current authority. Financial/prayer-like stage labels create no financial or participation evidence.

#### Scenario: US27-P37 AC27-P26-04

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Test the same authorized source meaning on staff and missionary projections, with native operation when optional Studio/providers are unavailable. Stage change does not resume/close work, alter assignments, waive contact suppression, certify participation or become a financial transaction. Initial stage/preset/correction/bulk capabilities are not smuggled in.

#### Scenario: US27-P37 AC27-P26-05

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Preserve applicable existing next work without duplicate chores; expose the owned actionable exception when appropriate. A repeat stage does not revive ended/consumed work. Deliver duplicate/out-of-order native/Studio events and ambiguous provider outcomes: current source applicability and one purpose identity control recovery.

#### Scenario: US27-P37 AC27-P26-06

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Unassessed active work has appropriate existing dated work/owned exception; missing accountability is still actionable. Absence alone creates zero new tasks/reminders/contact attempts and changes no giving, consent, participation, pause/close or completed-work fact. Optional Studio unavailable does not prevent qualified native operation.

#### Scenario: US27-P37 AC27-P26-07

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Accepted enrollment without stage is a real enrollment event, never a fake stage transition. Duplicate/delayed/out-of-order dispatch and projection failure preserve source truth and consumed-effect fences; optional systems do not block it. Authorized operators can diagnose/reconcile via the source without private narrative in logs or routine direct SQL repair.

#### Scenario: US27-P37 AC27-P26-08

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Compare accepted source identities, list references, stages, history, responsibilities, asks, work, consent and financial facts before/after creating another list. Active, paused, closed/reopened and unassessed examples remain unchanged. Creation is not a stage transition, acquisition, admission, donor response or instruction to send a message.

#### Scenario: US27-P37 AC27-P26-09

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Create/configure using real native sources with optional Studio/external providers unavailable. No new DON-14 binding or effect is inferred. Duplicate/out-of-order adapter events preserve source ownership, scoped intent and meaningful reconciliation; incompatible imports neither auto-create catalogs nor adopt another provider's labels as identity.

#### Scenario: US27-P37 AC27-P26-10

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Configuration retirement/restoration triggers no DON-14 enrollment/transition action, donor delivery, prayer/giving change or retirement-generated migration task. Authorized audit shows actor, list, before/after state, revision and business intent; unauthorized readers gain nothing.

#### Scenario: US27-P37 AC27-P26-11

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Exploratory choices create no business audit event, stage transition, task, DON-14 admission or outreach. Actual deliberate admission records its correct list and attribution and produces only its owner-qualified required effects once.

#### Scenario: US27-P37 AC27-P26-12

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

No prefill/default-change event creates a journey, stage assessment, task, outreach, consent or finance/participation fact. Actual admitted work records its concrete list and trusted actor and produces only the required owner-qualified effects once.

#### Scenario: US27-P37 AC27-P26-13

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

A switch emits no false enrollment/contact/consent/financial event and does not cancel or regenerate unrelated tasks/asks. Any genuinely required owner effect uses one durable purpose identity through native/optional consumers and retries.

#### Scenario: US27-P37 AC27-P26-14

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

List-change absence preserves owner/next work/review date/consent/financial facts and produces no independent task/contact/enrollment/activation. Qualified required effects survive retries/outages without duplication.

#### Scenario: US27-P37 AC27-P26-15

- **GIVEN** source state required for a mutation is unavailable or indeterminate,
- **WHEN** the dependent action is submitted,
- **THEN** the mutation does not proceed using invented absence; current permitted input and a truthful recovery route remain.

### Requirement: US27-P38 — Deliberately change the list on the same work

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to deliberately change the list on the same work, so that I can correct a mistaken selection or adapt the process while preserving earlier assessments. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P22.

#### Scenario: US27-P38 AC27-P22-01

- **GIVEN** The specified existing partnership, current binding and destination proposal.

- **WHEN** a deliberate list change, return, cancellation or conflicting proposal is processed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Change A to eligible B for the same authorized continuing partnership: journey ID, supporter, ministry, purpose, source scope and independent facts remain unchanged; there is one current binding and no second enrollment.

#### Scenario: US27-P38 AC27-P22-02

- **GIVEN** The specified existing partnership, current binding and destination proposal.

- **WHEN** a deliberate list change, return, cancellation or conflicting proposal is processed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Keep A, customize a same-meaning stage, or add an isolated task without changing the list. D18 never forces a switch because a default or catalog label/order changed. A genuinely different purpose cannot be smuggled into this command.

#### Scenario: US27-P38 AC27-P22-03

- **GIVEN** The specified existing partnership, current binding and destination proposal.

- **WHEN** a deliberate list change, return, cancellation or conflicting proposal is processed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Existing work on withdrawn A remains usable under D15. Moving to eligible B succeeds only under the qualified policy. Moving back to withdrawn A rejects even if A was used before; restoring A does not automatically move work.

#### Scenario: US27-P38 AC27-P22-04

- **GIVEN** The specified existing partnership, current binding and destination proposal.

- **WHEN** a deliberate list change, return, cancellation or conflicting proposal is processed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

A different-list change accepts an eligible destination stage or clearly intended no current stage coherently with its list, history and result. Wrong-list stage, unknown/retired selected target and absent required scope cannot be saved or silently converted to absence. Same labels/order do not map. Optional new-work assessment alone cannot authorize clearing a prior assessment outside the qualified change operation.

#### Scenario: US27-P38 AC27-P22-05

- **GIVEN** The specified existing partnership, current binding and destination proposal.

- **WHEN** a deliberate list change, return, cancellation or conflicting proposal is processed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

The offered lifecycle states match a ratified policy. Paused-work stage/list changes shall be allowed through REQ27-P29 without resuming work. Closed-work editing is not inferred or exposed through a generic patch, and a list change shall not reopen work. Bulk/cross-scope/historical commands are not inferred from this feature.

#### Scenario: US27-P38 AC27-P22-06

- **GIVEN** The specified existing partnership, current binding and destination proposal.

- **WHEN** a deliberate list change, return, cancellation or conflicting proposal is processed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Authorized assessed A → eligible B with intentional absence preserves identity/scope and accepted old assessment; current B has no stage and ordinary work remains visible.

#### Scenario: US27-P38 AC27-P22-07

- **GIVEN** The specified existing partnership, current binding and destination proposal.

- **WHEN** a deliberate list change, return, cancellation or conflicting proposal is processed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Already-unassessed A → B succeeds through the same clear Save with no extra acknowledgment, reason, assessment task or fabricated stage entry.

#### Scenario: US27-P38 AC27-P22-08

- **GIVEN** The specified existing partnership, current binding and destination proposal.

- **WHEN** a deliberate list change, return, cancellation or conflicting proposal is processed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

A → B with a valid B stage accepts the pair; equal names/order cannot make an A stage a valid B stage.

#### Scenario: US27-P38 AC27-P22-09

- **GIVEN** The specified existing partnership, current binding and destination proposal.

- **WHEN** a deliberate list change, return, cancellation or conflicting proposal is processed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Omitted, malformed, incompatible, hidden or unresolved stage input never becomes intentional absence. Explicit absence and explicit stage are valid distinguishable intent.

#### Scenario: US27-P38 AC27-P22-10

- **GIVEN** The specified existing partnership, current binding and destination proposal.

- **WHEN** a deliberate list change, return, cancellation or conflicting proposal is processed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Same-current-list selection, including an unsaved B-then-back-to-A draft, cannot clear A's saved stage. A stale A→B(null) after another A→B(stage) cannot clear B.

#### Scenario: US27-P38 AC27-P22-11

- **GIVEN** The specified existing partnership, current binding and destination proposal.

- **WHEN** a deliberate list change, return, cancellation or conflicting proposal is processed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Accepted A→B→A with intentional absence requires current A eligibility, retains all actual prior assessments and restores none automatically.
