### Requirement: US27-P40 — Have configuration and work changes obey exact current scope and permissions

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized tenant administrator, I want to have configuration and work changes obey exact current scope and permissions, so that I can protect records and histories across tenants, entities and roles. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P13, REQ27-P23.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P13-01

- **GIVEN** valid old rows are paired with hostile proposed Tenant, entity, Party, ministry, owner, actor or purpose fields,
- **WHEN** each admitted insert/update/upsert/delete/returning/RPC/service/import/repair path is exercised under real effective roles,
- **THEN** all independently forbidden resulting states and spoofed attribution fail without leaking hidden constraint/source details.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P13-02

- **GIVEN** source links are null, partially-null composite references, cross-Tenant or mismatched entity references,
- **WHEN** the command and applicable database constraints are exercised,
- **THEN** required identity cannot be fabricated and optional references are wholly absent or valid against their owners.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P13-03

- **GIVEN** member-without-grant, revoked-member, former-member-with-independent-grant and admitted service contexts exist,
- **WHEN** read/action/result commands are evaluated,
- **THEN** current exact authority determines outcomes; role labels, enrollment and membership neither add nor remove independent valid grants by inference.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P13-04

- **GIVEN** faults occur at every local acceptance/history/receipt/work/intent boundary,
- **WHEN** the command executes,
- **THEN** pre-commit failure leaves no partially accepted business change; post-commit secondary failure retains one source receipt and visible repairable pending/failed state.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P13-05

- **GIVEN** same intent is submitted through multiple transports or after a lost response,
- **WHEN** duplicates, changed payload and stale out-of-order events arrive,
- **THEN** one intended effect remains; changed meaning conflicts and old events/receipts do not overwrite newer state or acquire new disclosure authority.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P13-06

- **GIVEN** permission revocation and acceptance contend in independent transactions,
- **WHEN** each valid ordering is forced,
- **THEN** stale authority cannot create a new effect after the revocation boundary; accepted earlier history stays true without preserving future forbidden access.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P13-07

- **GIVEN** hidden source facts differ in paired fixtures while permitted inputs are identical,
- **WHEN** counts, ranking, filters, selection, cached responses, errors and exports are observed,
- **THEN** unauthorized facts do not alter observable permitted output; stale responses after Tenant/role change are rejected.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P13-08

- **GIVEN** sensitive source material is redacted, expires or is lawfully erased,
- **WHEN** history, task previews, notifications, reports, logs, exports, generated files and retained derivatives are reconciled,
- **THEN** admitted structural evidence remains only where permitted and protected bodies/identities are not restored or copied to evade source restrictions.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P13-09

- **GIVEN** an external owner returns an ambiguous timeout after possible success,
- **WHEN** recovery runs,
- **THEN** the source/provider receipt is reconciled before retry and no blind duplicate effect or false success is reported.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P23-01

- **GIVEN** The stated real principals, role grants, source scopes and current policy states, including denied or revoked cases.

- **WHEN** the specified command, read, result, cache, export or recovery door is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Real nonowner application roles and API/RPC entry points: two tenants, two entities, config-only actor, record-only actor, lead without config power, unauthorized aggregate/history reader, revoked principal and service job. Deny unauthorized old/new states, protected existence and disclosure, including after cached reads, replay and deferred service execution. Hidden-present/absent stage creation, reuse, fresh selection and unique/FK failures reveal no unauthorized detail; processing purpose and field/history/residency constraints still apply.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P23-02

- **GIVEN** The stated real principals, role grants, source scopes and current policy states, including denied or revoked cases.

- **WHEN** the specified command, read, result, cache, export or recovery door is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Unicode, RTL, long translated labels, confusing duplicate labels and empty/oversized inputs have usable safe rendering/validation without identity changes. Private content/usage cannot leak through labels, HTML, CSV/formula rendering, logs, generated documents or browser drafts; existing retention/erasure remains effective.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P23-03

- **GIVEN** The stated real principals, role grants, source scopes and current policy states, including denied or revoked cases.

- **WHEN** the specified command, read, result, cache, export or recovery door is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Exercise real nonowner application roles and API/RPC paths: cross-tenant/entity/preset targets, broad extra payload fields, scope/actor spoofing, lead/configurator without stage power and service jobs. Reject forbidden old/new states and preserve history atomically; verify actual grants/RLS/function owners/search paths.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P23-04

- **GIVEN** The stated real principals, role grants, source scopes and current policy states, including denied or revoked cases.

- **WHEN** the specified command, read, result, cache, export or recovery door is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Revoke authority during editing, deferred execution and result replay; enforce current source/purpose/field/history/residency policy. Hidden-present/absent targets, stale-editor identity and unique/FK errors reveal no unauthorized facts. Protected aliases remain protected.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P23-05

- **GIVEN** The stated real principals, role grants, source scopes and current policy states, including denied or revoked cases.

- **WHEN** the specified command, read, result, cache, export or recovery door is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Test stale caches, tenant/context switches, redaction/retention, CSV/document output, denied history and backup restore. Expose only currently permitted content; older responses cannot overwrite newer accepted revisions. No sensitive guidance/narrative remains in an unauthorized draft or shared cache.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P23-06

- **GIVEN** The stated real principals, role grants, source scopes and current policy states, including denied or revoked cases.

- **WHEN** the specified command, read, result, cache, export or recovery door is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Exercise actual authenticated, unauthenticated, limited missionary/staff, configuration-only, revoked and admitted service/operator paths. Cross-tenant/entity/preset, forged attribution and mutable-scope attempts fail; direct table/RPC/view routes cannot bypass the source. Prove effective USING/WITH CHECK and grants with real nonowner roles, plus privileged-path safety.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P23-07

- **GIVEN** The stated real principals, role grants, source scopes and current policy states, including denied or revoked cases.

- **WHEN** the specified command, read, result, cache, export or recovery door is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Hidden/absent targets and forbidden history/receipt cases reveal only permitted observations. Revoke access while previewing/submitting/replaying/exporting and restore a backup: no stale cache, actor, protected label/note or restored authority leaks. Audit retains lawful minimal attribution separately from technical logs.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P23-08

- **GIVEN** The stated real principals, role grants, source scopes and current policy states, including denied or revoked cases.

- **WHEN** the specified command, read, result, cache, export or recovery door is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Nonowner and real service/API/RPC tests cover SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/UPSERT/DELETE/RETURNING, WITH CHECK/new-state escape, extra permissive policies, field/history/count/export authority, revocation and trusted actor/owner validation. Null stage grants no capability. Hidden duplicate reuse reveals no protected record and creates no duplicate ordinary journey.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P23-09

- **GIVEN** The stated real principals, role grants, source scopes and current policy states, including denied or revoked cases.

- **WHEN** the specified command, read, result, cache, export or recovery door is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Configuration-only, journey-only, lead-only, read-only, missionary and revoked actors receive precisely their permissions. Configuration grants no usage counts/private journey history; ordinary staff do not enter settings or justify a new process to change a stage. Optional list guidance is not mandatory approval text or a journey field.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P23-10

- **GIVEN** The stated real principals, role grants, source scopes and current policy states, including denied or revoked cases.

- **WHEN** the specified command, read, result, cache, export or recovery door is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Test real nonowner and relevant service contexts across SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/UPSERT/DELETE/RETURNING, views/RPCs, grants, extra policies, tenant rebinding, config-to-journey escalation, forged attribution and revocation. Force both orders: revocation effective before acceptance prevents the effect; after acceptance it governs current disclosure without rewriting history. Prove both old-row access and allowed resulting state; a successful privileged fixture is not sufficient.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P23-11

- **GIVEN** The stated real principals, role grants, source scopes and current policy states, including denied or revoked cases.

- **WHEN** the specified command, read, result, cache, export or recovery door is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Restore before later config edits, revocation, redaction or completed effects; reconcile those fences before serving writes/reads/workers. No stale configuration, erased narrative or consumed effect is revived. Creator departure does not delete organization-owned lists, orphan stages or grant the replacement actor automatic authority. Script-like names/guidance and spreadsheet formula prefixes remain inert text through every offered display/export; no sensitive content appears in diagnostic logs.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P23-12

- **GIVEN** The stated real principals, role grants, source scopes and current policy states, including denied or revoked cases.

- **WHEN** the specified command, read, result, cache, export or recovery door is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Test two tenants, different entities/contexts, a configurator without journey visibility, lead without configuration authority, revoked assignment, expired policy and appropriate service contexts. No unauthorized operation or hidden dependency/count/result disclosure succeeds.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P23-13

- **GIVEN** The stated real principals, role grants, source scopes and current policy states, including denied or revoked cases.

- **WHEN** the specified command, read, result, cache, export or recovery door is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Test same user across tenant/assignment/source/policy changes, two users with different read/use/config powers, revoked authority and appropriate service contexts. Old options/drafts/results cannot leak or authorize another context. Config management is not required merely to use an independently authorized list.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P23-14

- **GIVEN** The stated real principals, role grants, source scopes and current policy states, including denied or revoked cases.

- **WHEN** the specified command, read, result, cache, export or recovery door is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Test real grants/RLS/RPC/views/raw access/upsert/import/jobs/repair: an allowed row update cannot mutate tenant, source list, trusted actor/owner or history into a forbidden state. Optional stage never weakens required list scope.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P23-15

- **GIVEN** The stated real principals, role grants, source scopes and current policy states, including denied or revoked cases.

- **WHEN** the specified command, read, result, cache, export or recovery door is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Names/guidance/usage counts/previews/export and audit are limited to independently authorized purpose and context. Hidden-present versus absent yields no unauthorized distinguishing result; logs omit protected descriptive content.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P23-16

- **GIVEN** The stated real principals, role grants, source scopes and current policy states, including denied or revoked cases.

- **WHEN** the specified command, read, result, cache, export or recovery door is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Compare forbidden default relationship with readable A, hidden target, absent default and permitted default. Selection, payloads, badges, errors, timing and history do not reveal protected facts. Only independently permitted default application or sole-choice evidence may prefill. Also test a person allowed to withdraw A but unable to know/clear its default relationship: success versus rejection must not reveal the hidden relationship; readiness disclosure must be independently permitted or outcomes safely indistinguishable.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P23-17

- **GIVEN** The stated real principals, role grants, source scopes and current policy states, including denied or revoked cases.

- **WHEN** the specified command, read, result, cache, export or recovery door is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Actual nonowner grants/RLS/views/RPC/INSERT/UPDATE/UPSERT/RETURNING, jobs/imports and service/repair paths preserve tenant/context, required scope, reference eligibility and trusted attribution. An allowed old row cannot become a forbidden new row.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P23-18

- **GIVEN** The stated real principals, role grants, source scopes and current policy states, including denied or revoked cases.

- **WHEN** the specified command, read, result, cache, export or recovery door is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Same-user Tenant/assignment/source/epoch changes, sign-out, stale tab and revocation cannot reuse old initializer data or authorize the new work. Test correct invalidation/partitioning independently of server write denial.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P23-19

- **GIVEN** The stated real principals, role grants, source scopes and current policy states, including denied or revoked cases.

- **WHEN** the specified command, read, result, cache, export or recovery door is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Durable config/admission evidence, security audit, technical logs, exports and authorized history are correctly attributed and minimized. Historical receipt recovery does not claim the old configuration is current; lawful redaction/restore rules remain effective.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P23-20

- **GIVEN** The stated real principals, role grants, source scopes and current policy states, including denied or revoked cases.

- **WHEN** the specified command, read, result, cache, export or recovery door is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Compare readable journey/hidden current-list metadata, permitted target/forbidden target, absent target, lost mutation power and lost result visibility. Only independently permitted effect facts are revealed; no raw constraint/name/count/history/timing oracle.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P23-21

- **GIVEN** The stated real principals, role grants, source scopes and current policy states, including denied or revoked cases.

- **WHEN** the specified command, read, result, cache, export or recovery door is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Actual nonowner INSERT/UPDATE/UPSERT/RETURNING/RPC/view paths and allowed-old/forbidden-new mutations preserve complete tenant/source/list/stage scope, actor attribution and history immutability. Test NULL stage separately from NULL required scope.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P23-22

- **GIVEN** The stated real principals, role grants, source scopes and current policy states, including denied or revoked cases.

- **WHEN** the specified command, read, result, cache, export or recovery door is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Import, API, job, privileged service and repair paths use equivalent current authority and invariants. Same-user tenant/assignment changes, stale tabs and revocation cannot reuse old cached choices or reveal old receipts.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P23-23

- **GIVEN** The stated real principals, role grants, source scopes and current policy states, including denied or revoked cases.

- **WHEN** the specified command, read, result, cache, export or recovery door is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Actual authenticated non-owner roles cannot cross tenant/source, mutate unauthorized work, forge actor/tenant/audit fields or gain access by choosing absence; test direct DML and reached privileged paths.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P23-24

- **GIVEN** The stated real principals, role grants, source scopes and current policy states, including denied or revoked cases.

- **WHEN** the specified command, read, result, cache, export or recovery door is exercised.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Permitted current/history/result labels appear correctly; restricted details remain concealed in UI, API, history, errors, counts, exports and logs. Set stage is present only where permitted.

#### Scenario: US27-P40 AC27-P23-25

- **GIVEN** a service/operator execution or a user with restricted history attempts a list change,
- **WHEN** the action and minimum effect result are authorized,
- **THEN** execution uses its actual context and only the independently permitted understandable effect is disclosed; absent minimum authority yields the uniform unavailable outcome rather than a borrowed human context or private-history leak.

### Requirement: US27-P41 — Resolve conflicting changes without overwriting newer work

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to resolve conflicting changes without overwriting newer work, so that I can retain one attributable current stage or list and trustworthy history. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P24.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-01

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Exhaustively test small bounded permutation fixtures and generate cases across declared supported sizes; test empty/partial/duplicate/unknown/foreign manifests, and simultaneous add/retire/rename/reorder. Stale expected revisions reject without partial configuration/history changes. A nonempty scope cannot be replaced by an empty filtered view.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-02

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Cross-tenant/preset references, nullable/mutable identity tricks, unauthorized direct inserts/updates/deletes, cascade attempts and privileged-path parameter spoofing fail. Actual grants, RLS policies, function owners/search paths and exposed views match the intended boundary.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-03

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Deterministically interleave selection and retirement in both commit orders, plus conflicting editor saves. A precheck-only counterexample must fail qualification. Approved protocol retains a pre-retirement accepted reference and rejects a post-retirement fresh selection; bounded deadlock/serialization-abort handling causes no lost or duplicate accepted effect.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-04

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Fail before commit, after commit before response, during projection and during replay. One intended configuration effect has one stable accepted outcome/actor/time; mismatched idempotency reuse conflicts, current authority controls readback, and cancel never undoes committed state.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-05

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Drop/duplicate/delay cache invalidation and qualified projection events; newer accepted revisions cannot be overwritten by older delivery. Current writes reject stale choices despite old UI. Scoped reconciliation restores views without exposing hidden records or interrupting unrelated tenants/work.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-06

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Deterministically interleave two enrollment edits and retirement versus selection; exercise duplicate business effects, different content under the same effect key and, where separately admitted by the transition contract, a genuine later return to the same stage. No stale overwrite, duplicate accepted event or mistaken lifetime deduplication occurs.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-07

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Given an editor at revision n/X and source changes X→Y→X at n+2, its stale write is rejected/reconciled despite matching X. Reopen retains the last authoritative stage under D9, including retired/unknown history, without a fabricated new assessment.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-08

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Interleave competing updates at one revision; at most one conflicting intent wins. Duplicate simultaneous deliveries of one intent yield one effect. Changed payload under that key fails. Inject failure after source writes but before source commit: current value, required history/result/effect intent roll back atomically.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-09

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Actual migrations/constraints accept valid no-stage scope and reject missing/foreign required scope and wrong-tenant/preset stage. Verify FK deletion actions, null/check semantics, required indexes and dedupe independent of stage. A known stage is not erased on definition retirement.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-10

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Competing stage assignments with the same expected revision produce one accepted change and a safe stale result. Retirement, revocation and closed-state changes before acceptance are checked. Same accepted intent replays without a new event; a different normalized payload under the same intent key conflicts. A genuinely new deliberate intent uses its own identity and current expected revision; a missing required revision is not treated as an unconditional update.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-11

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Inject failure before commit at each required write: no partial source/history/result/work. After commit/lost response, recover the original result separately from current state. No-stage admission creates no stage visit; later assignment uses its own accepted time and null-to-stage fact; known-stage admission creates no invented prior unassessed duration.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-12

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Actual migrations/commands reject cross-tenant and same-tenant wrong-list stages, missing required scope, mutable scope, inappropriate FK cascade/SET NULL and raw list reassignment. D13 absence still requires a valid list/source. No list identity in a dedupe key permits a second ordinary journey for the same business scope.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-13

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Two submissions of the same business intent, including simultaneous execution and lost response, create one list and one required result/history. Different normalized payload under the same key conflicts. A fresh intent does not reuse a prior list merely because names match; it obeys current name validation. Test no bypass through import/RPC.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-14

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

After creation, accept a later authorized edit, then replay the old create. Recover the original permitted receipt separately from current revision; never reset the edit, recreate deleted/redacted text or repeat side effects. If disclosure was revoked, the replay response reveals no forbidden configuration or journey information.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-15

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Controlled admission-first and withdrawal-first interleavings produce respectively retained existing work or rejected new use, never an accepted post-withdrawal attachment. Stale form, background retry, copied IDs, backdated input and future switch-back cannot bypass the rule. In one fixed reviewed group, preserve already accepted members, allow qualified current-bound reuse, reject unaccepted new members after withdrawal and reconcile uncertain receipts; review/enqueue is not admission, and retry never expands or retargets the group.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-16

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Same intent delivered concurrently yields one accepted effect/history/result. Changed payload conflicts. Retirement → restoration → retry of the old retirement does not retire again; a stale fresh command conflicts despite matching the old Boolean state.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-17

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Inject failure before commit, after commit before response and during secondary projection. No partial source/history/receipt commits; accepted source is reconciled after response loss; projections catch up without repeating business effects. UI never claims cancellation undid a commit.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-18

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Reject foreign-list stage and foreign-tenant list, nullable-stage scope escape, tenant/actor/history tampering and direct availability mutation through every exposed role/RPC/view/service path. An allowed row update cannot move it into a forbidden scope.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-19

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Concurrent last-stage edits, availability edits and dependency changes preserve valid configuration and acceptance order; deadlock/serialization recovery remains bounded without lost/duplicated business effects. Revocation before acceptance blocks; later revocation limits subsequent disclosure.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-20

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Submit A1 with list B, foreign tenant/list, absent required scope or stale stage eligibility. Reject without partial journey/history/work. The server never silently changes the submitted stage to null or B1.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-21

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Two concurrent same-purpose starts with different selected lists obey D3/D4's qualified collision/reuse policy; list ID does not create another ordinary identity. Admission/withdrawal and permission-change races follow actual acceptance order.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-22

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Exact submitted-intent retry produces one durable effect/result; changed list/stage/payload under the same bound submitted intent conflicts. Lost response is reconciled before a changed selection is offered as new work; confirmed nonacceptance permits only the qualified revised-intent path. No stale callback regresses current context.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-23

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Target withdrawal and switch execute in both controlled orders: acceptance first yields retained attached work; withdrawal first rejects new attachment. A pending form/job or historical target use cannot create an exception.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-24

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Relevant target-stage retirement or permission/lifecycle/source change before acceptance is handled by actual current policy, without silent substitution. Cosmetic rename/order or unrelated catalog change alone does not invalidate an otherwise valid proposal.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-25

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Concurrent stage update versus list switch in both orders preserves the winning accepted result and rejects/resolves stale intent; neither can leave a mismatched pair or lose a recorded assessment.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-26

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Two switches, opposing A↔B changes and stale A→B→A views preserve serial source meaning without lost updates or indefinite waits. An old matching value does not bypass relevant revision checks; failures are visible and retryable by the qualified protocol.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-27

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Failure before required history/result or required effect intent commits leaves no partial current change. Independent readers see an old or new coherent committed binding/history/result. No caller observes an authoritative intermediate pair.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-28

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Same-list no-op, exact accepted retry, failed-before-acceptance retry and same-key changed payload have distinct truthful outcomes. No duplicate history, stage assessment, enrollment or follow-up is created.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-29

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

After A→B and later B→C, retry the old A→B command: return only the permitted historical result without changing C. UI does not overwrite current C with the old receipt. A desired return to B is a fresh eligible command.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-30

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Inject failure between binding/history/result writes: no partial accepted result. An independent observer sees coherent committed states only.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-31

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Lost response plus exact retry yields one accepted history/result; changed absence/stage payload fails. Old receipt after later work reports earlier acceptance without overwriting current work.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-32

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Concurrent stage edit/list change and competing list changes preserve one valid sequence or visible conflict; prove relevant orders on actual source boundary.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-33

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Revisions protect ABA and stale same-list transformations; repeat delivery cannot cause a second business effect even through another transport request.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-34

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Target withdrawal/stage retirement or permission change between preparation and acceptance cannot yield an invalid result or silent no-stage fallback.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-35

- **GIVEN** The specified valid and adversarial source states, revisions and durable intents.

- **WHEN** the actual mutation protocol runs through the stated races, retries or injected failures.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

With null stage, invalid/missing/cross-scope list references still fail; with present stage, wrong-list/stale reference fails. Deletion cannot orphan required current/history relationships.

#### Scenario: US27-P41 AC27-P24-36

- **GIVEN** schema versions, timestamp ties, partial-null optional-stage references and mixed effective policies are present,
- **WHEN** real source commands and database roles exercise stale, forged, partial-null and same-key changed-meaning mutations,
- **THEN** actual concurrency revisions and scoped normalized intent govern ordering/replay, legitimate absence remains valid with required scope enforced, and every forbidden resulting state is rejected through qualified grants/policies/commands rather than cross-row checks or timestamp guesses.

### Requirement: US27-P42 — Operate stage and partnership controls with readable status and focus

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a staff member using a keyboard, screen reader or touch, I want to operate stage and partnership controls with readable status and focus, so that I can complete ordinary work independently across devices and languages. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P25, REQ27-P30.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-01

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

For the same applicable preset/revision, staff/missionary consumers resolve consistent source meaning under permitted projections. Stage-ordered views follow that order, boards include empty columns, and existing retired-stage records remain accessible as permitted; ordinary list/report sorts remain valid. Different presets need not have equivalent lists. Saved historical filters and separately qualified retired-stage history imports/corrections work. A stale fresh-selection import/API/qualified workflow target gets an appropriately disclosed failure, not remap/auto-creation. Reports never infer cross-preset equivalence or progress from current rank alone.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-02

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

A representative missions staff member can customize a preset, cancel edits and understand retirement without developer help. Keyboard, screen reader and tap/click users can reorder, apply and recover errors; focus/status announcements, 320 CSS px layout, zoom, Core touch targets and slow network are exercised. No additional donor-facing step appears.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-03

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

From an authorized partnership context, identify current stage, understand the proposed stage's meaning, choose and save through one compact interaction without leaving for settings. Browsing/help/cancel has no source effect; one deliberate commit is sufficient. Missing optional guidance is not a validation error.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-04

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Disconnect/fail before commit, after commit before response and during downstream projection. UI distinguishes known rejection from unconfirmed outcome; recovery finds the authorized accepted result. Late errors cannot roll back newer data. Closing/canceling after submit never claims to undo a committed update.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-05

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Long Unicode/RTL/translated labels and international names remain distinguishable under 200% text resizing and reflow at an effective 320 CSS px width (including a 1280 CSS px viewport at 400% browser zoom). Relevant controls stay reachable with soft keyboard and slow connection. Locale/timezone changes alter presentation, not identity or D6 date-only meaning.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-06

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Complete normal, cancellation, failure and conflict flows using keyboard, screen reader and tap/click; verify names, described help/errors, focus and restoration, material status announcements, light/dark contrast, Core touch targets and reduced motion. Drag is optional; automated axe checks supplement manual outcomes.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-07

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Lose the accepted response, close/reopen the editor, deliver old responses after newer changes and interrupt secondary refresh. UI distinguishes unconfirmed, historical accepted and current state; reconciliation reuses intent and never regresses the journey or another list row. A saved change that removes an item from the current filter remains understandable, with the known permitted reason, an authorized way to locate it and useful focus. Post-commit failure never undoes source acceptance.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-08

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Through the actual browser, complete and cancel from every offered entry point with keyboard, screen reader, touch and a non-drag single-pointer path. Browsing/Escape before submission does not save. Focus, names, help, pending/error/conflict and success announcements are correct. No color/toast/hover-only essential information or edit affordance implying authority on a read-only journey.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-09

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Current retired value, no choice, no matches, long/duplicate/localized labels, changed guidance and failed loading remain distinguishable. No accidental blank/default value or fabricated new stage. Participants identify current vs proposed vs saved and understand that moving back keeps history.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-10

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Verify exact shared design-system composition, contrast, Core touch targets, text resize, reflow, RTL and mobile soft keyboard. On a declared low-bandwidth/high-latency/disconnection profile, no false saved state, duplicate effect or inaccessible recovery. Do not claim offline save support without separate qualification.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-11

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Cancel, slow connection, offline failure, denied capability, expired access, stale revision and uncertain response retain truthful permitted state/intent. Late callbacks cannot restore null over an accepted stage. Staff can identify whether to retry/reconcile or choose again.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-12

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Exercise both start paths and later Set/Change stage with keyboard, screen reader and touch, 200% text resize, 320 CSS px reflow, long translated names, RTL, zoom, reduced motion and mobile keyboard. All actions, full distinctions and recovery remain accessible; no color/drag-only interaction. Automated checks supplement manual tasks, not replace them.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-13

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Exercise slow save, duplicate click, offline failure, timeout before commit, lost response after commit, stale index/cache, delayed callback and permission loss. Pending/accepted/unconfirmed/conflict are distinguishable; draft and next action remain usable where permitted. Operators can reconcile through the qualified source rather than routine direct SQL edits.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-14

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Test pagination, list/stage filters, combined views and offered exports with multiple lists, equal labels, retired stages, unassessed records and denied/partial coverage. Authorized population totals reconcile; equal names/ranks never manufacture progress/forecast equivalence or leak hidden usage. Configuration history is not counted as stage movement.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-15

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

In actual browsers, exercise create/cancel/edit, daily Set/Change stage and recovery with keyboard, screen reader, touch and non-drag controls; test 200% text resize, 320 CSS px effective reflow, RTL, long translations, reduced motion and soft keyboards. Representative tenant configurators/staff and appropriately authorized missionaries identify a list's purpose, configuration versus partnership changes, saved outcome and next action without moderator rescue for a critical task. Record observed failures and device/ assistive-tech versions; any reproducible critical confusion blocks expansion.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-16

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Equal labels across lists retain distinct identities and provenance; matching labels do not establish equivalence. Counts retain withdrawn-list work in declared scope and report incomplete reads honestly. Cache/export/history tests apply current authority and redaction; sensitive text is absent from operational logs.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-17

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Keyboard, screen reader, pointer/touch, narrow viewport, 200% text, 320 CSS-pixel reflow, long names/RTL, reduced motion and low-bandwidth states preserve understandable options, focus, errors and next actions. Automated accessibility scans complement manual checks.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-18

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Genuine authorized zero, search-no-match, loading, denied and network/source failure have distinguishable permitted UI outcomes. User sees the appropriate retry/search/settings/help path; none claims global absence or reveals hidden choices.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-19

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Late failed optimistic request, out-of-order search/detail responses and token/context refresh cannot restore a prior context's catalog or overwrite newer accepted work. Current source and disclosure remain authoritative.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-20

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Real browser keyboard/screen-reader/touch and narrow layouts preserve full distinguishable names, labels, focus and next actions. Check 200% text, 320 CSS-pixel reflow, RTL, long text, soft keyboard, reduced motion and slow-network recovery; complement automated accessibility with manual checks.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-21

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Options failure does not erase independently established list/stage truth or become Not yet assessed. On uncertain admission, pending/unconfirmed/accepted are truthful, input remains protected, and closing the view does not claim to undo a commit.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-22

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Late options/default/detail replies, optimistic rollback and pending admission do not overwrite newer context or actual accepted work. Loading, zero, denied, search-no-match, failed and unconfirmed outcomes remain distinguishable with real permitted recovery.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-23

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Keyboard/screen-reader/touch, 320 CSS-pixel reflow, 200% text, RTL, long labels, reduced motion and slow mobile interaction expose selected list, optional stage, alternatives and next action. Exercise the qualified deliberate same-work list-change flow for saved-choice correction, preserving identity/history without deletion/recreation and applying current destination eligibility and stage-or-absence semantics.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-24

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

A corrective change made now preserves earlier actual recorded choices and intervening periods. No ordinary user input backdates acceptance or rewrites accepted history. A return is not an undo of independent tasks/finance/consent.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-25

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Pending, rejection, accepted-but-refresh-failed and lost-response states preserve exact submitted intent and distinguish unconfirmed from unsaved. Closing/reopening the UI cannot accidentally repeat the effect. If successful switching removes the row from the current filter, explain its movement and preserve a permitted route to the saved work.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-26

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Options/default/refetch/context changes and late responses cannot replace the submitted target or restore an obsolete source state. Safe unrelated draft input survives rejection; Cancel before submission makes no source mutation.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-27

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Current grouping and historical reporting retain distinct meanings across rename, switch and return. No new-donor count, stage progression, forecast probability or old time-in-stage result is fabricated from list rank or current labels. A no-stage list change is not a new assessment; any later assessment records its actual acceptance time and does not credit the no-stage interval to that stage.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-28

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Keyboard, screen reader, touch, 320 CSS-pixel reflow, 200% text, RTL, long labels and slow mobile conditions expose current/proposed state, actual stage result, save/cancel and durable recovery. Essential errors are not toast-only; selection does not persist immediately.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-29

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

History identifies actual list change and stage-or-absence; no false assessment/contact/progress time; close/reopen preserves current absence rather than restoring historical non-null state. Later stage assignment preserves the unassessed interval and records real accepted time.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-30

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Keyboard and screen reader can choose stage or explicit absence, understand current/proposed states, save/cancel and recover from pending/conflict/lost response without hidden changes or focus loss.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-31

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Real reached controls/adapters preserve explicit absence and stage choices through rerenders/refetch, target changes and server result; verify installed dependency versions, not only latest docs.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P25-32

- **GIVEN** a stage flow is operated on touch and its duration view includes a skip, return, current stay, completed stay and unknown interval,
- **WHEN** controls and reporting definitions are inspected and exercised,
- **THEN** Core 44 CSS-pixel controls remain understandable without ordinary diagnostic IDs, while each duration declares its clock/population/stay meaning and preserves gaps, returns and unknown coverage.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P30-01

- **GIVEN** ordinary start, pause, close, reopen and stage/list flows contain all admitted context,
- **WHEN** representative staff and missionaries complete relevant tasks without coaching,
- **THEN** they identify the supporter, ministry, responsibility and next action and distinguish internal intent from supporter agreement.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P30-02

- **GIVEN** the same context uses long Thai, Arabic, Latin, combining-character names, RTL and restricted aliases,
- **WHEN** the flow is rendered in supported locales and mobile widths,
- **THEN** full safe context is understandable without Western splitting, US-address assumptions, clipped critical labels or legal-name/location fallback.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P30-03

- **GIVEN** the flow contains dragging, dialogs, removed rows, validation and status changes,
- **WHEN** keyboard-only, screen-reader and single-pointer users complete it,
- **THEN** actions have working alternatives, focus is visible/returned correctly, errors are associated and status changes are announced.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P30-04

- **GIVEN** the user operates at 320 CSS pixels, 400% zoom, light/dark modes and mobile keyboard conditions,
- **WHEN** each ordinary action is completed,
- **THEN** meaning, required controls and safe source links remain usable with measured contrast and target compliance.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P30-05

- **GIVEN** a save response is delayed, dropped or rejected after permitted input is entered,
- **WHEN** the user navigates, retries or returns,
- **THEN** the UI preserves allowed drafts and recovers the same intent while distinguishing accepted, pending, rejected and unconfirmed outcomes.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P30-06

- **GIVEN** current permissions or active Tenant change while old requests are pending,
- **WHEN** late responses arrive,
- **THEN** denied draft content is removed and old-context responses cannot overwrite the current context or claim a forbidden success.

#### Scenario: US27-P42 AC27-P30-07

- **GIVEN** a confirmed source mutation removes a row from the current list,
- **WHEN** the result appears,
- **THEN** focus moves predictably, the durable result and next action remain understandable beyond a toast, and no unsafe generic Undo erases accepted or reused work.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Current retired value, no choice, no matches, long/duplicate/localized labels, changed guidance and failed loading remain distinguishable. No accidental blank/default value or fabricated new stage. Participants identify current vs proposed vs saved and understand that moving back keeps history.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P25-10

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Verify exact shared design-system composition, contrast, Core touch targets, text resize, reflow, RTL and mobile soft keyboard. On a declared low-bandwidth/high-latency/disconnection profile, no false saved state, duplicate effect or inaccessible recovery. Do not claim offline save support without separate qualification.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P25-11

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Cancel, slow connection, offline failure, denied capability, expired access, stale revision and uncertain response retain truthful permitted state/intent. Late callbacks cannot restore null over an accepted stage. Staff can identify whether to retry/reconcile or choose again.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P25-12

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Exercise both start paths and later Set/Change stage with keyboard, screen reader and touch, 200% text resize, 320 CSS px reflow, long translated names, RTL, zoom, reduced motion and mobile keyboard. All actions, full distinctions and recovery remain accessible; no color/drag-only interaction. Automated checks supplement manual tasks, not replace them.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P25-13

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Exercise slow save, duplicate click, offline failure, timeout before commit, lost response after commit, stale index/cache, delayed callback and permission loss. Pending/accepted/unconfirmed/conflict are distinguishable; draft and next action remain usable where permitted. Operators can reconcile through the qualified source rather than routine direct SQL edits.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P25-14

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Test pagination, list/stage filters, combined views and offered exports with multiple lists, equal labels, retired stages, unassessed records and denied/partial coverage. Authorized population totals reconcile; equal names/ranks never manufacture progress/forecast equivalence or leak hidden usage. Configuration history is not counted as stage movement.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P25-15

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

In actual browsers, exercise create/cancel/edit, daily Set/Change stage and recovery with keyboard, screen reader, touch and non-drag controls; test 200% text resize, 320 CSS px effective reflow, RTL, long translations, reduced motion and soft keyboards. Representative tenant configurators/staff and appropriately authorized missionaries identify a list's purpose, configuration versus partnership changes, saved outcome and next action without moderator rescue for a critical task. Record observed failures and device/ assistive-tech versions; any reproducible critical confusion blocks expansion.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P25-16

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Equal labels across lists retain distinct identities and provenance; matching labels do not establish equivalence. Counts retain withdrawn-list work in declared scope and report incomplete reads honestly. Cache/export/history tests apply current authority and redaction; sensitive text is absent from operational logs.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P25-17

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Keyboard, screen reader, pointer/touch, narrow viewport, 200% text, 320 CSS-pixel reflow, long names/RTL, reduced motion and low-bandwidth states preserve understandable options, focus, errors and next actions. Automated accessibility scans complement manual checks.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P25-18

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Genuine authorized zero, search-no-match, loading, denied and network/source failure have distinguishable permitted UI outcomes. User sees the appropriate retry/search/settings/help path; none claims global absence or reveals hidden choices.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P25-19

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Late failed optimistic request, out-of-order search/detail responses and token/context refresh cannot restore a prior context's catalog or overwrite newer accepted work. Current source and disclosure remain authoritative.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P25-20

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Real browser keyboard/screen-reader/touch and narrow layouts preserve full distinguishable names, labels, focus and next actions. Check 200% text, 320 CSS-pixel reflow, RTL, long text, soft keyboard, reduced motion and slow-network recovery; complement automated accessibility with manual checks.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P25-21

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Options failure does not erase independently established list/stage truth or become Not yet assessed. On uncertain admission, pending/unconfirmed/accepted are truthful, input remains protected, and closing the view does not claim to undo a commit.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P25-22

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.
