- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Late options/default/detail replies, optimistic rollback and pending admission do not overwrite newer context or actual accepted work. Loading, zero, denied, search-no-match, failed and unconfirmed outcomes remain distinguishable with real permitted recovery.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P25-23

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Keyboard/screen-reader/touch, 320 CSS-pixel reflow, 200% text, RTL, long labels, reduced motion and slow mobile interaction expose selected list, optional stage, alternatives and next action. Exercise the qualified deliberate same-work list-change flow for saved-choice correction, preserving identity/history without deletion/recreation and applying current destination eligibility and stage-or-absence semantics.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P25-24

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

A corrective change made now preserves earlier actual recorded choices and intervening periods. No ordinary user input backdates acceptance or rewrites accepted history. A return is not an undo of independent tasks/finance/consent.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P25-25

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Pending, rejection, accepted-but-refresh-failed and lost-response states preserve exact submitted intent and distinguish unconfirmed from unsaved. Closing/reopening the UI cannot accidentally repeat the effect. If successful switching removes the row from the current filter, explain its movement and preserve a permitted route to the saved work.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P25-26

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Options/default/refetch/context changes and late responses cannot replace the submitted target or restore an obsolete source state. Safe unrelated draft input survives rejection; Cancel before submission makes no source mutation.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P25-27

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Current grouping and historical reporting retain distinct meanings across rename, switch and return. No new-donor count, stage progression, forecast probability or old time-in-stage result is fabricated from list rank or current labels. A no-stage list change is not a new assessment; any later assessment records its actual acceptance time and does not credit the no-stage interval to that stage.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P25-28

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Keyboard, screen reader, touch, 320 CSS-pixel reflow, 200% text, RTL, long labels and slow mobile conditions expose current/proposed state, actual stage result, save/cancel and durable recovery. Essential errors are not toast-only; selection does not persist immediately.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P25-29

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

History identifies actual list change and stage-or-absence; no false assessment/contact/progress time; close/reopen preserves current absence rather than restoring historical non-null state. Later stage assignment preserves the unassessed interval and records real accepted time.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P25-30

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Keyboard and screen reader can choose stage or explicit absence, understand current/proposed states, save/cancel and recover from pending/conflict/lost response without hidden changes or focus loss.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P25-31

- **GIVEN** The stated source facts, interface state, access context, device and connection conditions.

- **WHEN** the actual offered interaction, history read or recovery path is completed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Real reached controls/adapters preserve explicit absence and stage choices through rerenders/refetch, target changes and server result; verify installed dependency versions, not only latest docs.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P25-32

- **GIVEN** a stage flow is operated on touch and its duration view includes a skip, return, current stay, completed stay and unknown interval,
- **WHEN** controls and reporting definitions are inspected and exercised,
- **THEN** Core 44 CSS-pixel controls remain understandable without ordinary diagnostic IDs, while each duration declares its clock/population/stay meaning and preserves gaps, returns and unknown coverage.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P31-01

- **GIVEN** the latest qualifying source fact or relevant obligation lies beyond all current display caps,
- **WHEN** the complete projection is computed,
- **THEN** the admitted total or latest fact includes that evidence or explicitly reports incomplete coverage, never a falsely complete capped result.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P31-02

- **GIVEN** finance source coverage is absent or amounts use different currencies/periods and recognition meanings,
- **WHEN** a cultivation summary is displayed,
- **THEN** unknown is not zero and incomparable values are not summed, converted or represented as received cash.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P31-03

- **GIVEN** one source gift or encounter appears in multiple allowed views,
- **WHEN** organization totals and source identity are reconciled,
- **THEN** the source fact is counted once for its admitted aggregate meaning and remains a reference rather than another financial or encounter record.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P31-04

- **GIVEN** the newest source fact is corrected, restricted, merged or erased,
- **WHEN** derived views rebuild,
- **THEN** the result may move backward or become unknown; stale cached maxima and forbidden derivatives do not remain authoritative.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P31-05

- **GIVEN** a protected fact differs between otherwise equivalent fixtures,
- **WHEN** authorized summaries, ranks, filters and export contents are compared,
- **THEN** the restricted fact does not change admitted output unless an independently qualified aggregate disclosure exists.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P31-06

- **GIVEN** staff change a ministry target, stage, lifecycle or relationship purpose,
- **WHEN** source owners are reconciled,
- **THEN** gifts, commitments, Designations, consent, recurring support and publishing authority do not change by implication.

#### Scenario: US27-P43 AC27-P31-07

- **GIVEN** a reader fails or a claimed-complete projection disagrees with source evidence,
- **WHEN** the authorized user opens the affected view,
- **THEN** it exposes incomplete/unavailable or pending reconciliation truth and a permitted owner resolution route instead of false zero or healthy status.

### Requirement: US27-P44 — Recover a failed or uncertain stage/list save

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to recover a failed or uncertain stage/list save, so that I can retain permitted intent without false success or duplicate changes. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P26.

#### Scenario: US27-P44 AC27-P26-01

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Add/select/retire/reorder stages with lifecycle-, finance-, consent- and task-like names. No money, pledge, participation, consent, closure, task or donor-message effect occurs merely from those labels/configuration. Existing D6 follow-up and D7/D8 lifecycle remain independently correct; Studio may be off.

#### Scenario: US27-P44 AC27-P26-02

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

A real stage change records actual actor/time/from/to provenance without requiring a narrative. It changes no contact/giving/consent/closure fact. Preserve valid existing work and expose genuine missing accountability; no duplicate task or false completion appears. Optional context is not copied into unrestricted logs. Where an existing qualified flow submits text with a stage, inject partial success/response loss: report each actual accepted outcome without discarding or duplicating text or certifying an unsaved note.

#### Scenario: US27-P44 AC27-P26-03

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Native updates work with Studio and unrelated providers unavailable. DON-14 cannot gate or reverse the accepted stage; duplicate/out-of-order downstream delivery preserves one source effect and current authority. Financial/prayer-like stage labels create no financial or participation evidence.

#### Scenario: US27-P44 AC27-P26-04

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Test the same authorized source meaning on staff and missionary projections, with native operation when optional Studio/providers are unavailable. Stage change does not resume/close work, alter assignments, waive contact suppression, certify participation or become a financial transaction. Initial stage/preset/correction/bulk capabilities are not smuggled in.

#### Scenario: US27-P44 AC27-P26-05

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Preserve applicable existing next work without duplicate chores; expose the owned actionable exception when appropriate. A repeat stage does not revive ended/consumed work. Deliver duplicate/out-of-order native/Studio events and ambiguous provider outcomes: current source applicability and one purpose identity control recovery.

#### Scenario: US27-P44 AC27-P26-06

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Unassessed active work has appropriate existing dated work/owned exception; missing accountability is still actionable. Absence alone creates zero new tasks/reminders/contact attempts and changes no giving, consent, participation, pause/close or completed-work fact. Optional Studio unavailable does not prevent qualified native operation.

#### Scenario: US27-P44 AC27-P26-07

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Accepted enrollment without stage is a real enrollment event, never a fake stage transition. Duplicate/delayed/out-of-order dispatch and projection failure preserve source truth and consumed-effect fences; optional systems do not block it. Authorized operators can diagnose/reconcile via the source without private narrative in logs or routine direct SQL repair.

#### Scenario: US27-P44 AC27-P26-08

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Compare accepted source identities, list references, stages, history, responsibilities, asks, work, consent and financial facts before/after creating another list. Active, paused, closed/reopened and unassessed examples remain unchanged. Creation is not a stage transition, acquisition, admission, donor response or instruction to send a message.

#### Scenario: US27-P44 AC27-P26-09

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Create/configure using real native sources with optional Studio/external providers unavailable. No new DON-14 binding or effect is inferred. Duplicate/out-of-order adapter events preserve source ownership, scoped intent and meaningful reconciliation; incompatible imports neither auto-create catalogs nor adopt another provider's labels as identity.

#### Scenario: US27-P44 AC27-P26-10

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Configuration retirement/restoration triggers no DON-14 enrollment/transition action, donor delivery, prayer/giving change or retirement-generated migration task. Authorized audit shows actor, list, before/after state, revision and business intent; unauthorized readers gain nothing.

#### Scenario: US27-P44 AC27-P26-11

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Exploratory choices create no business audit event, stage transition, task, DON-14 admission or outreach. Actual deliberate admission records its correct list and attribution and produces only its owner-qualified required effects once.

#### Scenario: US27-P44 AC27-P26-12

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

No prefill/default-change event creates a journey, stage assessment, task, outreach, consent or finance/participation fact. Actual admitted work records its concrete list and trusted actor and produces only the required owner-qualified effects once.

#### Scenario: US27-P44 AC27-P26-13

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

A switch emits no false enrollment/contact/consent/financial event and does not cancel or regenerate unrelated tasks/asks. Any genuinely required owner effect uses one durable purpose identity through native/optional consumers and retries.

#### Scenario: US27-P44 AC27-P26-14

- **GIVEN** The stated stage/list operation and independently owned work, financial, communication and participation facts.

- **WHEN** acceptance, projection, optional integration or retry occurs.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

List-change absence preserves owner/next work/review date/consent/financial facts and produces no independent task/contact/enrollment/activation. Qualified required effects survive retries/outages without duplication.

#### Scenario: US27-P44 AC27-P26-15

- **GIVEN** source state required for a mutation is unavailable or indeterminate,
- **WHEN** the dependent action is submitted,
- **THEN** the mutation does not proceed using invented absence; current permitted input and a truthful recovery route remain.

### Requirement: US27-P45 — Activate and restore source-backed work safely

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized operations staff member, I want to activate and restore source-backed work safely, so that I can preserve history, restrictions and accepted effects through upgrades. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P27, REQ27-P32.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-01

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Rehearse actual retained-data mapping/backfill, ambiguous names, interrupted retries, unchanged/customized template adoption, old/new reader-writer combinations, tenant gating, config-write kill switch, backup restore from before retirement/revocation/redaction/accepted effects, and compatible roll-forward after new IDs are referenced. Preserve tenant choices/history/current authority.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-02

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Publish and exercise numeric catalog/input/history/tenant/concurrency profiles, relevant query plans, p95 latency and lock-wait budgets. Largest supported shapes pass without unbounded journey scans; over-limit operations fail safely with understandable remediation. Measured results, not prototype tests, qualify performance.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-03

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Inventory retained data/clients, rehearse additive reader/writer compatibility, map actual legacy status meanings explicitly, and test slice activation, write kill switch, safe reads and forward repair. Restored snapshots cannot replace later authoritative stage/history or revive retired choices, revoked access, redacted content or consumed effects. No migration is inferred solely from a demo enum.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-04

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Representative missions staff, including less experienced staff and relevant authorized support-raising users, can explain the selected stage, its limits, what remains unknown and whether the change saved. Include prayer-only partnership, church/multiple-ministry context and incomplete handoff history. Resolve every observed critical task failure before general availability; record findings and retests without inventing a success rate or preference claim.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-05

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Declare finite supported catalog/text/history/tenant profiles, query plans, request/lock budgets and throttled-network test conditions; measure them on the actual composed flow. Guidance opening does not scan all journeys or full financial/contact history. Over-limit and timeout outcomes remain safe and understandable.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-06

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Dry-run retained-data mapping and mixed-version/rollback/restore with repeated stages, retired identities, unknown historical dates, current revocation/redaction and consumed effects. No fictional intermediate history, unsafe old writer, duplicate outreach or dropped valid history; reconcile discrepancies before cohort activation.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-07

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Publish tested numeric workload/query/latency/concurrency limits and execute production-shaped queries under real authorization. A representative staff/missionary usability pilot can identify the correct partnership, change directly, cancel, interpret return history and recover conflict without coaching or unsafe workaround. Critical failures block expansion.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-08

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Qualify all supported old/new client/API/ schema combinations before activation. Census/map legacy blanks and known stages without invented assessments. Reject unknown selected IDs with clear permitted repair. Imports do not automatically admit work or trigger retrospective contact. Disabled feature/rollback preserves new stage-less rows/history without guessed fills.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-09

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Restore a snapshot before assessment/revocation/ redaction; reconcile source revisions/current disclosure/effect fences before activating. No stale null overwrites current truth, erased text returns, or task/ outreach replays. Pause/close/reopen preserve D6–D9 and truthful stage provenance.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-10

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Declare workload dimensions and agreed release budgets before testing production-shaped data; retain query plans and p50/p95 timings with units. Representative tenant staff and appropriately authorized missionaries must demonstrate both start choices, who acts next, later stage change and recovery without moderator rescue for a critical action. Record actual devices, participant context, failures and changes; any reproduced critical confusion blocks expansion. No invented sample-size, success-rate or “beautiful UI” claim substitutes for evidence. Phase-wide full journey pilot remains required.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-11

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Trace each accepted effect to actual source, trusted actor, time, operation/intent and permitted result; independently inspect current-history access, security audit and minimal technical telemetry. Reconcile D13 terminology/IDs through glossary and subsequent authorized formal artifacts. Retention/export/restore tests honor qualified minimization and erasure.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-12

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Census retained data and qualify supported old/new code/schema combinations. Preserve proven source identities and uncertainty; no silent same-name merging, per-surface splits, customized starter overwrite or automatic assessment/outreach. Gate incompatible paths; disable/roll forward without dropping accepted additional lists or rewriting history.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-13

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Declare representative list/stage/ history counts, tenant skew, payload bytes/characters, concurrent users and approved budgets before workload tests. Retain plans, p50/p95 milliseconds and boundary/ one-over results; no silent truncation or incomplete totals. Trace each D14 R/AC through later authorized spec/design/tasks/issues, real source tests, audit and release evidence. Avoid private names/guidance in routine telemetry.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-14

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Rehearse compatible mixed-version rollout, retained-data census/backfill, disabled mutations, failed migration, restore and roll-forward with real retained fixtures. Old readers cannot hide work; old writers cannot ignore withdrawal; existing accepted history/receipts survive.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-15

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Measure plans, query counts, milliseconds, lock waits and payload sizes at owner-declared workload/budget boundaries and one-over cases, including skew and long-lived withdrawn lists. Withdrawal cost must not grow with a mass journey rewrite. Capacity is qualified before release.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-16

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

With representative agency configurators and working staff/missionaries, test stop-new-use, continue existing work, first assessment, reverse a mistake and resolve no new-work choices. Each participant must correctly explain whether partnerships/tasks ended and identify the next action. Any wrong end/contact/access interpretation is a design blocker to fix and retest; record observed results, not invented feedback.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-17

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Before activation, exercise evidenced retained data, mixed readers/writers, failed rollout, kill switch and restore; accepted bindings/history/receipts survive. Exercise deliberate correction of a mistaken saved list choice through the qualified same-work list-change command, preserving journey identity and history without deletion/recreation. Measure authorized complete/paged catalog and details at declared workload boundaries and one-over cases, with plans, query counts, payload units, latency and concurrency.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-18

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Representative staff/missionaries/configurators can explain what the list controls, compare overlapping purposes, select appropriately, revise an unsaved choice, find existing work and recover from zero/unconfirmed states. Any observed belief that choice grants access, changes giving or creates another partnership is a release blocker for that flow; record findings and retest fixes.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-19

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Test evidenced old data, mixed readers/writers, failed rollout, containment and restore. No guessed defaults or changed journey binding. Measure initializer/option/detail plans, query counts, payload bytes, latency in milliseconds and concurrency at declared actual workloads/boundaries; no per-option journey/history scan.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-20

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Representative agency users can explain what the prefilled list means, inspect alternatives, change it, start without forced stage assessment and recover from unavailable/uncertain states. Observed belief that it guarantees suitability, changes giving/access or removes choice blocks that flow's release until corrected and retested. Record actual findings rather than invent a pass rate.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-21

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Old/new readers and writers, interrupted rollout, restore, containment and forward repair preserve accepted identity/history/receipts and current permissions. Logs/exports/backups obey protected-context and retention rules; no guessed backfill or stale-command revival.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-22

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Representative missions staff can explain what changes and what stays, inspect alternatives, make/correct a choice and recover from conflict/uncertain save. Believing it starts another partnership, erases history or guarantees a suitable assessment blocks that flow's release until corrected and retested. Record actual observations, not an invented pass rate.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-23

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

At declared production-shaped tenant sizes/history skew and concurrency, record query plans, rows examined, query counts, payload bytes, latency units and contention/aborted retries. No per-option history scan, long human-held transaction, duplicate per-app mutation or optional vendor requirement.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-24

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Retained-data census/backfill can distinguish valid absence from unknown; no prior assessment or historical gap is fabricated.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-25

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Full snapshots, partial imports/events, export/restore and mixed-version clients preserve absent-versus-omitted semantics; disabled writes/rollback retain new no-stage data and lawful recovery.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-26

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Representative staff/missionary pilot can explain current absence versus prior history, change a list without guessing, find Set stage, and recover from conflict on mobile/low bandwidth; measured layout and query budgets pass.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-27

- **GIVEN** The stated retained data, supported versions, real reached paths and declared release cohort/workload.

- **WHEN** migration, qualification, rollout, restore or representative user tasks are executed.

- **THEN** every stated observable result and invariant below SHALL hold, with the specified fixtures and boundary cases included in verification:

Decision, glossary, canonical requirements/design/tasks, authorized tickets, real tests and cohort release evidence agree on optionality, authority, history and supported lifecycle. No research artifact is presented as shipped proof.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P27-28

- **GIVEN** history timestamps tie, several Tenants create catalogs concurrently and a backup predates expiry,
- **WHEN** pagination, measured access plans, concurrent creation and restoration are exercised,
- **THEN** history neither skips nor duplicates tied records, referencing access paths are qualified without global Tenant serialization, and expired presentations/content do not revive after restore.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P32-01

- **GIVEN** optional Studio/public/finance products are absent but required owner seams are qualified,
- **WHEN** native identified-ministry work is exercised,
- **THEN** ordinary work remains usable and no profile/seed/parallel-system fallback substitutes for a missing required source.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P32-02

- **GIVEN** retained rows include ambiguous one-profile-to-many-ministry mappings and unknown historical purpose/time,
- **WHEN** migration runs repeatedly and is interrupted/resumed,
- **THEN** only evidenced mappings are retained, unresolved associations stay explicit and no newly approved enrollment/stage/task/contact/message effect is fabricated.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P32-03

- **GIVEN** old and new application/worker/schema combinations are deployed,
- **WHEN** supported combinations execute and unsupported writers are fenced,
- **THEN** no incompatible command can bypass new source invariants or misinterpret new state; accepted evidence remains reconciled.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P32-04

- **GIVEN** new work has committed before admission/effect disable or rollback,
- **WHEN** containment and recovery execute,
- **THEN** accepted work/history/receipts remain readable under authority and repairable while new unsafe effects stop; rollback cannot reopen an old bypass.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P32-05

- **GIVEN** a restored snapshot predates revocation, erasure, schedule change, completed review or consumed delivery,
- **WHEN** reads and workers resume,
- **THEN** current owner dispositions are reconciled first; forbidden content, revoked recipients, completed work and consumed effects cannot revive.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P32-06

- **GIVEN** backfill or a recipient/surface profile changes from Reserved to Live,
- **WHEN** activation runs,
- **THEN** no historical attention is emitted; any eligible legacy-work reminder proves a distinct prospective qualified source transition.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P32-07

- **GIVEN** the declared largest Tenant, many small Tenants and concurrent interactive/batch work run together,
- **WHEN** query plans, source scans and bounded recovery are measured,
- **THEN** approved numeric budgets and honest coverage are met and one overloaded Tenant cannot starve the others.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P32-08

- **GIVEN** an eligible due review lacks required local attention,
- **WHEN** the delay passes 15 minutes from the later eligible due/schedule-recipient boundary,
- **THEN** the recorded platform-operations signal fires without retry-resetting age, substitute email or marking review complete.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P32-09

- **GIVEN** a local materialization reaches typed terminal failure or a qualified responsibility resolver is zero/indeterminate,
- **WHEN** operating recovery is evaluated,
- **THEN** the named owner receives an authorized source repair route and the original identity is reconciled without broadening access or routine SQL edits.

#### Scenario: US27-P45 AC27-P32-10

- **GIVEN** release evidence is assembled,
- **WHEN** requirements and source traces are checked,
- **THEN** positive/negative/boundary/concurrency/migration/accessibility outcomes have appropriate real evidence; unrun, mocked, demo and structural checks are labelled honestly and do not waive activation gates.

### Requirement: US27-P46 — Deliberately clear a current assessment on the same list

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to deliberately clear a current assessment on the same list, so that I can record that no stage currently fits without erasing earlier assessments. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P28.

#### Scenario: US27-P46 AC27-P28-01

- **GIVEN** an authorized partnership has a current stage,
- **WHEN** staff deliberately choose no current stage and save,
- **THEN** the current and proposed meanings are clear before Save; the same partnership/list now shows Not yet assessed and an available Set stage action, with earlier assessment retained.

#### Scenario: US27-P46 AC27-P28-02

- **GIVEN** a tenant stage is named Not yet assessed or equivalent,
- **WHEN** staff inspect or choose absence,
- **THEN** the configured stage and actual no-current-assessment option remain distinguishable by meaning and stable identity.

#### Scenario: US27-P46 AC27-P28-03

- **GIVEN** a request omits stage or the current value is denied, unloaded or unavailable,
- **WHEN** another edit is saved or a callback arrives,
- **THEN** the source current stage is not cleared and the UI never substitutes unknown coverage for accepted absence.

#### Scenario: US27-P46 AC27-P28-04

- **GIVEN** clear races another stage change, list switch, close or authority loss,
- **WHEN** both valid source orderings are exercised,
- **THEN** one permitted outcome commits atomically and stale input cannot erase or retarget the newer assessment/binding.

#### Scenario: US27-P46 AC27-P28-05

- **GIVEN** a successful clear has been followed by a newer assessment,
- **WHEN** the old command is retried after response loss,
- **THEN** the original receipt may be returned under current authority but the newer assessment remains.

#### Scenario: US27-P46 AC27-P28-06

- **GIVEN** the source already has current absence,
- **WHEN** the same-value command is submitted,
- **THEN** current authority/version checks still apply and no new assessment, transition timestamp, activity or duplicated history is created.

#### Scenario: US27-P46 AC27-P28-07

- **GIVEN** the same binding belongs to a withdrawn retained list,
- **WHEN** staff deliberately clear its assessment,
- **THEN** the binding stays usable under retained-work rules and no forced list migration or loss of past assessments occurs.

#### Scenario: US27-P46 AC27-P28-08

- **GIVEN** the cleared work is closed then genuinely reopened,
- **WHEN** the recorded state is reconstructed,
- **THEN** current absence remains; history is neither erased nor used as a last-non-null fallback.

#### Scenario: US27-P46 AC27-P28-09

- **GIVEN** current-stage filters, boards, counts and exports include the cleared work,
- **WHEN** authorized results are recomputed,
- **THEN** the work remains represented in the no-current-stage meaning without fake stage entry, last contact, gift or financial outcome.

#### Scenario: US27-P46 AC27-P28-10

- **GIVEN** staff use keyboard, screen reader, touch or a weak connection to clear,
- **WHEN** save succeeds, fails or returns an uncertain outcome,
- **THEN** input, current/proposed meaning, error/status and safe recovery remain understandable without required essay, second acknowledgment or invented follow-up.

### Requirement: US27-P47 — Update stage or list while work is paused

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to update stage or list while work is paused, so that I can keep records accurate without resuming work or completing its review. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P29.

#### Scenario: US27-P47 AC27-P29-01

- **GIVEN** paused work has a valid stage and scheduled internal review,
- **WHEN** authorized staff update the stage,
- **THEN** the new stage and history are saved while pause, review date and review outcome remain unchanged.

#### Scenario: US27-P47 AC27-P29-02

- **GIVEN** paused work has an assessment or uses a withdrawn retained list,
- **WHEN** authorized staff clear the assessment or continue using an eligible retained stage,
- **THEN** the deliberate change succeeds under its source rules without mandatory list migration, resuming or completing the review.

#### Scenario: US27-P47 AC27-P29-03

- **GIVEN** paused work is switched to another currently eligible approved list,
- **WHEN** staff save the reviewed destination and optional assessment,
- **THEN** the deliberate binding change preserves history and paused lifecycle; a withdrawn destination cannot be newly attached.

#### Scenario: US27-P47 AC27-P29-04

- **GIVEN** a review, resume, close or permission change races an edit from a paused form,
- **WHEN** the stale edit is submitted,
- **THEN** it cannot restore stale paused state or overwrite the concurrent review/lifecycle decision.

#### Scenario: US27-P47 AC27-P29-05

- **GIVEN** a user opens paused work on desktop, narrow mobile or assistive technology,
- **WHEN** they find edit actions,
- **THEN** Paused and permitted review context remain visible and normal stage/list actions clearly explain that these edits do not resume work.

### Requirement: US27-P48 — See complete permitted counts and truthful unavailable states

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to see complete permitted counts and truthful unavailable states, so that I can avoid deciding from partial pages or fabricated financial totals. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P31.

#### Scenario: US27-P48 AC27-P31-01

- **GIVEN** the latest qualifying source fact or relevant obligation lies beyond all current display caps,
- **WHEN** the complete projection is computed,
- **THEN** the admitted total or latest fact includes that evidence or explicitly reports incomplete coverage, never a falsely complete capped result.

#### Scenario: US27-P48 AC27-P31-02

- **GIVEN** finance source coverage is absent or amounts use different currencies/periods and recognition meanings,
- **WHEN** a cultivation summary is displayed,
- **THEN** unknown is not zero and incomparable values are not summed, converted or represented as received cash.

#### Scenario: US27-P48 AC27-P31-03

- **GIVEN** one source gift or encounter appears in multiple allowed views,
- **WHEN** organization totals and source identity are reconciled,
- **THEN** the source fact is counted once for its admitted aggregate meaning and remains a reference rather than another financial or encounter record.

#### Scenario: US27-P48 AC27-P31-04

- **GIVEN** the newest source fact is corrected, restricted, merged or erased,
- **WHEN** derived views rebuild,
- **THEN** the result may move backward or become unknown; stale cached maxima and forbidden derivatives do not remain authoritative.

#### Scenario: US27-P48 AC27-P31-05

- **GIVEN** a protected fact differs between otherwise equivalent fixtures,
- **WHEN** authorized summaries, ranks, filters and export contents are compared,
- **THEN** the restricted fact does not change admitted output unless an independently qualified aggregate disclosure exists.

#### Scenario: US27-P48 AC27-P31-06

- **GIVEN** staff change a ministry target, stage, lifecycle or relationship purpose,
- **WHEN** source owners are reconciled,
- **THEN** gifts, commitments, Designations, consent, recurring support and publishing authority do not change by implication.

#### Scenario: US27-P48 AC27-P31-07

- **GIVEN** a reader fails or a claimed-complete projection disagrees with source evidence,
- **WHEN** the authorized user opens the affected view,
- **THEN** it exposes incomplete/unavailable or pending reconciliation truth and a permitted owner resolution route instead of false zero or healthy status.

### Requirement: US27-P49 — Detect and repair source-backed reminder and projection failures

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized operations staff member, I want to detect and repair source-backed reminder and projection failures, so that I can restore the same work without guessed recipients or manual database edits. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P10, REQ27-P32.

#### Scenario: US27-P49 AC27-P10-01

- **GIVEN** the lead, creator, Party representative and finance owner are different people,
- **WHEN** review responsibility and due attention resolve,
- **THEN** only the qualified current lead or authorized explicit review assignee receives the appropriate source-backed work; other identities receive nothing without independent qualification.

#### Scenario: US27-P49 AC27-P10-02

- **GIVEN** a currently eligible explicit review assignee exists,
- **WHEN** the partnership lead changes,
- **THEN** the explicit review responsibility remains until a deliberate qualified handoff changes it; no duplicate recipient is inferred.

#### Scenario: US27-P49 AC27-P10-03

- **GIVEN** an enabled staff or missionary recipient profile is used,
- **WHEN** the user opens the task and attention,
- **THEN** each route resolves the same source review and allowed action with a working role-safe authenticated link; incomplete or reserved profiles cannot emit substitute attention.

#### Scenario: US27-P49 AC27-P10-04

- **GIVEN** a user reads an actionable review notification,
- **WHEN** they revisit work or try to archive it,
- **THEN** the unresolved work remains actionable and archive is unavailable; after source ending it is visible only within the authorized 90-day Recent ceiling.

#### Scenario: US27-P49 AC27-P10-05

- **GIVEN** zero eligible recipients, an indeterminate resolver or revocation immediately before release occurs,
- **WHEN** the worker processes the review,
- **THEN** no unqualified recipient receives attention and an authorized source repair exception remains; restored access does not revive an ended engagement.

#### Scenario: US27-P49 AC27-P10-06

- **GIVEN** two due workers and a reschedule/resume/end contend,
- **WHEN** both serialization orders and response-loss retries are exercised,
- **THEN** there is one intended admitted attention, no stale active attention or successor overwrite, and no old prepared envelope is sent to a newly chosen recipient.

#### Scenario: US27-P49 AC27-P10-07

- **GIVEN** a protected reason, restricted identity or worker location is associated with the source,
- **WHEN** task previews, notification payloads, logs, counts, exports and links are examined under each admitted role,
- **THEN** only independently allowed minimal metadata appears; public and donor surfaces receive no review context.

#### Scenario: US27-P49 AC27-P10-08

- **GIVEN** the optional email provider or Studio is unavailable,
- **WHEN** a qualified internal-only review becomes due,
- **THEN** native source work and permitted in-product attention remain functional without a substitute email or duplicate Studio effect.

#### Scenario: US27-P49 AC27-P32-01

- **GIVEN** optional Studio/public/finance products are absent but required owner seams are qualified,
- **WHEN** native identified-ministry work is exercised,
- **THEN** ordinary work remains usable and no profile/seed/parallel-system fallback substitutes for a missing required source.

#### Scenario: US27-P49 AC27-P32-02

- **GIVEN** retained rows include ambiguous one-profile-to-many-ministry mappings and unknown historical purpose/time,
- **WHEN** migration runs repeatedly and is interrupted/resumed,
- **THEN** only evidenced mappings are retained, unresolved associations stay explicit and no newly approved enrollment/stage/task/contact/message effect is fabricated.

#### Scenario: US27-P49 AC27-P32-03

- **GIVEN** old and new application/worker/schema combinations are deployed,
- **WHEN** supported combinations execute and unsupported writers are fenced,
- **THEN** no incompatible command can bypass new source invariants or misinterpret new state; accepted evidence remains reconciled.

#### Scenario: US27-P49 AC27-P32-04

- **GIVEN** new work has committed before admission/effect disable or rollback,
- **WHEN** containment and recovery execute,
- **THEN** accepted work/history/receipts remain readable under authority and repairable while new unsafe effects stop; rollback cannot reopen an old bypass.

#### Scenario: US27-P49 AC27-P32-05

- **GIVEN** a restored snapshot predates revocation, erasure, schedule change, completed review or consumed delivery,
- **WHEN** reads and workers resume,
- **THEN** current owner dispositions are reconciled first; forbidden content, revoked recipients, completed work and consumed effects cannot revive.

#### Scenario: US27-P49 AC27-P32-06

- **GIVEN** backfill or a recipient/surface profile changes from Reserved to Live,
- **WHEN** activation runs,
- **THEN** no historical attention is emitted; any eligible legacy-work reminder proves a distinct prospective qualified source transition.

#### Scenario: US27-P49 AC27-P32-07

- **GIVEN** the declared largest Tenant, many small Tenants and concurrent interactive/batch work run together,
- **WHEN** query plans, source scans and bounded recovery are measured,
- **THEN** approved numeric budgets and honest coverage are met and one overloaded Tenant cannot starve the others.

#### Scenario: US27-P49 AC27-P32-08

- **GIVEN** an eligible due review lacks required local attention,
- **WHEN** the delay passes 15 minutes from the later eligible due/schedule-recipient boundary,
- **THEN** the recorded platform-operations signal fires without retry-resetting age, substitute email or marking review complete.

#### Scenario: US27-P49 AC27-P32-09

- **GIVEN** a local materialization reaches typed terminal failure or a qualified responsibility resolver is zero/indeterminate,
- **WHEN** operating recovery is evaluated,
- **THEN** the named owner receives an authorized source repair route and the original identity is reconciled without broadening access or routine SQL edits.

#### Scenario: US27-P49 AC27-P32-10

- **GIVEN** release evidence is assembled,
- **WHEN** requirements and source traces are checked,
- **THEN** positive/negative/boundary/concurrency/migration/accessibility outcomes have appropriate real evidence; unrun, mocked, demo and structural checks are labelled honestly and do not waive activation gates.

### Requirement: US27-P50 — Distinguish recorded exchange, outreach and agreed follow-up

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized staff member or missionary, I want to distinguish recorded exchange, outreach and agreed follow-up, so that I can understand real contact without treating messages or opens as conversations. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P33.

#### Scenario: US27-P50 AC27-P33-01

- **GIVEN** a personal email or thank-you is sent without a reply being required,
- **WHEN** delivery, opening and a later genuine human exchange are recorded,
- **THEN** outreach updates independently; only qualified exchange evidence updates exchange recency, and no inferred pending-response/overdue warning is created.

#### Scenario: US27-P50 AC27-P33-02

- **GIVEN** a relevant human inbound arrives without a stored prior outbound and may be a brief decline,
- **WHEN** its source outcome is qualified,
- **THEN** it can count as an exchange without message pairing, length, sentiment or financial threshold; automated or uncertain reply evidence alone cannot count.

#### Scenario: US27-P50 AC27-P33-03

- **GIVEN** call attempts, voicemail, booked/cancelled/no-show meetings, passive attendance and an actual manually recorded conversation exist,
- **WHEN** exchange recency is computed,
- **THEN** only actual qualified exchange evidence contributes; calendar/task completion alone creates none.

#### Scenario: US27-P50 AC27-P33-04

- **GIVEN** two narratives or repeated transport/import/share/edit refer to one underlying meeting,
- **WHEN** staff and missionary permitted views display it,
- **THEN** one actual encounter remains with distinct attributed contributions and no duplicated logging step or event count.

#### Scenario: US27-P50 AC27-P33-05

- **GIVEN** history includes backdated, date-only, unknown-date, future-plan and invalid future-completed values across timezone travel,
- **WHEN** source dates and recency are displayed,
- **THEN** actual precision is retained; unknown never becomes save time/epoch or reliable aging and future planned/untrue completion does not become actual exchange.

#### Scenario: US27-P50 AC27-P33-06

- **GIVEN** the latest qualifying exchange lies outside the first fifty display rows,
- **WHEN** the latest source fact is found then corrected/restricted/merged,
- **THEN** source-complete recency finds it and subsequently derives an earlier or unknown truthful result rather than a stale permanent maximum.

#### Scenario: US27-P50 AC27-P33-07

- **GIVEN** a user supplies another Tenant, church-affiliation-only link, different ministry purpose or merely selected participant,
- **WHEN** a contribution or projection is requested,
- **THEN** no other partnership is updated and protected date/type/source/count information is not disclosed.

#### Scenario: US27-P50 AC27-P33-08

- **GIVEN** a source body expires or source authority is revoked,
- **WHEN** recency, summaries, source links and exports are requested,
- **THEN** only separately qualified permitted body-free facts remain; copied bodies or church access cannot bypass source restrictions.

#### Scenario: US27-P50 AC27-P33-09

- **GIVEN** a missionary chooses not to log or an actual reply arrives during paused/closed work,
- **WHEN** the ordinary workspace and relationship are evaluated,
- **THEN** normal use remains available without penalty, score or invented task; lifecycle and actual consent/obligations remain unchanged.

#### Scenario: US27-P50 AC27-P33-10

- **GIVEN** the user encounters empty, filtered, incomplete, undated, loading or unavailable history,
- **WHEN** the compact relationship context is displayed,
- **THEN** each admitted state is understandable without Never contacted claims or revealing private event existence.

#### Scenario: US27-P50 AC27-P33-11

- **GIVEN** a contribution save is slow, loses its response or races a scope change,
- **WHEN** the user recovers it,
- **THEN** permitted draft context and one accepted source intent remain, with no silent offline publication, duplicate encounter or false success.

#### Scenario: US27-P50 AC27-P33-12

- **GIVEN** keyboard/screen-reader/touch/mobile/RTL/long-name/low-bandwidth users complete the flow,
- **WHEN** they record, explain and correct an exchange,
- **THEN** instructions, source links, focus, contrast, reflow, dates and status remain usable without address forms, transcripts, duplicate logs or meaningfulness checkboxes.

#### Scenario: US27-P50 AC27-P33-13

- **GIVEN** representative staff and missionaries inspect sent outreach, real exchange, unknown coverage and agreed follow-up examples,
- **WHEN** they interpret the UI without coaching,
- **THEN** they distinguish all four meanings; any unsafe confusion blocks rollout expansion until corrected and retested.

#### Scenario: US27-P50 AC27-P33-14

- **GIVEN** donor checkout, donor account or public content is used,
- **WHEN** internal exchange recency changes or becomes old,
- **THEN** those surfaces gain no field/step/internal disclosure and no new contact is triggered merely by age or missing reply.

### Requirement: US27-P51 — Contribute a useful exchange voluntarily and correct its actual occurrence

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a missionary, I want to contribute a useful exchange voluntarily and correct its actual occurrence, so that I can share accurate working history without performance scoring or compulsory logging. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-P33.

#### Scenario: US27-P51 AC27-P33-01

- **GIVEN** a personal email or thank-you is sent without a reply being required,
- **WHEN** delivery, opening and a later genuine human exchange are recorded,
- **THEN** outreach updates independently; only qualified exchange evidence updates exchange recency, and no inferred pending-response/overdue warning is created.

#### Scenario: US27-P51 AC27-P33-02

- **GIVEN** a relevant human inbound arrives without a stored prior outbound and may be a brief decline,
- **WHEN** its source outcome is qualified,
- **THEN** it can count as an exchange without message pairing, length, sentiment or financial threshold; automated or uncertain reply evidence alone cannot count.

#### Scenario: US27-P51 AC27-P33-03

- **GIVEN** call attempts, voicemail, booked/cancelled/no-show meetings, passive attendance and an actual manually recorded conversation exist,
- **WHEN** exchange recency is computed,
- **THEN** only actual qualified exchange evidence contributes; calendar/task completion alone creates none.

#### Scenario: US27-P51 AC27-P33-04

- **GIVEN** two narratives or repeated transport/import/share/edit refer to one underlying meeting,
- **WHEN** staff and missionary permitted views display it,
- **THEN** one actual encounter remains with distinct attributed contributions and no duplicated logging step or event count.

#### Scenario: US27-P51 AC27-P33-05

- **GIVEN** history includes backdated, date-only, unknown-date, future-plan and invalid future-completed values across timezone travel,
- **WHEN** source dates and recency are displayed,
- **THEN** actual precision is retained; unknown never becomes save time/epoch or reliable aging and future planned/untrue completion does not become actual exchange.

#### Scenario: US27-P51 AC27-P33-06

- **GIVEN** the latest qualifying exchange lies outside the first fifty display rows,
- **WHEN** the latest source fact is found then corrected/restricted/merged,
- **THEN** source-complete recency finds it and subsequently derives an earlier or unknown truthful result rather than a stale permanent maximum.

#### Scenario: US27-P51 AC27-P33-07

- **GIVEN** a user supplies another Tenant, church-affiliation-only link, different ministry purpose or merely selected participant,
- **WHEN** a contribution or projection is requested,
- **THEN** no other partnership is updated and protected date/type/source/count information is not disclosed.

#### Scenario: US27-P51 AC27-P33-08

- **GIVEN** a source body expires or source authority is revoked,
- **WHEN** recency, summaries, source links and exports are requested,
- **THEN** only separately qualified permitted body-free facts remain; copied bodies or church access cannot bypass source restrictions.

#### Scenario: US27-P51 AC27-P33-09

- **GIVEN** a missionary chooses not to log or an actual reply arrives during paused/closed work,
- **WHEN** the ordinary workspace and relationship are evaluated,
- **THEN** normal use remains available without penalty, score or invented task; lifecycle and actual consent/obligations remain unchanged.

#### Scenario: US27-P51 AC27-P33-10

- **GIVEN** the user encounters empty, filtered, incomplete, undated, loading or unavailable history,
- **WHEN** the compact relationship context is displayed,
- **THEN** each admitted state is understandable without Never contacted claims or revealing private event existence.

#### Scenario: US27-P51 AC27-P33-11

- **GIVEN** a contribution save is slow, loses its response or races a scope change,
- **WHEN** the user recovers it,
- **THEN** permitted draft context and one accepted source intent remain, with no silent offline publication, duplicate encounter or false success.

#### Scenario: US27-P51 AC27-P33-12

- **GIVEN** keyboard/screen-reader/touch/mobile/RTL/long-name/low-bandwidth users complete the flow,
- **WHEN** they record, explain and correct an exchange,
- **THEN** instructions, source links, focus, contrast, reflow, dates and status remain usable without address forms, transcripts, duplicate logs or meaningfulness checkboxes.

#### Scenario: US27-P51 AC27-P33-13

- **GIVEN** representative staff and missionaries inspect sent outreach, real exchange, unknown coverage and agreed follow-up examples,
- **WHEN** they interpret the UI without coaching,
- **THEN** they distinguish all four meanings; any unsafe confusion blocks rollout expansion until corrected and retested.

#### Scenario: US27-P51 AC27-P33-14

- **GIVEN** donor checkout, donor account or public content is used,
- **WHEN** internal exchange recency changes or becomes old,
- **THEN** those surfaces gain no field/step/internal disclosure and no new contact is triggered merely by age or missing reply.

### Requirement: US27-F01 — Record significant financial, nonfinancial and narrowly described noncash invitations in the same Requests experience

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a development staff member, I want to record significant financial, nonfinancial and narrowly described noncash invitations in the same Requests experience, so that I can coordinate relevant ministry support without fake financial fields or another partnership journey. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F01, REQ27-F04, REQ27-F13, REQ27-F07, REQ27-F15.

#### Scenario: US27-F01 AC27-F01-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F01, REQ27-F04, REQ27-F13.

- **GIVEN** an authorized staff actor and one identified partnership.

- **WHEN** creating and reopening monetary, prayer, introduction, hosting, another significant nonfinancial and narrow noncash Requests.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Create and read prayer, introduction, hosting, another valid nonfinancial purpose, monetary and narrow noncash-intention requests on the same source model; no new journey or irrelevant money field is required.

#### Scenario: US27-F01 AC27-F04-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F04, REQ27-F07, REQ27-F15.

- **GIVEN** a permitted noncash intention without a qualified value or posted gift.

- **WHEN** saving it and reading financial/request totals.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Noncash intention can be recorded without invented value or gift; a display estimate, if independently admitted by its owner, cannot enter requested/received monetary totals as source money.

### Requirement: US27-F02 — Keep independently answerable invitations distinct while retaining one coherent partnership

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a partnership lead, I want to keep independently answerable invitations distinct while retaining one coherent partnership, so that agreement to prayer or hosting cannot silently become agreement to funding. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F02, REQ27-F15.

#### Scenario: US27-F02 AC27-F02-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F02, REQ27-F15.

- **GIVEN** one partnership with separately answerable funding and prayer invitations and one installment-based funding invitation.

- **WHEN** recording prayer acceptance and adding the funding installment or designation details.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: On one partnership, prayer acceptance leaves a separate funding request unanswered; several installments or designations for one decision do not duplicate requests.

### Requirement: US27-F03 — See qualified next work or an owned exception for outstanding Requests and preserve genuinely completed work without perpetual tasks

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a responsible staff member, I want to see qualified next work or an owned exception for outstanding Requests and preserve genuinely completed work without perpetual tasks, so that responsibility remains clear when a task completes, an owner leaves or one Request closes. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F03, REQ27-F17, REQ27-F08, REQ27-F10.

#### Scenario: US27-F03 AC27-F03-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F03, REQ27-F17.

- **GIVEN** outstanding Requests with qualified shared follow-up and a separate genuinely finished Request.

- **WHEN** completing the call, removing the active owner's eligibility and viewing the finished Request.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: A qualified follow-up is visible once through shared work; completing a shared call changes neither all linked responses nor source fulfillment. For outstanding work, loss of owner produces eligible reassignment or an owned visible exception. Genuinely finished requests retain history without new tasks or forced replacement owners.

#### Scenario: US27-F03 AC27-F03-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F03, REQ27-F08, REQ27-F10.

- **GIVEN** several Requests, an independently owned promise and outstanding accountable work.

- **WHEN** closing one Request or changing its responsible staff member.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Close/withdraw one request or change its responsible person: preserve other requests, source promises, current task accountability and meaningful history; assignment never creates access.

### Requirement: US27-F04 — Record exact qualified monetary terms and explicit unknowns while keeping nonfinancial records in ordinary counts

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a development staff member, I want to record exact qualified monetary terms and explicit unknowns while keeping nonfinancial records in ordinary counts, so that financial meaning and all Request totals remain truthful across currencies and source joins. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F04, REQ27-F10, REQ27-F11, REQ27-F05, REQ27-F15.

#### Scenario: US27-F04 AC27-F04-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F04, REQ27-F10, REQ27-F11.

- **GIVEN** qualified zero-, two- and three-decimal currencies and exact representable boundary amounts.

- **WHEN** saving valid, unknown, overflowing, invalid-currency and hidden nonfinancial monetary tuples.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Known monetary terms require qualified entity/currency and exact currency-aware values; unknown amount remains unknown. Nonfinancial terms with any hidden financial tuple are rejected, not coerced to zero. Test zero-, two- and three-decimal currency handling, range/overflow and invalid currency, against the actual qualified currency contract.

#### Scenario: US27-F04 AC27-F04-04

**Requirements:** REQ27-F04, REQ27-F05, REQ27-F15.

- **GIVEN** nonfinancial, unknown-amount and monetary Requests with duplicate source joins.

- **WHEN** reading ordinary counts and financial totals before and after source correction.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Ordinary request counts include nonfinancial and amount-unknown records; financial totals exclude nonapplicable amounts and do not change because a money row joined twice. Source corrections preserve forecast history.

### Requirement: US27-F05 — Record an already-presented invitation and response together, including late responses to earlier presented terms

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized history recorder, I want to record an already-presented invitation and response together, including late responses to earlier presented terms, so that the history reflects actual events without fabricated dates, sends or stale overwrites. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F05, REQ27-F06, REQ27-F13, REQ27-F09.

#### Scenario: US27-F05 AC27-F05-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F05, REQ27-F06, REQ27-F13.

- **GIVEN** an actual previously presented invitation with a known reply and unknown or partial occurrence date.

- **WHEN** recording presentation and response together in one authorized save.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Staff record an already-presented request and response in one save with unknown/partial date precision; record time remains trustworthy and distinct. No fictional send/task/presentation timeline is generated.

#### Scenario: US27-F05 AC27-F05-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F05, REQ27-F06, REQ27-F09.

- **GIVEN** a Request with replaced terms and an earlier presented version.

- **WHEN** submitting a stale edit and separately recording a late reply deliberately against that earlier version.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: A stale edit against replaced terms conflicts explicitly. A fresh authorized recording can deliberately identify older presented terms for a late response; it does not relabel that response against current terms. Corrected occurrence evidence retains original recording and correction history. Day-only dates and daylight-saving boundaries do not imply a fabricated instant.

### Requirement: US27-F06 — Retain original and revised proposals and forecast versions with explicit alternative and reporting scope

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a development manager, I want to retain original and revised proposals and forecast versions with explicit alternative and reporting scope, so that forecast comparison remains honest and overlapping funding views cannot inflate expectations. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F05, REQ27-F06, REQ27-F15.

#### Scenario: US27-F06 AC27-F05-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F05, REQ27-F06.

- **GIVEN** a presented monetary Request with an earlier staff forecast.

- **WHEN** revising requested terms and the forecast, then reading both histories.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Revise a presented request and a staff forecast: original terms, what was actually presented, revised terms and prior forecast remain independently reconstructible without reading today's labels as historical fact.

#### Scenario: US27-F06 AC27-F05-04

**Requirements:** REQ27-F05, REQ27-F15.

- **GIVEN** exclusive financial alternatives and overlapping staff, legal-giving and recognition views.

- **WHEN** computing financial totals within and across entity, currency and horizon lanes.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Alternatives, overlapping staff attribution, recognition and source gifts cannot be summed as independent funding; per-entity/currency/horizon reports identify their measure and coverage.

### Requirement: US27-F07 — Record no answer, partial or conditional response, deferral, decline and staff withdrawal distinctly

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a development staff member, I want to record no answer, partial or conditional response, deferral, decline and staff withdrawal distinctly, so that I can understand what the supporter actually said and choose appropriate follow-through. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F06, REQ27-F12, REQ27-F14.

#### Scenario: US27-F07 AC27-F06-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F06, REQ27-F12, REQ27-F14.

- **GIVEN** Requests with no answer, qualified conditional or partial answer, deferral, decline and staff withdrawal.

- **WHEN** recording and comparing those outcomes, including a withheld response projection.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Record partial/conditional response, deferral, decline and staff withdrawal; none collapses into the others or alters the original requested terms. No-answer remains distinct from denied/unavailable response data.

### Requirement: US27-F08 — View qualified source outcomes and invoke a deliberate independently authorized handoff

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized development staff member, I want to view qualified source outcomes and invoke a deliberate independently authorized handoff, so that recording a positive response does not create money, consent, subscriptions or bookings. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F07, REQ27-F08, REQ27-F12, REQ27-F15.

#### Scenario: US27-F08 AC27-F07-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F07.

- **GIVEN** a positive financial response and a separately eligible P16 command.

- **WHEN** saving the response and then deliberately invoking the commitment handoff.
