- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Positive funding response performs no charge, promise creation or gift write. A deliberate qualified P16 handoff proves its independent donor-instruction/identity/authorization and returns its own result.

#### Scenario: US27-F08 AC27-F07-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F07, REQ27-F08, REQ27-F12.

- **GIVEN** prayer, introduction and hosting responses plus applicable contact restrictions.

- **WHEN** recording positive responses and attempting related communication or execution.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Prayer/hosting/introduction acceptance sends no newsletter, grants no access, creates no unqualified referral edge, booking or consent. Never-ask and current communication rules remain enforced at actual outward action.

#### Scenario: US27-F08 AC27-F07-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F07, REQ27-F12, REQ27-F15.

- **GIVEN** qualified financial links with partial, reversed, unavailable and withheld source coverage.

- **WHEN** reading their current outcomes and trying to edit source money through the Request.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Financial source links display current permitted authoritative outcomes, including reversal/partial fulfillment, without copying them into a writable request amount. Unknown/denied coverage cannot appear as zero.

### Requirement: US27-F09 — Change relevant details and record permitted updates while work is paused or closed

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a Request editor, I want to change relevant details and record permitted updates while work is paused or closed, so that corrections preserve entered data, earlier terms and lifecycle intent. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F04, REQ27-F14, REQ27-F08.

#### Scenario: US27-F09 AC27-F04-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F04, REQ27-F14.

- **GIVEN** an unsaved monetary Request with entered values and a saved presented version.

- **WHEN** switching its current monetary applicability off and back on, then saving.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Switch an unsaved monetary request to nonfinancial and back: no silent data loss or hidden money submission. Saved changes preserve authorized prior terms; omission and explicit absence remain distinct.

#### Scenario: US27-F09 AC27-F08-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F08.

- **GIVEN** paused and closed partnerships with late responses or source gifts.

- **WHEN** recording authorized updates and comparing partnership and child-work lifecycle.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Edit/record response while partnership is paused, or record a late fact after closure: preserve pause/review/closure and ended effects. Genuine return uses explicit reopening; a late gift does not reopen work.

### Requirement: US27-F10 — Have Request commands, history and every derived projection preserve exact tenant, field, purpose and retention boundaries

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a tenant privacy administrator, I want to have Request commands, history and every derived projection preserve exact tenant, field, purpose and retention boundaries, so that links, role labels, imports and privileged paths cannot expose or rewrite forbidden facts. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F10, REQ27-F11, REQ27-F12.

#### Scenario: US27-F10 AC27-F10-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F10, REQ27-F11, REQ27-F12.

- **GIVEN** two tenants and otherwise valid foreign parent, subject, owner, task, interaction and financial identifiers.

- **WHEN** creating and changing Requests through commands, imports and privileged workers.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Two-tenant tests reject foreign partnership/Party/ministry/owner/task/interaction/entity/designation/history links on create, update, import and elevated worker paths, including absent financial fields.

#### Scenario: US27-F10 AC27-F10-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F10, REQ27-F11.

- **GIVEN** real prepare, read, edit and other exact capability actors with protected historical fields.

- **WHEN** attempting forged attribution, unauthorized field changes, retargeting and zero-row updates.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Test every real actor class/capability and old/new row transformation. Deny forged actor/approval/owner authority, parent retargeting, unauthorized protected fields and direct immutable-history changes. Verify zero-row UPDATE is not misreported as success.

#### Scenario: US27-F10 AC27-F12-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F12.

- **GIVEN** restricted Request content and an actor whose access is revoked after initial viewing.

- **WHEN** reading every projection, receipt and outbound representation.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Search, counts, list, history, brief, attachment, export, cache, notification and replay result each respect existence/field/purpose restrictions and current revocation; no raw sensitive prose enters technical logs.

#### Scenario: US27-F10 AC27-F10-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F10, REQ27-F11, REQ27-F12.

- **GIVEN** the actual supported Supabase role, grant, policy and privileged-writer configuration.

- **WHEN** executing positive and negative Request operations through each admitted database path.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Exercise real Supabase roles, grants, policies, views/RPCs and privileged paths; independently verify both eligible operations and prohibited transformed states. Elevated tests do not substitute for ordinary-role proof.

#### Scenario: US27-F10 AC27-F11-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F11, REQ27-F12.

- **GIVEN** retained Request history, restricted content and applicable disposal obligations.

- **WHEN** removing ordinary parents and separately invoking authorized redaction or retention.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Retention/redaction preserves only permitted business/audit facts, removes restricted content from read models/caches/exports as governed, and follows backup policy. History cannot be hard-deleted through ordinary child/parent removal.

### Requirement: US27-F11 — Save and safely check or retry one intended change despite conflict, interruption or downstream failure

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a development staff member on an unreliable connection, I want to save and safely check or retry one intended change despite conflict, interruption or downstream failure, so that accepted work is not lost, duplicated or reapplied over newer truth. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F09, REQ27-F16, REQ27-F13, REQ27-F14.

#### Scenario: US27-F11 AC27-F09-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F09, REQ27-F16.

- **GIVEN** one intended mutation with history, result and effect intent.

- **WHEN** injecting precommit failure and separately losing the response after a successful commit.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Inject failure before commit: request change, history, result and required effect intent all roll back. Lost response after commit returns the same authorized durable result; retry cannot duplicate or overwrite a newer change.

#### Scenario: US27-F11 AC27-F09-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F09, REQ27-F16.

- **GIVEN** two authorized staff and duplicate import or worker deliveries.

- **WHEN** controlling revise/respond/close/reassign races and delivering ambiguous or late acknowledgments.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Controlled concurrent revise/respond/close/owner-change, duplicate import/worker delivery, changed-payload retry and late downstream acknowledgment preserve invariants. External ambiguous success reconciles before repeat execution.

#### Scenario: US27-F11 AC27-F09-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F09, REQ27-F13, REQ27-F14.

- **GIVEN** mobile and international forms on slow connections with a concurrent edit or expired session.

- **WHEN** saving, recovering and reconciling the same intended operation.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Narrow mobile layout, long international names/translated labels, locale money/dates, slow/lost response, permission expiry and concurrent edit preserve clarity and current-safe draft recovery; one intentional save yields one business effect.

### Requirement: US27-F12 — Qualify the complete accessible Request journey, data adoption, native independence and measured workload

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a product and release owner, I want to qualify the complete accessible Request journey, data adoption, native independence and measured workload, so that the actual product is usable and recoverable within its evidenced supported envelope. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F13, REQ27-F14, REQ27-F18, REQ27-F07, REQ27-F16, REQ27-F17.

#### Scenario: US27-F12 AC27-F13-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F13.

- **GIVEN** the complete shared Request interface and keyboard/screen-reader users.

- **WHEN** adding, editing, recording a response and opening the actual next action.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Keyboard-only and screen-reader users can add, edit, record response and follow next action with labelled fields, focus continuity, non-color states, announced errors and no drag requirement. Verify WCAG 2.2 AA applicable criteria, not just an automated score.

#### Scenario: US27-F12 AC27-F13-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F13, REQ27-F14.

- **GIVEN** representative ministry staff and financial/nonfinancial scenarios.

- **WHEN** asking them to complete the flow and explain requested, responded and received meanings.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Representative ministry staff complete funding and nonfinancial scenarios, identify what was requested versus responded/received, change relevant details and find the next action without facilitator correction of those meanings. Any critical meaning/access/action error blocks pilot acceptance.

#### Scenario: US27-F12 AC27-F18-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F18.

- **GIVEN** production-shaped retained data with ambiguous legacy rows and old/new clients.

- **WHEN** rehearsing migration, quarantine, mixed writers and rollback after new writes.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: A production-shaped retained-data rehearsal proves explicit mapping, no fabricated responses, duplicate handling, counts/source totals, quarantine ownership, old/new writer compatibility and safe roll-forward/rollback after new records exist.

#### Scenario: US27-F12 AC27-F07-04

**Requirements:** REQ27-F07, REQ27-F16, REQ27-F18.

- **GIVEN** Phase 34/42 and unrelated optional operational products unavailable.

- **WHEN** completing ordinary nonfinancial work and attempting a source-dependent financial handoff.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: With Phase 34/42 and unrelated event/volunteer/application products unavailable, prepare/record/resolve a nonfinancial request and follow internal work. Financial handoffs fail visibly when their required owner is unavailable; no shadow replacement appears.

#### Scenario: US27-F12 AC27-F17-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F17, REQ27-F18.

- **GIVEN** a recorded supported workload and declared numeric input, range, page and batch budgets.

- **WHEN** measuring real authorized reads, writes, concurrency and recovery.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Under recorded supported tenant/request/history/actor workload and declared input/page/batch budgets, indexed plans, authorization cost, concurrency and recovery meet the adopted service budgets. Retain dataset, units, percentile/throughput definitions and limits; no unmeasured “scalable” claim.

### Requirement: US27-F13 — Choose a simple review default and explicit Request exceptions with their effective basis visible

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized organization policy owner, I want to choose a simple review default and explicit Request exceptions with their effective basis visible, so that routine work stays brief while required review cannot disappear through ordinary edits. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F19, REQ27-F32, REQ27-F34, REQ27-F20, REQ27-F24.

#### Scenario: US27-F13 AC27-F19-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F19, REQ27-F32, REQ27-F34.

- **GIVEN** known initial review-not-required policy and ordinary financial/nonfinancial Requests.

- **WHEN** preparing and proceeding with an independently authorized action.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: A routine financial or nonfinancial Request under known initial no-review policy adds no review step, fake decision or money field; authorized normal work remains available.

#### Scenario: US27-F13 AC27-F19-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F19, REQ27-F20.

- **GIVEN** inherit, require and not-required choices; ordinary and policy-qualified staff; an unavailable reviewer directory.

- **WHEN** reading or changing policy and taking proved review-free action.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Authorized default/explicit choices show the correct effective basis; an ordinary editor cannot change them through UI, API, import or omitted hidden fields. Unknown amount/type change does not clear review; a reviewer-directory outage does not block a proved review-free path.

#### Scenario: US27-F13 AC27-F19-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F19, REQ27-F24.

- **GIVEN** Requests in all explicit policy choices with pending or accepted review history.

- **WHEN** changing the organization default in both directions.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Change default in both directions across inherit/require/not-required fixtures. Explicit choices and past facts survive; pending work ends truthfully when no longer required; nothing sends automatically.

### Requirement: US27-F14 — Submit to one currently eligible reviewer or see a safe actionable routing problem

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a Request preparer, I want to submit to one currently eligible reviewer or see a safe actionable routing problem, so that my preparation is retained without false waiting, guessed recipients or access expansion. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F19, REQ27-F21, REQ27-F32, REQ27-F20, REQ27-F28, REQ27-F29.

#### Scenario: US27-F14 AC27-F19-04

**Requirements:** REQ27-F19, REQ27-F21, REQ27-F32.

- **GIVEN** no eligible reviewer, failed eligibility lookup, revoked reviewer and unknown-policy cases.

- **WHEN** saving preparation or trying to submit, decide or repair routing.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: No eligible reviewer, failed eligibility, permission loss and unknown policy are distinct from optional or routed waiting. Preparation can be retained; independently authorized source staff can discover and repair the routing problem.

#### Scenario: US27-F14 AC27-F20-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F20, REQ27-F28, REQ27-F29.

- **GIVEN** a reviewer with partial field access and some essential terms denied.

- **WHEN** opening and attempting a decision from Request detail, task, filters and counts.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: A reviewer sees sufficient authorized relevant terms, not unrelated private history. If an essential term is denied, no approval succeeds and no selection grants access. Filters/counts/errors do not expose hidden records.

### Requirement: US27-F15 — Approve exact proposed terms without restarting review for unrelated metadata

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an internal reviewer, I want to approve exact proposed terms without restarting review for unrelated metadata, so that the approval means what I reviewed and ordinary internal maintenance remains efficient. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F22, REQ27-F23, REQ27-F24.

#### Scenario: US27-F15 AC27-F22-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F22, REQ27-F23, REQ27-F24.

- **GIVEN** approved exact monetary and nonfinancial terms.

- **WHEN** changing each review-bearing field and later restoring an earlier value.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Approve identified monetary and nonfinancial terms. Change amount/currency/cadence/duration/outward text/condition/document version: old decision survives, changed terms cannot use it. Returning old values does not revive it automatically.

#### Scenario: US27-F15 AC27-F22-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F22.

- **GIVEN** an accepted review target with unrelated internal notes, forecasts and follow-up metadata.

- **WHEN** editing each incidental field and comparing current approval applicability.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Change only internal forecast/note/follow-up metadata outside the reviewed basis: the existing decision remains usable and no new review/task appears. Reviewed versus internal date is handled correctly.

### Requirement: US27-F16 — Withdraw, revise, request changes, reconsider or prospectively revoke a review through explicit actions

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a Request preparer or reviewer, I want to withdraw, revise, request changes, reconsider or prospectively revoke a review through explicit actions, so that each round and correction remains understandable without approving unseen terms. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F22, REQ27-F23, REQ27-F30, REQ27-F33.

#### Scenario: US27-F16 AC27-F22-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F22, REQ27-F23, REQ27-F30.

- **GIVEN** pending exact terms shown in two sessions.

- **WHEN** racing relevant edits or withdrawal against approval in both commit orders.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Pending relevant edit and approval race in both orders. One ordered outcome; reviewer never approves unseen replacement terms. Withdraw/supersede then use an old link: no decision on ended work.

#### Scenario: US27-F16 AC27-F23-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F23, REQ27-F33.

- **GIVEN** current and completed review rounds.

- **WHEN** requesting changes, refusing internally, withdrawing, reconsidering unchanged terms and prospectively revoking.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Request changes, internal refusal, withdrawal, same-terms reconsideration and authorized prospective revocation each preserve the correct prior decision and expose the appropriate next action. No original actor/time is edited.

### Requirement: US27-F17 — Separate preparation, policy, review and presentation powers and apply real identity-based independence floors

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a permission administrator, I want to separate preparation, policy, review and presentation powers and apply real identity-based independence floors, so that assignment or a second profile cannot manufacture decision authority. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F20, REQ27-F21, REQ27-F29, REQ27-F31.

#### Scenario: US27-F17 AC27-F20-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F20, REQ27-F21, REQ27-F29, REQ27-F31.

- **GIVEN** prepare-only, decide-only, policy-only, read-only and foreign-tenant identities.

- **WHEN** attempting the other actions using assignment, alternate profiles or old receipts.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Prepare-only, decide-only, policy-only, read-only and foreign-Tenant actors cannot acquire other powers by assignment, role label, changing owner/profile or replaying a receipt. Test same human across assignments.

#### Scenario: US27-F17 AC27-F21-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F21.

- **GIVEN** one person with qualified review authority and a separate fixture requiring independence.

- **WHEN** performing deliberate self-review, including through a second profile.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Qualified single-person review works deliberately where allowed; an actual independence floor rejects the same Party through a second profile. Missing eligible reviewer creates no automatic exemption/external fallback.

### Requirement: US27-F18 — Have current review, terms, authority and communication rules checked at actual action start

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized presenter, I want to have current review, terms, authority and communication rules checked at actual action start, so that a valid historical approval neither sends automatically nor bypasses a later governing restriction. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F24, REQ27-F25, REQ27-F26, REQ27-F30, REQ27-F21.

#### Scenario: US27-F18 AC27-F24-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F24, REQ27-F25, REQ27-F26.

- **GIVEN** a reviewable Request with independent supporter, partnership and finance facts.

- **WHEN** accepting or refusing review and then attempting supported communication.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Approve or refuse review while supporter response, stage, pause/closure, pledge, gifts and consent remain unchanged. Supported communication still enforces suppression, exact content and its own owner approval floors.

#### Scenario: US27-F18 AC27-F24-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F24, REQ27-F25, REQ27-F30.

- **GIVEN** approved queued outward work and independently changeable policy or revocation.

- **WHEN** racing tightening/revocation against authoritative action start in both orders.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Policy tightening/revocation and actual supported action start race in both orders. No action starts under stale clearance; a genuinely accepted earlier start remains history. Queue admission alone is insufficient.

#### Scenario: US27-F18 AC27-F21-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F21, REQ27-F24, REQ27-F30.

- **GIVEN** an eligible reviewer and a current exact pending round.

- **WHEN** racing authority revocation against acceptance, then testing later presenter authority.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Race reviewer authority loss against decision acceptance in both orders: revocation first rejects the decision; decision first retains its valid historical result. A currently permitted presenter still needs current action checks. Unrelated routing/policy metadata changes do not force review.

### Requirement: US27-F19 — Reassign exact review work safely within the shared Tasks Hub

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a review coordinator, I want to reassign exact review work safely within the shared Tasks Hub, so that the current eligible reviewer acts while stale tasks or generic completion cannot decide. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F21, REQ27-F23, REQ27-F27, REQ27-F30, REQ27-F28, REQ27-F34.

#### Scenario: US27-F19 AC27-F21-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F21, REQ27-F23, REQ27-F27, REQ27-F30.

- **GIVEN** a pending round assigned to one reviewer.

- **WHEN** racing reassignment against decision and attempting the old task/link afterward.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Pending reviewer reassignment and decision race in both orders; no stale recipient acts. Reassigned source work preserves lawful history/engagement rules, and old task completion cannot approve.

#### Scenario: US27-F19 AC27-F27-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F27, REQ27-F28, REQ27-F34.

- **GIVEN** one submitted source review occurrence.

- **WHEN** using generic shared-task complete, read, archive, bulk and API actions.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: One exact review occurrence yields at most one shared source task. Generic Complete/read/archive/API/bulk completion cannot decide. Removed recipients receive no new disclosure; stale links resolve current safe context.

### Requirement: US27-F20 — Receive one durable source decision and a currently permitted replay result

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a reviewer using an intermittent connection, I want to receive one durable source decision and a currently permitted replay result, so that concurrent retries and projection failures cannot create contradictory approvals or misleading success. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F29, REQ27-F30, REQ27-F31, REQ27-F27, REQ27-F28, REQ27-F33, REQ27-F32.

#### Scenario: US27-F20 AC27-F29-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F29, REQ27-F30, REQ27-F31.

- **GIVEN** one current pending round and one intended decision.

- **WHEN** submitting same-intent retries, altered-key reuse and competing different decisions concurrently.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Concurrent same-intent decision retry creates one accepted result/receipt/internal intent; same operation identity with altered decision conflicts. Distinct competing decisions on one round cannot both win.

#### Scenario: US27-F20 AC27-F27-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F27, REQ27-F30.

- **GIVEN** a prepared decision with mandatory local evidence and outbox intent.

- **WHEN** failing required local acceptance and separately failing only its postcommit projection.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Inject failure after decision preparation but before required local evidence/outbox completes: the transaction leaves no accepted partial decision. Secondary projection failure after commit preserves one accepted decision and recoverable intent.

#### Scenario: US27-F20 AC27-F28-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F28, REQ27-F31, REQ27-F33.

- **GIVEN** an accepted historical decision receipt and a newer current proposal.

- **WHEN** replaying the receipt before and after current read access changes.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Replaying a historical accepted receipt rechecks current read authority, reveals only permitted result and never replaces newer proposal/review state. Logs distinguish retry/conflict/current state without private payload.

#### Scenario: US27-F20 AC27-F30-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F30, REQ27-F31, REQ27-F32.

- **GIVEN** a review submission or decision accepted before its response is lost.

- **WHEN** checking the result, retrying the same intent and closing the editor.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Drop response after accepted submit/decision; UI truthfully checks/replays the same intent. Show saved decision separately from notification failure, retain recoverable feedback, and do not pretend dismissal canceled accepted work.

### Requirement: US27-F21 — Use clear accessible review controls and safe in-context recovery on mobile or assistive technology

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a review participant, I want to use clear accessible review controls and safe in-context recovery on mobile or assistive technology, so that I can identify the requirement, actual scope, next actor and correction path without confusion. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F32, REQ27-F28, REQ27-F37.

#### Scenario: US27-F21 AC27-F32-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F32.

- **GIVEN** the composed Request/reviewer/settings interface.

- **WHEN** testing keyboard, screen reader, focus, reflow, RTL, themes and long labels.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Keyboard, screen reader, focus return/status messages, 320-CSS-pixel reflow, zoom, RTL, light/dark and long translations/names pass in the real shared interface; no essential hover-only or obscured action.

#### Scenario: US27-F21 AC27-F28-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F28, REQ27-F32.

- **GIVEN** an active editor with malformed feedback, interrupted network or lost permission.

- **WHEN** reconnecting, retrying and inspecting rendered/persisted content.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Slow/disconnected network, reconnect, stale tab, malformed feedback and access loss preserve safe recovery and escaping. No raw HTML execution, default sensitive browser persistence or accidental resubmit on reconnect.

#### Scenario: US27-F21 AC27-F32-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F32, REQ27-F37.

- **GIVEN** actual staff/missionary participants and representative review scenarios.

- **WHEN** asking them to identify the requirement, next actor, approved scope and past-fact path.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: In moderated tests, actual staff/missionary participants can identify whether review is needed, who acts next, what was approved, how to request changes and how to record a past fact. Any unsafe misunderstanding is fixed and retested before release.

### Requirement: US27-F22 — Record truthful past facts and reconcile review effects under the same real authorization rules

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized source operator, I want to record truthful past facts and reconcile review effects under the same real authorization rules, so that historical recording, jobs and provider uncertainty do not fabricate approval or revive ended work. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F20, REQ27-F28, REQ27-F29, REQ27-F25, REQ27-F26, REQ27-F33, REQ27-F27, REQ27-F30, REQ27-F31, REQ27-F34, REQ27-F35.

#### Scenario: US27-F22 AC27-F20-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F20, REQ27-F28, REQ27-F29.

- **GIVEN** actual admitted database and privileged mutation paths.

- **WHEN** attempting foreign scope, target, actor and resulting-row substitutions including hidden conflicts.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Actual grants/RLS/functions/views/storage and privileged/service/import paths reject wrong Tenant/source/target/actor and forbidden resulting-row transformations. Test nonowner roles and hidden FK/uniqueness conflicts.

#### Scenario: US27-F22 AC27-F25-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F25, REQ27-F26, REQ27-F33.

- **GIVEN** an actual historical presentation or reply lacking known prior approval.

- **WHEN** recording it now against paused or closed work.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Record an actual past presentation/late response while review was absent or historical policy unknown, including paused/closed work. No fake approval, backdated clearance, automatic send or journey reopening.

#### Scenario: US27-F22 AC27-F27-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F27, REQ27-F30, REQ27-F31, REQ27-F34, REQ27-F35.

- **GIVEN** source review/work facts and delayed, repeated or reordered secondary deliveries.

- **WHEN** reconciling events and an ambiguous provider result.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Reconcile delayed/duplicated/out-of-order work/attention events and ambiguous provider result under exact owner contracts. Core review remains source truth; no second send or revived ended review results.

### Requirement: US27-F23 — Qualify review with mixed versions, restored data, unavailable optional products and measured concurrency

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a release owner, I want to qualify review with mixed versions, restored data, unavailable optional products and measured concurrency, so that activation preserves enforced review and truthful history rather than depending on an unproved badge. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F29, REQ27-F36, REQ27-F28, REQ27-F33, REQ27-F25, REQ27-F27, REQ27-F34, REQ27-F30, REQ27-F35, REQ27-F37.

#### Scenario: US27-F23 AC27-F29-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F29, REQ27-F36.

- **GIVEN** mixed client/worker versions and retained imported labels.

- **WHEN** migrating, partially activating and disabling new decisions.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Mixed old/new clients and workers, staged backfill/import, partial rollout and disabling new decisions preserve history and enforced review. Neither a default value nor imported label manufactures prior approval.

#### Scenario: US27-F23 AC27-F28-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F28, REQ27-F33, REQ27-F36.

- **GIVEN** a backup preceding revocation, access loss or privacy disposal.

- **WHEN** restoring then attempting affected reads, decisions and workers.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Restore a backup predating review revocation/access loss/disposal. Reconcile current fences before affected readers/workers resume; erased content and ended review do not revive. Roll-forward preserves accepted new data.

#### Scenario: US27-F23 AC27-F25-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F25, REQ27-F27, REQ27-F34.

- **GIVEN** native qualified owner contracts and unavailable Studio/external approval vendors.

- **WHEN** completing preparation, required review, permitted prospective action and actual response.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Native prepare→required review→permitted prospective action→actual response works with P34/P42 and external approval vendors unavailable; no CMS lane or mandatory second inbox is required.

#### Scenario: US27-F23 AC27-F29-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F29, REQ27-F30, REQ27-F35, REQ27-F37.

- **GIVEN** an admitted numeric tenant/request/history and concurrent-policy workload.

- **WHEN** measuring percentile latency, lock waits, RLS query cost and recovery backlog.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Record numeric Tenant/request/history sizes, largest-Tenant skew, concurrent decisions/policy edits, pagination and low-bandwidth profile; measure p50/p95/p99, lock waits, RLS queries and recovery backlog against named agreed release budgets. Required paths pass at the admitted envelope; no unmeasured capacity claim.

### Requirement: US27-F24 — Add eligible people or organizations to a recorded working list and use filters only for discovery

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an appeal organizer, I want to add eligible people or organizations to a recorded working list and use filters only for discovery, so that the plan stays deliberate when a query changes. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F38, REQ27-F39, REQ27-F40, REQ27-F48, REQ27-F51, REQ27-F52, REQ27-F41.

#### Scenario: US27-F24 AC27-F38-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F38, REQ27-F39, REQ27-F40, REQ27-F48, REQ27-F51, REQ27-F52.

- **GIVEN** an authorized open appeal and eligible person/organization Parties.

- **WHEN** adding them individually without an email, financial gift or prior journey.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Add a permitted person and organization individually: one planned entry each, no contact expansion, request, journey, gift, message, approval or automatic per-row task. Initial empty view is understandable.

#### Scenario: US27-F24 AC27-F38-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F38, REQ27-F41.

- **GIVEN** an accepted working list derived from a saved filter.

- **WHEN** editing, deleting or refreshing that filter.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: A saved-filter change never adds/removes an accepted entry. New discovery may show candidates, but does not resume excluded/removed work or alter actual outcomes.

### Requirement: US27-F25 — Review and capture a bounded finite group with explicit page versus all-results scope

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an appeal organizer, I want to review and capture a bounded finite group with explicit page versus all-results scope, so that large selections remain predictable and later matches cannot silently join the operation. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F38, REQ27-F44, REQ27-F51, REQ27-F53, REQ27-F45, REQ27-F46, REQ27-F55.

#### Scenario: US27-F25 AC27-F38-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F38, REQ27-F44, REQ27-F51, REQ27-F53.

- **GIVEN** a permitted multi-page candidate query and a finite reviewed selection.

- **WHEN** adding the selected group while later matches and page contents change.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Select current page versus all matching results across pagination/filter changes. The explicit server-captured identities are preserved despite browser cache eviction, later additions/removals or filter deletion.

#### Scenario: US27-F25 AC27-F44-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F44, REQ27-F45, REQ27-F46, REQ27-F53, REQ27-F55.

- **GIVEN** a recorded numeric volume/skew/batch/concurrency/permission/network envelope.

- **WHEN** measuring capture, per-member execution, queries, locks and recovery.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Declare numeric Tenant/Party/appeal/member/history volumes, largest-Tenant skew, group size, concurrent changes, role/permission shape and low-bandwidth profile. Measure p50/p95/p99, scans/locks, capture and recovery against named budgets; no silent truncation or unqualified higher capacity claim.

### Requirement: US27-F26 — Receive durable per-member outcomes and safely retry or cancel the same operation

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an appeal organizer, I want to receive durable per-member outcomes and safely retry or cancel the same operation, so that duplicate additions and lost replies cannot restore a later removal or exclusion. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F40, REQ27-F42, REQ27-F44, REQ27-F45, REQ27-F46, REQ27-F41, REQ27-F51.

#### Scenario: US27-F26 AC27-F40-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F40, REQ27-F42, REQ27-F44, REQ27-F45, REQ27-F46.

- **GIVEN** duplicate candidate IDs and an existing visible or hidden membership.

- **WHEN** executing simultaneous and repeated Add operations.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Duplicate candidate IDs, concurrent same-Party Add and already-in-list Add create at most one membership. Hidden existing membership produces no existence leak or workaround duplicate.

#### Scenario: US27-F26 AC27-F41-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F41, REQ27-F44, REQ27-F45, REQ27-F46.

- **GIVEN** competing Add, Exclude and current-head changes.

- **WHEN** executing controlled schedules, exact replay and changed-payload key reuse.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Controlled concurrent inclusion/exclusion in both orders, competing current-head changes, same-intent duplicates and changed payload under same operation key have one coherent outcome; exclusion cannot be overwritten by stale Add.

#### Scenario: US27-F26 AC27-F44-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F44, REQ27-F45, REQ27-F46, REQ27-F51.

- **GIVEN** some accepted group members, a lost reply and a later removal.

- **WHEN** retrying the original operation or cancelling the remaining work.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Lose response after accepted group items, then remove one entry and retry the original operation. It returns permitted original results without re-adding, recapturing or overwriting newer state. Cancellation stops future unaccepted items, not completed ones.

### Requirement: US27-F27 — See current eligibility, restricted or unavailable coverage and genuine empty states distinctly

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an appeal organizer, I want to see current eligibility, restricted or unavailable coverage and genuine empty states distinctly, so that I do not mistake a failed load or protected list for no planned supporters. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F42, REQ27-F43, REQ27-F50, REQ27-F51, REQ27-F53.

#### Scenario: US27-F27 AC27-F42-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F42, REQ27-F43, REQ27-F50, REQ27-F51.

- **GIVEN** denied or unknown planning authority and an existing later-restricted entry.

- **WHEN** attempting new inclusion and reading retained membership.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: New inclusion fails safely when planning authority/eligibility is denied or unknown. An existing retained entry under later restriction is shown only within current rights and cannot authorize outreach; loading/error is not zero.

#### Scenario: US27-F27 AC27-F42-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F42, REQ27-F43, REQ27-F51, REQ27-F53.

- **GIVEN** genuinely empty, all-removed, all-restricted, wholly hidden and failed-query cases.

- **WHEN** opening the working list and its counts/actions.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: All entries removed, all currently restricted, no permitted entries, failed query and genuinely zero entries yield distinct truthful views/counts/actions within disclosure limits. No forced owner task for historical-only entries.

### Requirement: US27-F28 — Deliberately remove, exclude or return a supporter with clear consequences

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized appeal staff member, I want to deliberately remove, exclude or return a supporter with clear consequences, so that planning can change without erasing history or silently restarting completed outreach. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F39, REQ27-F41, REQ27-F43, REQ27-F48, REQ27-F42, REQ27-F46, REQ27-F47.

#### Scenario: US27-F28 AC27-F39-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F39, REQ27-F41, REQ27-F43, REQ27-F48.

- **GIVEN** an included Party and an eligible candidate never previously included.

- **WHEN** removing the former and excluding either from the appeal.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Remove preserves lawful requests/responses and history. Exclude records an appeal-specific restriction, including an eligible-to-record candidate not previously included. Neither changes global consent or records a donor decline.

#### Scenario: US27-F28 AC27-F41-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F41, REQ27-F42, REQ27-F43, REQ27-F46, REQ27-F47, REQ27-F48.

- **GIVEN** removed/excluded membership with completed tasks and messages.

- **WHEN** trying ordinary Add/import/retry, then deliberate authorized Return.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Plain Add/import/retry skips removed/excluded entries. Explicit Return shows previous disposition, checks current expected head and eligibility, and restores planning only; no ended tasks/reviews/messages revive.

### Requirement: US27-F29 — Retain and record real Requests, responses and independent obligations after list changes

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a development staff member, I want to retain and record real Requests, responses and independent obligations after list changes, so that a changed plan does not delete actual history or cancel unrelated required work. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F39, REQ27-F48, REQ27-F47, REQ27-F52.

#### Scenario: US27-F29 AC27-F39-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F39, REQ27-F48.

- **GIVEN** removed, excluded or stopped appeal work and a truthful late reply.

- **WHEN** recording the actual past Request or response.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Record real late/past request or response after removal/exclusion, including stopped work, without re-inclusion, fabricated Asked/approval or automatic contact.

#### Scenario: US27-F29 AC27-F39-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F39, REQ27-F47, REQ27-F48, REQ27-F52.

- **GIVEN** a frozen send population plus independent receipt, service and fulfillment work.

- **WHEN** excluding a member, later returning it and trying purpose relabelling.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Source exclusion narrows a captured P32 send without adding replacements. Independently required responses/receipts/service or fulfillment remain under their owners, even with an appeal link. A later Return does not resume the ended solicitation occurrence; relabelling that purpose cannot bypass exclusion.

### Requirement: US27-F30 — Enforce current membership-dependent solicitation restrictions at dispatch admission

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a communication operations owner, I want to enforce current membership-dependent solicitation restrictions at dispatch admission, so that removal stops unreleased affected work while in-flight and uncertain provider outcomes stay truthful. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F43, REQ27-F45, REQ27-F46, REQ27-F47, REQ27-F52, REQ27-F53.

#### Scenario: US27-F30 AC27-F43-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F43, REQ27-F45, REQ27-F46, REQ27-F47.

- **GIVEN** membership-dependent solicitation waiting for dispatch admission.

- **WHEN** racing Remove/Exclude against admission in both orders with secondary projection stalled.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Remove/exclude and membership-dependent solicitation admission race in both orders: earlier accepted invalidation blocks later admission even with the secondary projection worker unavailable; already-dispatching/submitted/indeterminate work remains truthful and reconciles. No recall claim.

#### Scenario: US27-F30 AC27-F45-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F45, REQ27-F46, REQ27-F47, REQ27-F52, REQ27-F53.

- **GIVEN** accepted membership and duplicate or reordered worker events.

- **WHEN** delivering secondary callbacks and recovering an uncertain provider result.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Delayed/duplicate/out-of-order worker and projection events preserve membership and exact effect identity. Failure of a secondary projection cannot falsify acceptance; provider uncertainty cannot trigger another send or list revival.

### Requirement: US27-F31 — Retain distinct organization and human identities and resolve only affected current membership collisions

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a qualified identity steward, I want to retain distinct organization and human identities and resolve only affected current membership collisions, so that contact changes or merges cannot bypass exclusions, double-count or freeze unrelated appeal work. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F39, REQ27-F40, REQ27-F42, REQ27-F43, REQ27-F46, REQ27-F49, REQ27-F41, REQ27-F50, REQ27-F54.

#### Scenario: US27-F31 AC27-F39-04

**Requirements:** REQ27-F39, REQ27-F40, REQ27-F42, REQ27-F43.

- **GIVEN** a church and distinct pastor, spouse, household, advisor and donor identities.

- **WHEN** selecting contacts and applying an organization appeal exclusion.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Church, pastor, household, spouse, legal donor and advisor identities are not conflated by email/address/name. Changing human contact cannot bypass the same organization's appeal exclusion; unrelated personal scope is independently evaluated.

#### Scenario: US27-F31 AC27-F40-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F40, REQ27-F46, REQ27-F49.

- **GIVEN** two source Parties with compatible or conflicting current appeal intentions.

- **WHEN** performing an owner-qualified merge.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Qualified Party merge of duplicate compatible membership avoids double counting and retains both origins; conflicting current inclusion/exclusion/removal becomes a bounded member conflict, not actionable survivor-wins or whole-appeal freeze.

#### Scenario: US27-F31 AC27-F41-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F41, REQ27-F43, REQ27-F49, REQ27-F50, REQ27-F54.

- **GIVEN** merged membership with later Return, exclusion, access change or disposal.

- **WHEN** unmerging or purging an allowed source shell.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Unmerge after later Return/exclusion/access change and source-shell purge safeguards preserve current restrictions/history. An old removal superseded by valid Return is not misread as an eternal exclusion.

### Requirement: US27-F32 — Enforce current scope through discovery, capture, mutation, results and exports

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a tenant privacy administrator, I want to enforce current scope through discovery, capture, mutation, results and exports, so that foreign references, revoked access and hidden uniqueness conflicts cannot reveal protected membership. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F40, REQ27-F41, REQ27-F42, REQ27-F43, REQ27-F45, REQ27-F50, REQ27-F44, REQ27-F46, REQ27-F51.

#### Scenario: US27-F32 AC27-F40-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F40, REQ27-F41, REQ27-F42, REQ27-F43, REQ27-F45, REQ27-F50.

- **GIVEN** actual nonowner and privileged command/database paths.

- **WHEN** attempting tenant/appeal/Party substitutions and forged provenance or forbidden new state.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Actual Tenant/appeal/Party substitution, forbidden resulting-row mutation, forged actor/provenance and capability-only/role-label shortcuts fail across API, RLS, functions, views and privileged writers without partial change or existence leak.

#### Scenario: US27-F32 AC27-F42-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F42, REQ27-F43, REQ27-F44, REQ27-F45, REQ27-F46, REQ27-F50.

- **GIVEN** a captured group whose actor or target authority changes.

- **WHEN** racing revocation against capture and each acceptance across tenant assignments.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Race current permission/suppression loss with capture and each member acceptance in both orders. Prior valid acceptance remains history; later unauthorized effects fail. Same user in another Tenant assignment cannot reuse its authority/results.

#### Scenario: US27-F32 AC27-F42-04

**Requirements:** REQ27-F42, REQ27-F50, REQ27-F51.

- **GIVEN** restricted reasons and records plus exportable untrusted content.

- **WHEN** reading counts, differences, caches, replay and exports after current rights change.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Counts/filter facets/difference queries, exports, caches, late responses after context switch, logs and result receipts respect row/field/purpose floors. Test formula/script injection, protected reasons and revoked export release/download.

### Requirement: US27-F33 — Understand selection, restrictions, disposition and recovery in the actual shared interface

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an appeal organizer using keyboard, mobile or assistive technology, I want to understand selection, restrictions, disposition and recovery in the actual shared interface, so that I can change the plan accurately without hidden off-page actions or misleading recall language. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F51, REQ27-F50, REQ27-F55.

#### Scenario: US27-F33 AC27-F51-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F51.

- **GIVEN** a multi-page selectable list with mixed states and restricted rows.

- **WHEN** using only keyboard and screen reader through Add/Remove/Exclude/Return.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Keyboard/screen reader can discover selection scope, mixed selection, current restrictions and Add/Remove/Exclude/Return; focus and announced results remain useful as rows leave a filtered view.

#### Scenario: US27-F33 AC27-F50-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F50, REQ27-F51.

- **GIVEN** the actual mobile list with long translated/RTL names and poor connectivity.

- **WHEN** testing reflow, contrast, reduced motion, reconnect and safe input recovery.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Real UI passes 320-CSS-pixel/equivalent zoom, RTL, long names/translations, contrast and reduced-motion tests. Slow network/reconnect preserves safe input/result recovery without default sensitive browser persistence.

#### Scenario: US27-F33 AC27-F51-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F51, REQ27-F55.

- **GIVEN** actual staff and mixed selection/disposition scenarios.

- **WHEN** observing whether they distinguish plan, Asked, Remove, Exclude and partial results.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Moderated real-staff scenarios verify understanding of planned versus asked, Remove versus Exclude, current restriction, selected scope and partial results. Any unsafe misconception is corrected and retested before expansion.

### Requirement: US27-F34 — Adopt and operate recorded membership with atomic protection, native independence and safe upgrade/restore

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a release and source operations owner, I want to adopt and operate recorded membership with atomic protection, native independence and safe upgrade/restore, so that accepted plans and restrictions survive failures without fabricated outreach or incompatible legacy writers. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F44, REQ27-F45, REQ27-F46, REQ27-F47, REQ27-F38, REQ27-F39, REQ27-F40, REQ27-F41, REQ27-F42, REQ27-F43, REQ27-F48, REQ27-F49, REQ27-F50, REQ27-F54, REQ27-F52.

#### Scenario: US27-F34 AC27-F44-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F44, REQ27-F45, REQ27-F46, REQ27-F47.

- **GIVEN** a finite group with independent member commits.

- **WHEN** failing mandatory member history, receipt or fence advancement and then a secondary projection.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Inject failure of required local history, receipt or producer-fence advancement after member preparation: none of that member is falsely accepted with protection missing. Independent successful members remain, unresolved ones are visible and safely recoverable. A permitted secondary projection failure after valid commit does not undo acceptance.

#### Scenario: US27-F34 AC27-F38-04

**Requirements:** REQ27-F38, REQ27-F39, REQ27-F40, REQ27-F41, REQ27-F42, REQ27-F43, REQ27-F48, REQ27-F49, REQ27-F50, REQ27-F54.

- **GIVEN** retained list tags, actual asks, historical planning evidence and imports.

- **WHEN** mapping canonical Parties and importing under current authority.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Imports/backfills distinguish evidenced prior planning from list tags/actual asks/consent, resolve canonical Parties under current authority, and never create current outreach or approvals from historical labels.

#### Scenario: US27-F34 AC27-F40-04

**Requirements:** REQ27-F40, REQ27-F41, REQ27-F42, REQ27-F43, REQ27-F44, REQ27-F45, REQ27-F46, REQ27-F47, REQ27-F54.

- **GIVEN** old/new clients, jobs and schema during staged rollout.

- **WHEN** writing or disabling membership and consuming affected events.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Mixed old/new clients/jobs, additive migration and activation gates reject writers that cannot honor disposition/current-head/receipt semantics. Disabling mutation/consumer paths preserves history and restrictions.

#### Scenario: US27-F34 AC27-F47-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F47, REQ27-F49, REQ27-F50, REQ27-F54.

- **GIVEN** a backup older than exclusion, revocation, merge and disposal.

- **WHEN** restoring and attempting reads or outward worker admission.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Restore a backup before later exclusions, revocation, merge or disposal. Affected readers/workers remain fenced until reconciled; no erased content or ended outreach returns. Roll-forward preserves newly accepted data.

#### Scenario: US27-F34 AC27-F38-05

**Requirements:** REQ27-F38, REQ27-F39, REQ27-F52.

- **GIVEN** Studio and external campaign providers unavailable.

- **WHEN** performing native planning and attempting external-list or saved-view synchronization writes.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Native appeal planning works with P34/P42 and external campaign providers unavailable. A saved view, giving campaign, workflow or provider list cannot write appeal membership by synchronization convention.

### Requirement: US27-F35 — Record an attributed actual offline receipt separately from promises, dates and assigned responsibility

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized gift reporter, I want to record an attributed actual offline receipt separately from promises, dates and assigned responsibility, so that finance can act on truthful reported evidence without claiming verified continuous custody. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F56, REQ27-F57, REQ27-F58, REQ27-F59, REQ27-F65, REQ27-F71, REQ27-F60, REQ27-F63.

#### Scenario: US27-F35 AC27-F56-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F56, REQ27-F57, REQ27-F58, REQ27-F59, REQ27-F65, REQ27-F71.

- **GIVEN** a report-only authorized actor with an actually received check.

- **WHEN** saving one receipt report.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Report an actually received check with a report-only actor: one qualified intake identity and required next work; no contribution, donor creation, receipt, message, journey or automatic fulfillment.

#### Scenario: US27-F35 AC27-F57-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F57, REQ27-F59, REQ27-F60, REQ27-F63.

- **GIVEN** a promise, ordinary draft, standalone received date and report-on-behalf fixture.

- **WHEN** changing responsibility and correcting the report date.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Promise of an incoming check, generic draft, received-date field, responsible-person change and report-on-behalf do not fabricate present possession or impersonate the recorder. Correct reported date without changing tax date.

### Requirement: US27-F36 — Retain incomplete or wrong-payee intake with an owned exception

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized gift reporter, I want to retain incomplete or wrong-payee intake with an owned exception, so that missing donor, entity or amount does not force a guessed identity or invalid financial row. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F58, REQ27-F59, REQ27-F60, REQ27-F68.

#### Scenario: US27-F36 AC27-F58-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F58, REQ27-F59, REQ27-F60, REQ27-F68.

- **GIVEN** actual receipt with unknown donor, amount, intended entity or wrong payee.

- **WHEN** recording and resolving bounded intake.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Unknown donor/amount/intended entity and wrong-payee receipt retain truthful qualified source intake/accountability; no fake identity/default currency/nullable financial-root workaround or cross-Tenant transfer.

### Requirement: US27-F37 — Continue the same reported intake using the existing finance entry and validation flow

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a finance staff member, I want to continue the same reported intake using the existing finance entry and validation flow, so that staff do not enter a second gift or lose the known source context. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F56, REQ27-F59, REQ27-F61, REQ27-F70, REQ27-F57, REQ27-F58, REQ27-F63.

#### Scenario: US27-F37 AC27-F56-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F56, REQ27-F59, REQ27-F61, REQ27-F70.

- **GIVEN** a qualified reported intake with permitted known details.

- **WHEN** continuing into normal finance entry and validation.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Finance continues the same reported intake with known permitted information and existing validations; no second gift data entry or P27 writer.

#### Scenario: US27-F37 AC27-F57-04

**Requirements:** REQ27-F57, REQ27-F58, REQ27-F59, REQ27-F63, REQ27-F70.

- **GIVEN** report-only and finance actors with a shared intake and an exception.

- **WHEN** completing report, continuation, restricted completion viewing and correction.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Ordinary staff report, finance continuation, restricted completion view and exception correction show known facts, next actor and permitted action without duplicate entry, dead links, mandatory extra approval or confusing finance controls.

### Requirement: US27-F38 — Preserve one canonical actual intake across appeal references and qualified duplicate-report resolution

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an intake steward, I want to preserve one canonical actual intake across appeal references and qualified duplicate-report resolution, so that the same check is not multiplied while truly separate checks remain distinguishable. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F61, REQ27-F62, REQ27-F63, REQ27-F68, REQ27-F60, REQ27-F64, REQ27-F67.

#### Scenario: US27-F38 AC27-F61-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F61, REQ27-F62, REQ27-F63, REQ27-F68.

- **GIVEN** one intake linked to two appeals and several designations, plus a distinct check.

- **WHEN** reading and processing all qualified references.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Exact same source item is visible through two authorized appeals and several designation lines without duplicate intake, work, contributions or additive money. Distinct checks remain separate.

#### Scenario: US27-F38 AC27-F60-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F60, REQ27-F61, REQ27-F62, REQ27-F64, REQ27-F67.

- **GIVEN** same-intent report retries and two independent reports of one actual item.

- **WHEN** replaying the intent and separately reconciling a proved duplicate.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Two independent reports about one item and repeated same-intent submissions have qualified distinct recovery: exact replay is idempotent; confirmed duplicates preserve both origins and resolve one canonical intake without auto-merging by amount/name/check number.

### Requirement: US27-F39 — Resolve recording only from exact covered source lineage, including an already-recorded gift

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized finance reconciler, I want to resolve recording only from exact covered source lineage, including an already-recorded gift, so that similar amounts or a missing projection cannot trigger a second posting, receipt or fulfillment application. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F59, REQ27-F60, REQ27-F61, REQ27-F62, REQ27-F68, REQ27-F63, REQ27-F64, REQ27-F67.

#### Scenario: US27-F39 AC27-F59-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F59, REQ27-F60, REQ27-F61, REQ27-F62, REQ27-F68.

- **GIVEN** exact, wrong-source, foreign-scope, partial and withheld completion candidates.

- **WHEN** resolving awaiting-recording status.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Only exact owner-confirmed covered posting references resolve the same intake. Wrong source/tenant/entity, generic draft and equal-amount recurring gift do not; missing/withheld/partial source coverage is not complete or zero.

#### Scenario: US27-F39 AC27-F59-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F59, REQ27-F60, REQ27-F61, REQ27-F62, REQ27-F63, REQ27-F64, REQ27-F67.

- **GIVEN** an independently already-recorded contribution and the same actual intake.

- **WHEN** linking exact proved lineage and correcting an erroneous link.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: A known existing source-recorded gift is reconciled to this intake by authorized exact lineage; no new posting/receipt/P16 application. Correcting an erroneous link preserves its evidence and does not alter immutable money.

### Requirement: US27-F40 — See recording, physical handling and partial item completion as separate facts

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a responsible intake handler, I want to see recording, physical handling and partial item completion as separate facts, so that posting-first, transfer-first and several gifts from one supporter remain actionable without a misleading exclusive status. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F57, REQ27-F62, REQ27-F63, REQ27-F64, REQ27-F65, REQ27-F70, REQ27-F61, REQ27-F68.

#### Scenario: US27-F40 AC27-F57-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F57, REQ27-F62, REQ27-F63, REQ27-F64, REQ27-F65, REQ27-F70.

- **GIVEN** an item whose posting and physical transfer are independently possible.

- **WHEN** processing posting-first and transfer-first sequences.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Finance records before physical transfer: show recorded fact and any real remaining handling. Transfer before recording: show supported handling evidence and keep recording work pending. No forced posting/deposit order.

#### Scenario: US27-F40 AC27-F61-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F61, REQ27-F62, REQ27-F63, REQ27-F68, REQ27-F70.

- **GIVEN** several distinct items from one supporter.

- **WHEN** posting only a subset and reading the appeal.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Partial processing of multiple items preserves unresolved identities. Recorded and pending items from one supporter coexist without a mutually exclusive supporter bucket or loss of historical facts.

### Requirement: US27-F41 — Record truthful exceptions and preserve outstanding responsibility through financial correction, archive and appeal closure

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an intake steward, I want to record truthful exceptions and preserve outstanding responsibility through financial correction, archive and appeal closure, so that real property handling cannot silently disappear or reopen from an unrelated reversal. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F57, REQ27-F62, REQ27-F63, REQ27-F64, REQ27-F65, REQ27-F66, REQ27-F68, REQ27-F61, REQ27-F69, REQ27-F72.

#### Scenario: US27-F41 AC27-F57-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F57, REQ27-F62, REQ27-F63, REQ27-F64, REQ27-F65.

- **GIVEN** lost, returned, disputed, rejected or mistakenly reported intake.

- **WHEN** recording source dispositions and trying generic task Complete/Delete.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Lost, returned, disputed, rejected and mistakenly reported items get truthful authorized dispositions and remaining work. An exception disposition or generic task Complete/Delete cannot itself establish Recorded by finance or erase a previously source-confirmed recording. Preserve current owner-qualified financial status/history and any remaining handling.

#### Scenario: US27-F41 AC27-F62-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F62, REQ27-F63, REQ27-F64, REQ27-F66, REQ27-F68.

- **GIVEN** an intake whose linked gift was recorded earlier.

- **WHEN** applying an owner-qualified reversal, NSF or correction.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: P13 reversal/NSF/correction changes financial truth under its owner without reopening old physical possession, erasing original recording history or creating a replacement contribution.

#### Scenario: US27-F41 AC27-F61-06

**Requirements:** REQ27-F61, REQ27-F63, REQ27-F64, REQ27-F65, REQ27-F69, REQ27-F72.

- **GIVEN** unresolved physical/intake work and qualified exception dispositions.

- **WHEN** archiving/carrying drafts, closing appeal, removing members and anonymizing staff.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Draft deletion/archive/carry-forward, appeal closure/member removal, staff anonymization and delayed handling preserve lawful evidence and actual remaining responsibility; qualified final exception closure ends only applicable work.

### Requirement: US27-F42 — Separate report, source routing, financial and export powers on every admitted path

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a tenant privacy administrator, I want to separate report, source routing, financial and export powers on every admitted path, so that reporters, assignees and historical authors gain no extra financial or protected missionary access. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F58, REQ27-F59, REQ27-F60, REQ27-F67, REQ27-F68, REQ27-F69, REQ27-F65, REQ27-F66, REQ27-F70.

#### Scenario: US27-F42 AC27-F58-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F58, REQ27-F59, REQ27-F60, REQ27-F67, REQ27-F68, REQ27-F69.

- **GIVEN** report, finance, service and repair paths with two tenants/entities.

- **WHEN** attempting foreign reference/assignee substitution and forbidden resulting state.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Cross-Tenant/entity/appeal/source/assignee substitution and forbidden resulting-row mutation fail across real API/RLS/RPC/views/import/service/repair paths. Foreign/hidden references yield no partial effect or existence leak.

#### Scenario: US27-F42 AC27-F59-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F59, REQ27-F60, REQ27-F65, REQ27-F66, REQ27-F67, REQ27-F69.

- **GIVEN** reporter and finance identities with changing current eligibility.

- **WHEN** racing revocation against capture, reassignment, resolution and result read.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Permission/recipient eligibility loss races capture, reassignment, completion and result read in both orders. Current finance authority can continue after reporter departure, while reporter access and task/notification disclosure narrow immediately.

#### Scenario: US27-F42 AC27-F59-04

**Requirements:** REQ27-F59, REQ27-F60, REQ27-F69, REQ27-F70.

- **GIVEN** protected finance, missionary and handoff content.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Protected financial detail, bank/check content, missionary identity/location, counts, notes, logs, exports, caches and deep links obey current field/purpose floors. No image picker/public upload/sensitive browser persistence is introduced.

#### Scenario: US27-F42 AC27-F59-05

**Requirements:** REQ27-F59, REQ27-F60, REQ27-F65, REQ27-F69.

- **GIVEN** report-only, historical-author and task-assignee identities.

- **WHEN** reading minimal completion evidence and attempting full finance or export access.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Report-only/currently restricted actor sees only qualified handoff/completion facts, not full ledger/provider details; task assignee and historical author gain no new rights. Exports have separate current authorization.

### Requirement: US27-F43 — Retain atomic local results and exact lineage across source races, duplicate callbacks and lost responses

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a finance integration operator, I want to retain atomic local results and exact lineage across source races, duplicate callbacks and lost responses, so that accepted money remains intact and reconciliation never reposts to repair a consumer. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F61, REQ27-F62, REQ27-F66, REQ27-F67, REQ27-F63, REQ27-F64, REQ27-F72.

#### Scenario: US27-F43 AC27-F61-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F61, REQ27-F62, REQ27-F66, REQ27-F67.

- **GIVEN** a local report/resolution operation and an independently committed financial source.

- **WHEN** failing required evidence/receipt/work disposition or consumer processing.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Inject failure of required local evidence/receipt/work disposition after preparing a report/resolution: no half-accepted source mutation. Independently already-posted money survives a failed consumer; reconciliation never retries posting.

#### Scenario: US27-F43 AC27-F61-04

**Requirements:** REQ27-F61, REQ27-F62, REQ27-F66, REQ27-F67.

- **GIVEN** one accepted source operation with a lost reply and later correction.

- **WHEN** replaying concurrently, changing payload under its key and delivering late completion.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Lost accepted reply, concurrent exact replay, changed payload under one key, and late/out-of-order completion produce one durable effect or an explicit conflict; old results cannot reapply over later corrections.

#### Scenario: US27-F43 AC27-F61-05

**Requirements:** REQ27-F61, REQ27-F62, REQ27-F63, REQ27-F64, REQ27-F66, REQ27-F67.

- **GIVEN** material report correction and exact financial resolution in competing sessions.

- **WHEN** executing both orders and separately changing incidental note or responsibility.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Material handoff correction and exact source resolution race in both orders with controlled interleavings. Stale resolution cannot overwrite newer facts; accepted financial source history remains and unresolved correspondence is owned. Incidental note/display/assignment edits do not erase lineage or restart recording.

#### Scenario: US27-F43 AC27-F61-07

**Requirements:** REQ27-F61, REQ27-F62, REQ27-F63, REQ27-F66, REQ27-F67, REQ27-F72.

- **GIVEN** normal P15 commit with exact lineage and a delayed P27 projection.

- **WHEN** delivering duplicate and out-of-order callbacks.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Normal P15 commit and exact source lineage are coherent; source-recorded gift with delayed projection remains real. Duplicate/out-of-order integration/worker callbacks neither double-post nor falsely resolve unqualified coverage.

### Requirement: US27-F44 — Complete the short report and recovery flow with clear next actor and status

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a gift reporter using a phone or assistive technology, I want to complete the short report and recovery flow with clear next actor and status, so that I understand the difference between receipt, handling, assignment, recording and clearance without using the full entry grid. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F70, REQ27-F67, REQ27-F69, REQ27-F57, REQ27-F58, REQ27-F63, REQ27-F74.

- **GIVEN** the real reporting and resolution flow.

- **WHEN** operating by keyboard, screen reader and touch through save and recovery.

#### Scenario: US27-F44 AC27-F67-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F67, REQ27-F69, REQ27-F70.

- **GIVEN** small-screen report/status UI and the separately scoped finance grid.

- **WHEN** testing 320px/zoom, translated RTL names, reduced motion, disconnect and tenant switch.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Real small report/status/handoff browser flow passes 320-CSS-pixel/zoom, long names/translations/RTL, contrast/reduced-motion and low-bandwidth recovery, without expanding P15's full entry grid to phones. Closing or timing out does not pretend cancellation; context switch blocks late private responses.

#### Scenario: US27-F44 AC27-F57-06

**Requirements:** REQ27-F57, REQ27-F58, REQ27-F63, REQ27-F70, REQ27-F74.

- **GIVEN** actual report and finance users with same-item, unknown-detail and recovery scenarios.

- **WHEN** observing completion and their interpretation of possession, responsibility, posting and clearance.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Real staff scenarios distinguish reported receipt, present handling, responsibility, finance recording and clearance; complete same-item/unknown-details/exception/recovery flows without unsafe misconceptions. Correct and retest any such misconception before expansion.

### Requirement: US27-F45 — See handoff only for applicable qualified intake and continue ordinary work without unrelated optional products

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an appeal organizer, I want to see handoff only for applicable qualified intake and continue ordinary work without unrelated optional products, so that online gifts, prayer requests and ordinary members acquire no invented manual finance stage. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F57, REQ27-F63, REQ27-F68, REQ27-F71, REQ27-F56, REQ27-F65, REQ27-F69, REQ27-F72.

#### Scenario: US27-F45 AC27-F57-05

**Requirements:** REQ27-F57, REQ27-F63, REQ27-F68, REQ27-F71.

- **GIVEN** a direct online gift, a qualified noncash intake and a prayer/hosting Request.

- **WHEN** viewing the appeal and reporting applicable facts.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Direct online gift has no invented physical step. Applicable noncash intake follows acceptance/describe-never-value rules; prayer/hosting remains D22 nonfinancial participation. No tax/acknowledgment action follows a handoff alone.

#### Scenario: US27-F45 AC27-F56-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F56, REQ27-F65, REQ27-F69, REQ27-F71, REQ27-F72.

- **GIVEN** Studio/general files/optional providers unavailable and one unqualified finance capability.

- **WHEN** using qualified native intake and the affected dependent lane.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Qualified handoff works natively with Studio/P34/P42, general files and optional providers unavailable. Missing exact finance source capability blocks only the affected lane; no retired writer or shadow fallback is activated.

### Requirement: US27-F46 — Qualify the narrow intake extension, safe data adoption, measured recovery and restore before activation

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a P15 release and operations owner, I want to qualify the narrow intake extension, safe data adoption, measured recovery and restore before activation, so that accepted reports remain recoverable with named owners and no false production proof. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F64, REQ27-F67, REQ27-F69, REQ27-F72, REQ27-F60, REQ27-F61, REQ27-F62, REQ27-F66, REQ27-F73, REQ27-F65, REQ27-F70, REQ27-F57, REQ27-F63, REQ27-F56, REQ27-F74.

#### Scenario: US27-F46 AC27-F64-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F64, REQ27-F67, REQ27-F69, REQ27-F72.

- **GIVEN** a backup predating posting, routing, correction, disposition and disposal.

- **WHEN** restoring then attempting related reads, workers and posting.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Restore from before later posting, correction, routing/access loss, disposition or privacy disposal; affected reads/workers remain fenced until reconciled, with no resurrected custody, erased content or duplicate posting.

#### Scenario: US27-F46 AC27-F60-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F60, REQ27-F61, REQ27-F62, REQ27-F66, REQ27-F67, REQ27-F73.

- **GIVEN** a recorded numeric intake/history/fanout/role/concurrency workload.

- **WHEN** measuring real queries, commands, locks, pagination and recovery.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Declare numeric report/pending/history volumes, large-Tenant skew, source-reference fanout, concurrent operations, roles and query/command/recovery budgets; measure p50/p95/p99, plans/locks and permission-safe pagination.

#### Scenario: US27-F46 AC27-F64-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F64, REQ27-F65, REQ27-F66, REQ27-F67, REQ27-F70, REQ27-F73.

- **GIVEN** accepted source facts with stalled projections and exhausted retry budget.

- **WHEN** viewing status and using authorized recovery controls.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Stall work/projection/reconciliation and exhaust bounded retries; staff see accepted source truth separately from delayed processing, and the named source owner can recover without SQL repair or duplicate intake.

#### Scenario: US27-F46 AC27-F57-07

**Requirements:** REQ27-F57, REQ27-F60, REQ27-F61, REQ27-F62, REQ27-F63, REQ27-F64, REQ27-F72.

- **GIVEN** retained labels, drafts and notes plus mixed clients/jobs.

- **WHEN** censusing, migrating, enabling and rolling back after real new reports.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Retained-data census and mixed-client/job rollout do not convert old received labels/drafts/notes into present possession. Compatible readers/writers precede activation; rollback preserves accepted reports and qualified recovery for real property.

#### Scenario: US27-F46 AC27-F56-04

**Requirements:** REQ27-F56, REQ27-F72, REQ27-F73, REQ27-F74.

- **GIVEN** the complete owner extension and all report obligations.

- **WHEN** checking source-to-test-to-release traceability without substituting synthetic evidence.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: D25's exact P15 extension, glossary and REQ27-F56–REQ27-F74 with their acceptance criteria trace into owner formal requirements/design/tasks and real test/release evidence; no synthetic or dated research result is relabelled production qualification.

### Requirement: US27-F47 — Coordinate financial, significant nonfinancial or mixed support around one purpose with optional goals

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an appeal organizer, I want to coordinate financial, significant nonfinancial or mixed support around one purpose with optional goals, so that the product supports meaningful ministry work without a monetary target or unrelated setup. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F75, REQ27-F87, REQ27-F88.

#### Scenario: US27-F47 AC27-F75-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F75, REQ27-F87.

- **GIVEN** an authorized development staff actor.
