- **WHEN** creating financial-only, nonfinancial-only and mixed appeals with narrative purpose and no numeric goal.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Create/read financial-only, nonfinancial-only and mixed appeals on the same model with a narrative purpose and no numerical goal; irrelevant money inputs are absent.

#### Scenario: US27-F47 AC27-F75-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F75, REQ27-F88.

- **GIVEN** ordinary individual Requests/tasks and unavailable optional products.

- **WHEN** working with and without an appeal.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Ordinary individual requests/tasks need no appeal; an appeal requires no event, prayer-content, Studio, public campaign or rating setup.

### Requirement: US27-F48 — See and correct each specific Request and response within a mixed appeal

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a development staff member, I want to see and correct each specific Request and response within a mixed appeal, so that conditional interest, contradictory contact replies and different invitations cannot collapse into one church-wide outcome. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F76, REQ27-F87, REQ27-F86.

#### Scenario: US27-F48 AC27-F76-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F76, REQ27-F87.

- **GIVEN** one church with hosting, funding and prayer Requests.

- **WHEN** recording agreed hosting, declined funding and no prayer reply.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: One church has hosting agreed, funding declined and prayer unanswered; each response and next work remains clear and independently editable.

#### Scenario: US27-F48 AC27-F76-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F76, REQ27-F86.

- **GIVEN** an attributed partial or conditional answer from a known-capacity representative.

- **WHEN** recording its exact terms and viewing response versus execution.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Conditional/partial responses preserve their exact terms and known speaker capacity without implying final booking, commitment or participation.

#### Scenario: US27-F48 AC27-F76-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F76, REQ27-F86, REQ27-F87.

- **GIVEN** an already-presented invitation with reply and date-only or unknown occurrence.

- **WHEN** saving both facts together.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Record an already-presented Request and reply in one save, with honest unknown/date-only precision and no fictional intermediate history.

#### Scenario: US27-F48 AC27-F76-04

**Requirements:** REQ27-F76, REQ27-F86.

- **GIVEN** earlier presented terms and conflicting replies by different contacts.

- **WHEN** revising or correcting terms and determining current effective response.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Revising or correcting terms preserves prior presented terms and answers; no-answer, declined, deferred and actual outcome remain distinguishable. Contradictory contact replies require source-qualified clarification, not last-write-wins organizational agreement.

### Requirement: US27-F49 — Deliberately reference the same relevant Request from multiple appeals

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an appeal organizer, I want to deliberately reference the same relevant Request from multiple appeals, so that coordination preserves one source history and work occurrence without copying records or backdating attribution. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F77, REQ27-F83, REQ27-F85, REQ27-F86.

#### Scenario: US27-F49 AC27-F77-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F77, REQ27-F83, REQ27-F85.

- **GIVEN** one canonical Request and two eligible appeals.

- **WHEN** linking the Request to both and replaying those link operations.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Link one Request into two eligible appeals; retries do not duplicate references, Request history or source work. Both authorized views show the same current source fact.

#### Scenario: US27-F49 AC27-F77-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F77, REQ27-F86.

- **GIVEN** a historical Request reference and current independent work.

- **WHEN** unlinking, relinking and adding the old Request to a new appeal.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Unlink/relink preserves lawful previous association history; no membership, response, money, ownership or task state changes merely because of the link. Linking an old Request does not attribute its earlier presentation to the new appeal.

### Requirement: US27-F50 — Read explicit-grain current and historical measures with exact source money and denominator coverage

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a development analyst, I want to read explicit-grain current and historical measures with exact source money and denominator coverage, so that overlapping appeals, goal edits, member removal, source correction or paginated data cannot inflate or rewrite results. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F78, REQ27-F79, REQ27-F88, REQ27-F86.

#### Scenario: US27-F50 AC27-F78-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F78, REQ27-F79, REQ27-F88.

- **GIVEN** more than 5,000 qualified gift lines with overlapping appeals, duplicate joins and separate currency/recognition lanes.

- **WHEN** reading exact totals with complete and intentionally truncated coverage.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Exact source-set totals stay correct across duplicate joins, two appeals, currency lanes, recognition roles and more than 5,000 qualifying gifts; truncated input reports incomplete coverage.

#### Scenario: US27-F50 AC27-F78-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F78, REQ27-F79, REQ27-F86.

- **GIVEN** current and historical views with merge, refund, unlink, revision, membership removal and goal revision.

- **WHEN** rebuilding measures including over-target and denied/incomplete denominators.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Party merge, source refund/correction, retained historical unlink and request revision update the correct current read model while preserving lawful earlier facts; no guessed money allocation. Removing members or revising a goal cannot rewrite historical response rates; over-target, no-target and denied/incomplete-denominator cases remain truthful.

### Requirement: US27-F51 — Close or reopen coordination deliberately while preserving independent work and late facts

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an appeal organizer, I want to close or reopen coordination deliberately while preserving independent work and late facts, so that finishing an effort cannot erase genuine obligations or restart ended outreach. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F84, REQ27-F85, REQ27-F86, REQ27-F83.

#### Scenario: US27-F51 AC27-F84-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F84, REQ27-F85, REQ27-F86.

- **GIVEN** removed/excluded or closed work with a late unsolicited reply or gift.

- **WHEN** recording currently authorized source facts.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: A late unsolicited response or gift after removal/closure is recordable under current authority; no membership restoration, outreach or ended-task revival occurs.

#### Scenario: US27-F51 AC27-F83-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F83, REQ27-F84.

- **GIVEN** an open appeal with legitimate hosting and P15 intake obligations outstanding.

- **WHEN** closing appeal coordination.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Close with outstanding legitimate hosting/finance work: continuation has an eligible owner/next action or owned exception; only legally ended appeal work ends.

#### Scenario: US27-F51 AC27-F84-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F84.

- **GIVEN** a closed appeal, an open appeal with its last member removed and a reached goal.

- **WHEN** reopening deliberately or changing those unrelated conditions.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Reopening keeps the earlier closure and requires deliberate fresh work; last-member removal and goal attainment never auto-close or reopen.

### Requirement: US27-F52 — Use clear list and Request-level board controls with lossless relevant fields

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an appeal user, I want to use clear list and Request-level board controls with lossless relevant fields, so that selection and display changes cannot fabricate responses, financial facts or silent hidden-value submission. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F85, REQ27-F87, REQ27-F79, REQ27-F86, REQ27-F76.

#### Scenario: US27-F52 AC27-F85-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F85, REQ27-F87.

- **GIVEN** a paginated list with an all-matching selection.

- **WHEN** reviewing and acting through keyboard/touch while filters change.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Keyboard/touch users can select a page or a finite all-matching population with accurate labels and review; changing filters cannot silently substitute the selected operation.

#### Scenario: US27-F52 AC27-F79-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F79, REQ27-F86, REQ27-F87.

- **GIVEN** financial terms entered unsaved and saved qualified financial facts.

- **WHEN** changing applicability, purpose, links or archive visibility.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Hidden financial inputs never submit after an unsaved applicability change; saved source financial facts survive purpose changes, unlink and archive.

#### Scenario: US27-F52 AC27-F76-05

**Requirements:** REQ27-F76, REQ27-F79, REQ27-F87.

- **GIVEN** a Request-level board displaying responses and source financial facts.

- **WHEN** moving cards or using equivalent explicit actions.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Board moves cannot create presentation, supporter reply, money, fulfillment or booking; an explicit permitted action and its source validation own any change.

### Requirement: US27-F53 — Keep appeal, child Request, shared-update and optional-rating access independent

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a tenant privacy administrator, I want to keep appeal, child Request, shared-update and optional-rating access independent, so that coordination grants no financial, missionary, private nonfinancial or public disclosure rights. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F80, REQ27-F81, REQ27-F78, REQ27-F82, REQ27-F85.

#### Scenario: US27-F53 AC27-F80-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F80, REQ27-F81.

- **GIVEN** two tenants and actual API, database, import, job and privileged paths.

- **WHEN** substituting Request/member/owner/source IDs or replaying a switched-tenant draft.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Cross-tenant Request/member/owner/source IDs fail through API, direct admitted DB role, RPC, import, job and privileged path; tenant-switch draft replay fails.

#### Scenario: US27-F53 AC27-F78-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F78, REQ27-F80, REQ27-F81, REQ27-F82.

- **GIVEN** appeal-readable but child-finance or protected nonfinancial-denied identities.

- **WHEN** opening detail/search/count/sort/export/cache and brief or AI projections.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Appeal visibility alone reveals no unauthorized child fields, finance or protected prayer/location data through detail, search, count, sort, export, cache or AI/brief.

#### Scenario: US27-F53 AC27-F80-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F80, REQ27-F81, REQ27-F82, REQ27-F85.

- **GIVEN** a lawful preview followed by authority loss.

- **WHEN** saving, exporting, downloading or dispatching after revocation.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Current revocation between preview and save/export/download/dispatch narrows output or rejects the action without leaking protected existence. Test effective old/new-row policies and constraint errors.

#### Scenario: US27-F53 AC27-F80-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F80, REQ27-F82.

- **GIVEN** missionary, donor and public actors plus D26 shared-update and D28 Off fixtures.

- **WHEN** opening appeal and derived projections.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Missionary/donor/public roles gain no access from appeal membership; D26 shared update remains narrowly scoped and D28 Off does not leak ratings.

### Requirement: US27-F54 — Apply source mutations, closure and dispatch fences in a defined idempotent order

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a source operations owner, I want to apply source mutations, closure and dispatch fences in a defined idempotent order, so that concurrent edits, uncertain responses and alternate appeal references cannot bypass restrictions or lose facts. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F85, REQ27-F90, REQ27-F80, REQ27-F84, REQ27-F86.

#### Scenario: US27-F54 AC27-F85-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F85, REQ27-F90.

- **GIVEN** one source response change with required history and effect intent.

- **WHEN** injecting precommit and postcommit projection failures.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Source-write/history/effect-intent fault injection proves all-or-none source commit; after-commit projection failure preserves response and allows idempotent repair.

#### Scenario: US27-F54 AC27-F80-04

**Requirements:** REQ27-F80, REQ27-F84, REQ27-F85.

- **GIVEN** appeal-originated queued solicitation and changing closure/exclusion/authority.

- **WHEN** racing current fences against dispatch admission and trying an alternate appeal.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Race close/exclude/revoke against source dispatch admission; permitted ordering is explicit, admitted work is reported honestly and a different appeal cannot bypass the fence.

#### Scenario: US27-F54 AC27-F85-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F85, REQ27-F86.

- **GIVEN** competing current replies, terms and links plus a stale pre-reopen editor.

- **WHEN** saving in controlled concurrent orders.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Concurrent replies/terms/link changes and an old form after reopen produce a valid source result or a clear conflict; no lost update or duplicate business effect.

#### Scenario: US27-F54 AC27-F85-05

**Requirements:** REQ27-F85, REQ27-F86, REQ27-F90.

- **GIVEN** duplicated/reordered callbacks, lost acknowledgments, partial bulk cancellation and uncertain external outcome.

- **WHEN** reconciling source operations.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Duplicate/out-of-order callbacks, lost acknowledgments, partial bulk cancellation and ambiguous provider outcomes converge through exact source identity with truthful per-member results.

### Requirement: US27-F55 — See only genuine source work once across coordination views

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a partnership staff member, I want to see only genuine source work once across coordination views, so that links, list inclusion or ongoing prayer agreement do not create duplicate or perpetual obligations. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F83, REQ27-F85, REQ27-F75.

#### Scenario: US27-F55 AC27-F83-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F83, REQ27-F85.

- **GIVEN** one source work occurrence visible through native and optional workflows.

- **WHEN** delivering duplicate effects and completing only one view's action.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Native and optional workflow delivery for the same source occurrence converge on one task/effect; claiming or completing a view does not complete another obligation.

#### Scenario: US27-F55 AC27-F75-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F75, REQ27-F83.

- **GIVEN** an ongoing prayer agreement, new membership and a new appeal link.

- **WHEN** saving each and inspecting generated work.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Ongoing prayer agreement, audience addition and appeal link create no mandatory recurring check-in; real independent work remains visible in the shared Tasks Hub.

### Requirement: US27-F56 — Complete the actual create, link, respond, close and recover journey with understandable states

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an appeal user on mobile or assistive technology, I want to complete the actual create, link, respond, close and recover journey with understandable states, so that I can act confidently on long translated content or slow connections without hidden outcomes. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F87, REQ27-F90, REQ27-F85, REQ27-F88.

#### Scenario: US27-F56 AC27-F87-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F87.

- **GIVEN** the composed create/list/link/respond/close/recover UI.

- **WHEN** performing manual and automated accessibility checks.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Manual keyboard, focus restoration, screen-reader names/state/announcements and automated accessibility checks pass for create/list/link/respond/close/recover flows.

#### Scenario: US27-F56 AC27-F87-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F87.

- **GIVEN** 320px/zoom layouts, long Unicode names, translated RTL text and both themes.

- **WHEN** reading and operating the complete appeal flow.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: At 320 CSS px and applicable WCAG reflow/zoom, long Unicode/translatable names, RTL text and statuses remain readable; no mandatory surname or forced address. Touch targets and light/dark contrast meet WCAG 2.2 AA; existing reduced-motion behavior remains intact.

#### Scenario: US27-F56 AC27-F87-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F87, REQ27-F90.

- **GIVEN** representative staff using financial, prayer-only and mixed scenarios.

- **WHEN** observing response correction and understanding of work continuing after closure.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Representative staff complete financial, prayer-only and mixed-response scenarios, find/correct the relevant Request and identify what continues after closure. Any critical meaning, access or action error blocks pilot acceptance.

#### Scenario: US27-F56 AC27-F85-04

**Requirements:** REQ27-F85, REQ27-F87, REQ27-F88.

- **GIVEN** throttled mobile network and independently unavailable optional panels.

- **WHEN** loading, editing, saving and recovering a lost Request response.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: On throttled mobile conditions, core context loads independently of optional panels; failure preserves authorized input, announces state and permits safe retry with no duplicate Request.

### Requirement: US27-F57 — Keep ordinary nonfinancial work usable while qualifying source-dependent financial views against measured bounds

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a product and operations owner, I want to keep ordinary nonfinancial work usable while qualifying source-dependent financial views against measured bounds, so that optional outages and unknown financial coverage do not become false zero or stop unrelated safe work. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F79, REQ27-F88, REQ27-F90.

#### Scenario: US27-F57 AC27-F79-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F79, REQ27-F88.

- **GIVEN** optional services off and qualified finance temporarily unavailable.

- **WHEN** doing ordinary nonfinancial work and reading dependent financial totals.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: With optional services off and qualified finance temporarily unavailable, ordinary nonfinancial work succeeds; dependent totals visibly report unavailable/updating instead of zero.

#### Scenario: US27-F57 AC27-F88-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F88, REQ27-F90.

- **GIVEN** a documented numeric tenant/appeal/request/concurrency workload and freshness budget.

- **WHEN** qualifying real query, RLS, index, latency and error behavior.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Qualify documented tenant/appeal/request cardinalities, largest tenant, concurrent staff/bulk load, p95 latency/error limits and source freshness against real queries, RLS and indexes before rollout. Record exact tested envelope, not “scalable.”

### Requirement: US27-F58 — Trace and safely deploy the complete mixed-purpose contract with lawful retention and compatible restore

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a release and privacy owner, I want to trace and safely deploy the complete mixed-purpose contract with lawful retention and compatible restore, so that new accepted evidence survives upgrades and documentation is never mistaken for runtime qualification. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-F82, REQ27-F90, REQ27-F89.

#### Scenario: US27-F58 AC27-F82-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F82, REQ27-F90.

- **GIVEN** protected prose/documents and applicable retention/redaction with older backups.

- **WHEN** emitting logs/notifications and disposing/restoring data.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Logs/notifications omit protected free text and documents; lawful redaction/retention applies to history, exports, attachments and restored backups without resurrecting deleted content.

#### Scenario: US27-F58 AC27-F89-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-F89.

- **GIVEN** legacy records with unknown meanings and mixed client versions.

- **WHEN** performing resumable backfill and old/new writes.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Census/backfill preserves unknowns and source IDs, runs resumably without outreach, and mixed old/new clients cannot manufacture financial/nonfinancial state or erase new records.

#### Scenario: US27-F58 AC27-F89-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-F89.

- **GIVEN** new mixed-purpose records and independently owned obligations.

- **WHEN** disabling a cohort/effect, rolling forward or restoring safely.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Cohort disable, kill switch, rollback and restore after new mixed-purpose writes preserve history/obligations; new effects stop without destructive schema downgrade.

#### Scenario: US27-F58 AC27-F89-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-F89, REQ27-F90.

- **GIVEN** the completed formal decision and implementation evidence records.

- **WHEN** checking every source, requirement, design, issue, test and release link.

- **THEN** this complete observable contract shall hold: Trace Q29 → this exact D29 → glossary → canonical owner/OpenSpec reconciliation → design/tasks/issues → source/UI/security tests → release evidence; no formal or implementation status is inferred from document completion.

### Requirement: US27-C01 — Choose whether to contribute shared context without reporting quotas

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a missionary, I want to choose whether to contribute shared context without reporting quotas, so that I can coordinate voluntarily while honoring real source obligations. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C01.

#### Scenario: US27-C01 AC27-C01-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: choose whether to contribute shared context without reporting quotas.
- **THEN** A missionary can decline to write and continue ordinary permitted use without penalty, required stage/task, activity report or coaching enrollment; staff cannot configure such coercion through this lane.

#### Scenario: US27-C01 AC27-C01-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: choose whether to contribute shared context without reporting quotas.
- **THEN** Saving or reading an update leaves managed-work admission, pause/closure, source obligations and staff status unchanged; a gift handoff cannot be completed by a note.

#### Scenario: US27-C01 AC27-C01-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: choose whether to contribute shared context without reporting quotas.
- **THEN** An independently selected personal follow-up is created once through its source. Its failure or cancellation neither fails nor duplicates an already accepted update.

### Requirement: US27-C02 — Use a clearly shared composer and a genuinely separate qualified private destination

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a missionary or staff collaborator, I want to use a clearly shared composer and a genuinely separate qualified private destination, so that I do not accidentally publish protected or imported context. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C02.

#### Scenario: US27-C02 AC27-C02-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: use a clearly shared composer and a genuinely separate qualified private destination.
- **THEN** Existing private/imported/internal/care/location-sensitive/financial fixtures remain protected after activation, import, refresh and shared-history migration.

#### Scenario: US27-C02 AC27-C02-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: use a clearly shared composer and a genuinely separate qualified private destination.
- **THEN** The private destination discloses its actual reader scope, and its absence leaves a clear unavailable/cancel option with no shared save.

#### Scenario: US27-C02 AC27-C02-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: use a clearly shared composer and a genuinely separate qualified private destination.
- **THEN** A newly authored eligible update can be saved directly in its qualified shared context without a repeated sharing checkbox or supervisory approval.

### Requirement: US27-C03 — Understand current and possible future authorized readers before saving

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a partnership collaborator, I want to understand current and possible future authorized readers before saving, so that I can share appropriate context with confidence and retain privacy after role changes. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C03.

#### Scenario: US27-C03 AC27-C03-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: understand current and possible future authorized readers before saving.
- **THEN** Two ministries sharing one church and two tenants cannot see or write each other’s updates or protected audience metadata.

#### Scenario: US27-C03 AC27-C03-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: understand current and possible future authorized readers before saving.
- **THEN** A new assignee gains no earlier history from assignment alone; an independently history-authorized collaborator sees only the qualified earlier slice with the audience explanation.

#### Scenario: US27-C03 AC27-C03-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: understand current and possible future authorized readers before saving.
- **THEN** Revocation before acceptance blocks the write; revocation after acceptance blocks subsequent unauthorized body/result disclosure while preserving accepted history. Both serialized orders are demonstrated.

#### Scenario: US27-C03 AC27-C03-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: understand current and possible future authorized readers before saving.
- **THEN** Users can identify the exact partnership and permitted audience before saving; a changed or unavailable audience does not silently broaden the destination or disclose restricted names.

### Requirement: US27-C04 — Record my attributed account once against its actual source and partnership

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a staff or missionary contributor, I want to record my attributed account once against its actual source and partnership, so that the same encounter remains consistent across permitted surfaces without lost authorship. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C04.

#### Scenario: US27-C04 AC27-C04-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: record my attributed account once against its actual source and partnership.
- **THEN** Staff and missionary projections resolve the same permitted source update; two accounts of one meeting preserve distinct authorship and a single encounter, without copied editable transcripts.

#### Scenario: US27-C04 AC27-C04-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: record my attributed account once against its actual source and partnership.
- **THEN** Caller-forged tenant/actor/author and cross-tenant/context/source references are rejected without mutation or protected diagnostic disclosure.

#### Scenario: US27-C04 AC27-C04-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: record my attributed account once against its actual source and partnership.
- **THEN** Actual database roles cannot retarget origin or author through direct mutation, view, RPC or service paths; authorized source correction remains attributable, and destructive relationship removal does not erase history.

#### Scenario: US27-C04 AC27-C04-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: record my attributed account once against its actual source and partnership.
- **THEN** Concurrent edit or policy narrowing is revalidated at acceptance; stale intent cannot overwrite accepted history or publish beyond current authority.

### Requirement: US27-C05 — Recover the same accepted update after a lost response

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a contributor on an unreliable connection, I want to recover the same accepted update after a lost response, so that I do not create duplicates or retrieve information after access ends. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C05.

#### Scenario: US27-C05 AC27-C05-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: recover the same accepted update after a lost response.
- **THEN** Failure of required audit/history/outcome persistence leaves no accepted update; an identical retry accepts one effect, and changed payload/context under that identity conflicts.

#### Scenario: US27-C05 AC27-C05-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: recover the same accepted update after a lost response.
- **THEN** A lost reply recovers the accepted update once; a retry after read revocation returns no protected stored body or event-existence detail.

#### Scenario: US27-C05 AC27-C05-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: recover the same accepted update after a lost response.
- **THEN** An accepted update with delayed projection remains truthfully accepted/pending, and replay repairs the permitted derivative once without another update, encounter, notification or permission grant.

#### Scenario: US27-C05 AC27-C05-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: recover the same accepted update after a lost response.
- **THEN** Save-versus-revoke/narrow and competing edits are exercised in both applicable orders using actual source/database boundaries.

### Requirement: US27-C06 — Retain the correct draft and clear save status for each partnership

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a collaborator moving between records, I want to retain the correct draft and clear save status for each partnership, so that a late response or changed record cannot publish or erase the wrong text. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C06.

#### Scenario: US27-C06 AC27-C06-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: retain the correct draft and clear save status for each partnership.
- **THEN** An ordinary eligible update takes one deliberate save; the user can identify audience and correction/follow-through options without another form or approval.

#### Scenario: US27-C06 AC27-C06-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: retain the correct draft and clear save status for each partnership.
- **THEN** A draft for A cannot submit into B after a record/role switch; a late A response cannot erase B’s draft or text entered after A’s submission.

#### Scenario: US27-C06 AC27-C06-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: retain the correct draft and clear save status for each partnership.
- **THEN** Slow/lost saves, session loss and unavailable audience/history show distinct states and permit safe recovery without false Saved feedback or duplicate content.

#### Scenario: US27-C06 AC27-C06-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: retain the correct draft and clear save status for each partnership.
- **THEN** No automatic read receipt, all-collaborator broadcast, pressure to acknowledge, offline publication or newly exposed donor/public field follows from saving.

### Requirement: US27-C07 — Record and correct when an interaction actually occurred

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a staff or missionary contributor, I want to record and correct when an interaction actually occurred, so that history remains truthful without reopening work or rewriting someone else’s contribution. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C07.

#### Scenario: US27-C07 AC27-C07-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: record and correct when an interaction actually occurred.
- **THEN** Backdated, date-only and unknown occurrences remain distinguishable from recorded time across timezone changes; current owners do not replace departed authors.

#### Scenario: US27-C07 AC27-C07-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: record and correct when an interaction actually occurred.
- **THEN** Saving, sharing, viewing or correcting one meeting cannot add another encounter, change stage, complete work, send a message or count money.

#### Scenario: US27-C07 AC27-C07-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: record and correct when an interaction actually occurred.
- **THEN** Paused/closed contexts accept only source-permitted factual history/correction without reopening or reviving cancelled work; an actual current reply retains its own truthful date.

#### Scenario: US27-C07 AC27-C07-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: record and correct when an interaction actually occurred.
- **THEN** An authorized correction preserves prior lawful provenance and cannot silently rewrite another contributor’s account.

### Requirement: US27-C08 — Restrict and correct an accidental disclosure through its source

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized privacy operator, I want to restrict and correct an accidental disclosure through its source, so that future access and restored copies honor privacy while lawful evidence remains. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C08.

#### Scenario: US27-C08 AC27-C08-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: restrict and correct an accidental disclosure through its source.
- **THEN** Protected source facts and their existence do not leak through enabled consumers, result recovery, cache entries, sort/count or diagnostic output after revocation.

#### Scenario: US27-C08 AC27-C08-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: restrict and correct an accidental disclosure through its source.
- **THEN** A mistaken disclosure can be restricted/corrected through a qualified source action; downstream access stops, lawful audit remains, and delivered-copy limits are explained honestly.

#### Scenario: US27-C08 AC27-C08-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: restrict and correct an accidental disclosure through its source.
- **THEN** Restoring backup data reapplies source restriction/erasure/expiry before readability and does not republish private content.

#### Scenario: US27-C08 AC27-C08-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: restrict and correct an accidental disclosure through its source.
- **THEN** Technical logs and security audit retain required identifiers/outcomes without copying private bodies or becoming a second diary.

### Requirement: US27-C09 — Use dependable bounded shared history independently of optional products

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a tenant development administrator, I want to use dependable bounded shared history independently of optional products, so that staff and missionaries can coordinate without a second diary or unqualified legacy exposure. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C09.

#### Scenario: US27-C09 AC27-C09-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: use dependable bounded shared history independently of optional products.
- **THEN** Native shared history operates with optional workflow/external-sync services unavailable and without a second diary or blanket shared-account permission.

#### Scenario: US27-C09 AC27-C09-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: use dependable bounded shared history independently of optional products.
- **THEN** Private retained fixtures stay private under old/new clients, activation, stop, roll-forward and restore; the stop action preserves authorized corrective recovery.

#### Scenario: US27-C09 AC27-C09-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: use dependable bounded shared history independently of optional products.
- **THEN** Multiple tenants, a long-history tenant, maximum admitted payload/page and concurrent permission changes meet declared numeric budgets with permission-safe bounded queries.

#### Scenario: US27-C09 AC27-C09-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: use dependable bounded shared history independently of optional products.
- **THEN** An unavailable or failed history query is never presented as an empty, healthy history.

### Requirement: US27-C10 — See actual personal exchanges separately from sent outreach

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a staff or missionary collaborator, I want to see actual personal exchanges separately from sent outreach, so that I can understand contact history without treating every message or short reply incorrectly. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C10.

#### Scenario: US27-C10 AC27-C10-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: see actual personal exchanges separately from sent outreach.
- **THEN** A sent personal email updates outreach only; a source-qualified human response updates exchange recency using occurrence evidence, including a brief decline and an inbound without recorded outbound.

#### Scenario: US27-C10 AC27-C10-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: see actual personal exchanges separately from sent outreach.
- **THEN** Thank-you outreach needing no answer generates no pending-response/overdue warning; a short/negative response is not disqualified.

#### Scenario: US27-C10 AC27-C10-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: see actual personal exchanges separately from sent outreach.
- **THEN** Call attempts, voicemail, scheduled/no-show meetings, passive attendance, delivery/open signals, automated/uncertain replies and generic completed activities do not create exchange evidence.

#### Scenario: US27-C10 AC27-C10-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: see actual personal exchanges separately from sent outreach.
- **THEN** One truthful manually recorded conversation is sufficient without transcript, extra approval, meaningfulness checkbox or compulsory next task.

### Requirement: US27-C11 — See a complete, correctable and honestly dated exchange summary

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a staff member preparing follow-up, I want to see a complete, correctable and honestly dated exchange summary, so that old, undated or hidden history does not become false neglect or a fabricated recent conversation. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C11.

#### Scenario: US27-C11 AC27-C11-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: see a complete, correctable and honestly dated exchange summary.
- **THEN** The qualifying latest exchange beyond the first 50 display rows is found; restricting/correcting the newest event produces the truthful earlier or uncertain result.

#### Scenario: US27-C11 AC27-C11-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: see a complete, correctable and honestly dated exchange summary.
- **THEN** Historical entry, date-only travel, leap/date boundaries and unknown dates retain precision; no created-at/epoch fallback, negative age or future plan appears as completed exchange.

#### Scenario: US27-C11 AC27-C11-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: see a complete, correctable and honestly dated exchange summary.
- **THEN** An undated qualified event is retained and prevents an unsupported definitive latest-date/aging claim while independently known dated facts remain usable.

#### Scenario: US27-C11 AC27-C11-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: see a complete, correctable and honestly dated exchange summary.
- **THEN** Empty, filtered, incomplete, loading, unavailable and undated states are distinguishable without disclosing hidden history or using Never contacted.

### Requirement: US27-C12 — See the same qualified exchange meaning within my permitted partnership scope

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a missionary or staff reader, I want to see the same qualified exchange meaning within my permitted partnership scope, so that sharing, affiliation and old messages cannot expand access or create pressure to contact. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C12.

#### Scenario: US27-C12 AC27-C12-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: see the same qualified exchange meaning within my permitted partnership scope.
- **THEN** The same permitted source event yields consistent exchange meaning in staff and missionary views; two narratives/retries/imports count one encounter.

#### Scenario: US27-C12 AC27-C12-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: see the same qualified exchange meaning within my permitted partnership scope.
- **THEN** Cross-tenant/other-ministry/church-affiliation/selected-participant-only paths cannot update another partnership or disclose its date, source or count.

#### Scenario: US27-C12 AC27-C12-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: see the same qualified exchange meaning within my permitted partnership scope.
- **THEN** Body expiry retains only independently permitted body-free evidence; source revocation protects all unauthorized derivatives and recovered outputs.

#### Scenario: US27-C12 AC27-C12-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: see the same qualified exchange meaning within my permitted partnership scope.
- **THEN** Old recency, absent reply and a new reply to closed work create no automatic solicitation, stage/lifecycle change or missionary penalty and add no public/donor input.

### Requirement: US27-C13 — Choose optional financial ratings separately from automatic internal reviews

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a tenant administrator, I want to choose optional financial ratings separately from automatic internal reviews, so that the product remains useful without scores or unexpected review chores. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C13.

#### Scenario: US27-C13 AC27-C13-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: choose optional financial ratings separately from automatic internal reviews.
- **THEN** New tenant and legacy-zero fixtures remain ratings/review Off; facts and independent obligations work with optional workflow services unavailable.

#### Scenario: US27-C13 AC27-C13-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: choose optional financial ratings separately from automatic internal reviews.
- **THEN** Enabling analysis creates zero review tasks; review requires separate authorized activation and source/routing qualification.

#### Scenario: US27-C13 AC27-C13-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: choose optional financial ratings separately from automatic internal reviews.
- **THEN** Disabling/re-enabling ratings preserves ordinary work and requires deliberate review reactivation, with no restored old unread/tasks.

#### Scenario: US27-C13 AC27-C13-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: choose optional financial ratings separately from automatic internal reviews.
- **THEN** No default highest-giver ordering, relationship-value label, missionary quota, donor checkout field or public badge is introduced.

### Requirement: US27-C14 — Understand separate financial dimensions and approved ranges

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized financial staff member, I want to understand separate financial dimensions and approved ranges, so that I can inspect observed giving without a misleading relationship-value or peer-ranking score. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C14.

#### Scenario: US27-C14 AC27-C14-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: understand separate financial dimensions and approved ranges.
- **THEN** Users can inspect raw values, units, ranges, scope and source for each separate axis, without a composite number beside the person’s name.

#### Scenario: US27-C14 AC27-C14-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: understand separate financial dimensions and approved ranges.
- **THEN** A change in another donor, viewer filter or portfolio leaves this subject’s absolute-range result unchanged.

#### Scenario: US27-C14 AC27-C14-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: understand separate financial dimensions and approved ranges.
- **THEN** Configuration rejects unsupported inputs/formulas or unauthorized preview data; qualified presets remain visibly editable drafts until accepted.

#### Scenario: US27-C14 AC27-C14-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: understand separate financial dimensions and approved ranges.
- **THEN** Authorized ordinary configuration uses one deliberate save, while an actual disclosure-policy widening follows its independent maker/checker rule.

### Requirement: US27-C15 — Inspect the subject, lens and scope behind each giving pattern

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized financial reader, I want to inspect the subject, lens and scope behind each giving pattern, so that split gifts and recognition cannot double count or imply support for the wrong ministry. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C15.

#### Scenario: US27-C15 AC27-C15-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: inspect the subject, lens and scope behind each giving pattern.
- **THEN** One gift with three designations and replayed delivery counts one eligible occurrence and only its selected-scope amount; two genuine identical gifts remain two.

#### Scenario: US27-C15 AC27-C15-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: inspect the subject, lens and scope behind each giving pattern.
- **THEN** Legal giving and recognition—including DAF advisor, employer, household and overlapping roles—remain separate qualified lenses with no double-credit scalar.

#### Scenario: US27-C15 AC27-C15-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: inspect the subject, lens and scope behind each giving pattern.
- **THEN** Entity-wide giving is labeled correctly within a ministry partnership; unsupported purpose/campaign/recognition readers stay unavailable rather than guessed.

#### Scenario: US27-C15 AC27-C15-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: inspect the subject, lens and scope behind each giving pattern.
- **THEN** Changing screen, current household membership, name, staff assignment or an unrelated donor cannot alter authoritative attribution or reveal another scope.

### Requirement: US27-C16 — Have ratings use only qualified received monetary facts

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a finance staff member, I want to have ratings use only qualified received monetary facts, so that pending gifts, promises, noncash values and fees do not distort financial truth. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C16.

#### Scenario: US27-C16 AC27-C16-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: have ratings use only qualified received monetary facts.
- **THEN** USD, JPY and supported three-decimal amounts retain exact source units; processor costs, settlement conversion and tax benefits cannot move the original-currency band.

#### Scenario: US27-C16 AC27-C16-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: have ratings use only qualified received monetary facts.
- **THEN** Pending handoffs, unposted ACH, promises, match expectancies, monthly equivalents, securities valuations and liquidation do not add monetary occurrences/amounts; an admitted posted provisional check follows source truth.

#### Scenario: US27-C16 AC27-C16-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: have ratings use only qualified received monetary facts.
- **THEN** Prayer/hosting/introduction and recognition-only participation do not become a low-value relationship or adverse review solely from zero direct giving.

#### Scenario: US27-C16 AC27-C16-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: have ratings use only qualified received monetary facts.
- **THEN** A source-finance fact change is consumed through its owner; rating commands cannot post money, issue receipts, fulfill commitments or mutate payment execution.

### Requirement: US27-C17 — See exact count, amount and period-specific recency with honest empty states

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized financial reader, I want to see exact count, amount and period-specific recency with honest empty states, so that unknown history cannot be mistaken for zero or a low-value relationship. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C17.

#### Scenario: US27-C17 AC27-C17-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: see exact count, amount and period-specific recency with honest empty states.
- **THEN** Known empty history produces frequency 0, amount 0 and nonnumeric no-qualifying-gift recency; incomplete/denied data does not produce those known-zero claims.

#### Scenario: US27-C17 AC27-C17-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: see exact count, amount and period-specific recency with honest empty states.
- **THEN** For qualified roots in the period, independently computed exact root count, eligible monetary sum and calendar-day recency agree, including split/replay cases.

#### Scenario: US27-C17 AC27-C17-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: see exact count, amount and period-specific recency with honest empty states.
- **THEN** A known older out-of-period gift is shown separately and never included in the period’s count/amount/recency.

#### Scenario: US27-C17 AC27-C17-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: see exact count, amount and period-specific recency with honest empty states.
- **THEN** An unresolved eligible gift date/amount invalidates every affected axis without erasing independently complete facts or pretending full coverage.

### Requirement: US27-C18 — Choose a clearly dated supported observation period

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a tenant administrator, I want to choose a clearly dated supported observation period, so that calendar boundaries, timezones and partial periods do not manufacture changes. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C18.

#### Scenario: US27-C18 AC27-C18-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: choose a clearly dated supported observation period.
- **THEN** Leap-day/month-end fixtures produce the declared clamped lower boundary, exclude it, include the evaluation date and display matching inclusive dates.

#### Scenario: US27-C18 AC27-C18-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: choose a clearly dated supported observation period.
- **THEN** Future-dated evidence cannot create a completed gift or negative recency; an open fiscal period is labeled to-date and excludes later dates.

#### Scenario: US27-C18 AC27-C18-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: choose a clearly dated supported observation period.
- **THEN** Timezone travel and locale display do not shift source gift dates or fiscal boundaries; trailing months, fixed year, fiscal year and year-to-date stay distinct.

#### Scenario: US27-C18 AC27-C18-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: choose a clearly dated supported observation period.
- **THEN** Changing period or fiscal rollover establishes a comparable new basis rather than comparing partial values to a completed prior year and creating a decline.

### Requirement: US27-C19 — Edit complete numeric bands with precise validation

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a tenant administrator, I want to edit complete numeric bands with precise validation, so that every qualified value has one understandable financial classification. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C19.

#### Scenario: US27-C19 AC27-C19-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: edit complete numeric bands with precise validation.
- **THEN** Values exactly below/on/above each cutpoint, meaningful zero and the top open range resolve to exactly one disclosed band with correct units.

#### Scenario: US27-C19 AC27-C19-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: edit complete numeric bands with precise validation.
- **THEN** Gaps, overlaps, reversed bounds, incompatible precision/units and invalid intervals reject the entire change without a partially active policy.

#### Scenario: US27-C19 AC27-C19-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: edit complete numeric bands with precise validation.
- **THEN** Empty-period recency stays nonnumeric, while independently known zero frequency/amount can be banded without a relationship-worth implication.

#### Scenario: US27-C19 AC27-C19-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: edit complete numeric bands with precise validation.
- **THEN** Incomplete setup preserves useful facts and clear next steps; no arbitrary preset or peer population activates a rating automatically.

### Requirement: US27-C20 — See corrections and coverage changes explained as their actual causes

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized financial reader, I want to see corrections and coverage changes explained as their actual causes, so that returns, imports or missing evidence do not become a false relational decline. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C20.

#### Scenario: US27-C20 AC27-C20-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: see corrections and coverage changes explained as their actual causes.
- **THEN** Partial/full return, void and date correction produce the exact new count/amount/date while retaining history and explicit correction cause; unresolved refunds do not reduce posted money.

#### Scenario: US27-C20 AC27-C20-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: see corrections and coverage changes explained as their actual causes.
- **THEN** Incomplete imports, permission loss, asynchronous recognition and source outages cannot become a zero/low band; independently complete axes remain accurate.

#### Scenario: US27-C20 AC27-C20-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: see corrections and coverage changes explained as their actual causes.
- **THEN** Deduplication or identity correction does not double count roots, overwrite financial provenance or manufacture an adverse review episode.

#### Scenario: US27-C20 AC27-C20-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: see corrections and coverage changes explained as their actual causes.
- **THEN** Known no gifts, inapplicable nonfinancial participation and missing source history are visibly distinct and independently testable.

### Requirement: US27-C21 — Enforce staff financial permissions on every rating derivative

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a privacy administrator, I want to enforce staff financial permissions on every rating derivative, so that tenant enablement or shared history cannot disclose ratings to the wrong person or surface. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C21.

#### Scenario: US27-C21 AC27-C21-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: enforce staff financial permissions on every rating derivative.
- **THEN** Staff permitted to manage settings but not read a protected financial subject cannot obtain its values, bands, previews or aggregate existence through any enabled consumer.

#### Scenario: US27-C21 AC27-C21-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: enforce staff financial permissions on every rating derivative.
- **THEN** Cross-tenant/other-purpose/lens identifiers and caller-forged actor/owner/approval fields produce no write or protected diagnostic information.

#### Scenario: US27-C21 AC27-C21-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: enforce staff financial permissions on every rating derivative.
- **THEN** Missionary, donor and public APIs, briefs, exports, caches and legacy roster paths disclose no real D28 rating; sharing a partnership changes none of those rights.

#### Scenario: US27-C21 AC27-C21-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: enforce staff financial permissions on every rating derivative.
- **THEN** Permission removal after calculation or before replay/result release immediately blocks forbidden derivatives while preserving lawful source history.

### Requirement: US27-C22 — Change rating policy once with a clear authoritative outcome

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a tenant administrator, I want to change rating policy once with a clear authoritative outcome, so that concurrent saves and stale workers cannot restore disabled behavior. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C22.

#### Scenario: US27-C22 AC27-C22-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: change rating policy once with a clear authoritative outcome.
- **THEN** Concurrent editors cannot silently combine settings; a lost save response recovers the same accepted intent and changed payload under that intent conflicts.

#### Scenario: US27-C22 AC27-C22-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: change rating policy once with a clear authoritative outcome.
- **THEN** Off racing a worker in either order allows only effects admitted by the authoritative generation and blocks every stale result/task/export after the fence.

#### Scenario: US27-C22 AC27-C22-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: change rating policy once with a clear authoritative outcome.
- **THEN** Off remains available when calculation or impact-count preview fails; accepted Off is not dependent on successful asynchronous cleanup.

#### Scenario: US27-C22 AC27-C22-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: change rating policy once with a clear authoritative outcome.
- **THEN** Ordinary clients, old writers and optional workflows cannot activate or publish via a parallel boolean or forged generation.

### Requirement: US27-C23 — Receive an explicit repair choice when ratings are off or inaccessible

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a staff member using saved views or exports, I want to receive an explicit repair choice when ratings are off or inaccessible, so that my filter is not silently removed and my results are not broadened. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C23.

#### Scenario: US27-C23 AC27-C23-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: receive an explicit repair choice when ratings are off or inaccessible.
- **THEN** After Off, no current rating remains in lists/details/API/search/filter/sort/export or optional workflow use; stale responses and caches cannot restore it.

#### Scenario: US27-C23 AC27-C23-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: receive an explicit repair choice when ratings are off or inaccessible.
- **THEN** A disabled/unauthorized saved predicate shows an explicit repair state and never silently broadens a segment, export or result.

#### Scenario: US27-C23 AC27-C23-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: receive an explicit repair choice when ratings are off or inaccessible.
- **THEN** New/scheduled rating distribution stops while historical artifacts remain governed and clearly historical; normal independent financial reporting still works.

#### Scenario: US27-C23 AC27-C23-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: receive an explicit repair choice when ratings are off or inaccessible.
- **THEN** Off does not write zeros, delete source history or falsely promise recall of previously delivered material.

### Requirement: US27-C24 — Establish a fresh baseline when enabling or changing analysis

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a tenant administrator, I want to establish a fresh baseline when enabling or changing analysis, so that configuration and historical data changes do not create a backlog of invented declines. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C24.

#### Scenario: US27-C24 AC27-C24-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: establish a fresh baseline when enabling or changing analysis.
- **THEN** Off→On and review-Off→On create a fresh baseline without historical review tasks, old task identity or restored unread notifications.

#### Scenario: US27-C24 AC27-C24-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: establish a fresh baseline when enabling or changing analysis.
- **THEN** Changed ranges/lens/scope/subject/currency/period, historical imports and repaired coverage cannot manufacture decline by comparing incompatible versions.

#### Scenario: US27-C24 AC27-C24-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: establish a fresh baseline when enabling or changing analysis.
- **THEN** Fiscal rollover and partial versus full periods preserve explicit comparable basis; allowed rolling-window movement is identified as time/window movement.

#### Scenario: US27-C24 AC27-C24-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: establish a fresh baseline when enabling or changing analysis.
- **THEN** Preparation failure yields preparing/unavailable with useful ordinary facts, not a fabricated current band or saved-policy claim.

### Requirement: US27-C25 — Opt into a finite explained internal-review condition only when useful

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a tenant administrator, I want to opt into a finite explained internal-review condition only when useful, so that analysis can support staff judgment without automatically demanding solicitation. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C25.

#### Scenario: US27-C25 AC27-C25-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: opt into a finite explained internal-review condition only when useful.
- **THEN** Only separate authorized review opt-in plus qualified source/routing permits work; ratings-only mode creates none.

#### Scenario: US27-C25 AC27-C25-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: opt into a finite explained internal-review condition only when useful.
- **THEN** Selected band/known-empty conditions have the disclosed OR meaning; unknown or inapplicable evidence cannot become a true/false transition.

#### Scenario: US27-C25 AC27-C25-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: opt into a finite explained internal-review condition only when useful.
- **THEN** Recognition-only, annual-support, prayer/hosting and pending-finance examples retain context and do not generate adverse work solely from direct-giving zero.

#### Scenario: US27-C25 AC27-C25-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: opt into a finite explained internal-review condition only when useful.
- **THEN** No hidden fact, setup baseline, changed cohort/formula or retrospective import creates a review or contact instruction.

### Requirement: US27-C26 — Receive one review for one genuine qualified entry into the selected conditions

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a development reviewer, I want to receive one review for one genuine qualified entry into the selected conditions, so that retries, multiple axes and repeated calculations do not produce duplicate work. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C26.

#### Scenario: US27-C26 AC27-C26-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: receive one review for one genuine qualified entry into the selected conditions.
- **THEN** Three changed dimensions and native/workflow/retry/replay delivery of one transition converge on one source episode/shared effect.

#### Scenario: US27-C26 AC27-C26-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: receive one review for one genuine qualified entry into the selected conditions.
- **THEN** Completion while still inside the selected set creates no new review on later polls; qualified exit and subsequent re-entry may create exactly one successor.

#### Scenario: US27-C26 AC27-C26-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: receive one review for one genuine qualified entry into the selected conditions.
- **THEN** Unknown→known evidence or correction/import/policy change cannot manufacture exit/re-entry; complete false evidence withdraws an unresolved episode truthfully.

#### Scenario: US27-C26 AC27-C26-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: receive one review for one genuine qualified entry into the selected conditions.
- **THEN** Clock/window-boundary entry occurs only under its approved temporal condition with reason explaining time/window movement.

### Requirement: US27-C27 — Receive a source-owned internal action with correct responsibility and a No change needed outcome

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a development reviewer, I want to receive a source-owned internal action with correct responsibility and a No change needed outcome, so that I can resolve a review without pretending to contact someone or relying on a guessed assignee. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C27.

#### Scenario: US27-C27 AC27-C27-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: receive a source-owned internal action with correct responsibility and a No change needed outcome.
- **THEN** A qualifying episode reaches only the currently authorized responsible person with permitted explanation and a concrete internal source action.

#### Scenario: US27-C27 AC27-C27-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: receive a source-owned internal action with correct responsibility and a No change needed outcome.
- **THEN** Missing/revoked/indeterminate routing creates a source-safe repair state without arbitrary fallback delivery, leaked details, donor contact or implicit reopening.

#### Scenario: US27-C27 AC27-C27-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: receive a source-owned internal action with correct responsibility and a No change needed outcome.
- **THEN** No change needed resolves the source review honestly; reading/claiming/commenting or generic task completion cannot counterfeit source completion.

#### Scenario: US27-C27 AC27-C27-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: receive a source-owned internal action with correct responsibility and a No change needed outcome.
- **THEN** Deliberate independent follow-up creates its own source work and consent obligations without duplicating or converting the original automatic episode.

### Requirement: US27-C28 — Stop solely automatic unresolved reviews when turning their feature off

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a tenant administrator, I want to stop solely automatic unresolved reviews when turning their feature off, so that unnecessary work ends without losing independently planned follow-up or financial obligations. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C28.

#### Scenario: US27-C28 AC27-C28-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: stop solely automatic unresolved reviews when turning their feature off.
- **THEN** Off withdraws unclaimed, claimed and commented solely automatic reviews as No longer required and prevents their source actions before delayed task cleanup.

#### Scenario: US27-C28 AC27-C28-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: stop solely automatic unresolved reviews when turning their feature off.
- **THEN** Completed reviews, independently planned calls and existing D6/P15/P16/P28 obligations survive the mode change with accurate reasons and history.

#### Scenario: US27-C28 AC27-C28-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: stop solely automatic unresolved reviews when turning their feature off.
- **THEN** Re-enable never restores withdrawn work/unread state; only a new qualified activation/baseline and later eligible transition admits successor work.

#### Scenario: US27-C28 AC27-C28-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: stop solely automatic unresolved reviews when turning their feature off.
- **THEN** Unexpected projection lag is visible and repairable through source reconciliation, not by marking the old task Completed.

### Requirement: US27-C29 — Distinguish unavailable evidence from resolved work and recover accepted changes

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a staff member using financial context during an outage, I want to distinguish unavailable evidence from resolved work and recover accepted changes, so that service failures cannot invent zero values, completion or duplicate effects. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C29.

#### Scenario: US27-C29 AC27-C29-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: distinguish unavailable evidence from resolved work and recover accepted changes.
- **THEN** Source outage or incomplete coverage shows unavailable/stale, produces no downgrade/upgrade, preserves independent record facts and does not falsely complete a review.

#### Scenario: US27-C29 AC27-C29-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: distinguish unavailable evidence from resolved work and recover accepted changes.
- **THEN** Accepted write followed by task/cache failure recovers once with current authority; retry or partial cleanup does not resurrect disabled effects.

#### Scenario: US27-C29 AC27-C29-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: distinguish unavailable evidence from resolved work and recover accepted changes.
- **THEN** Correction changes affected reasons without claiming relational decline or generating a fresh adverse entry.

#### Scenario: US27-C29 AC27-C29-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: distinguish unavailable evidence from resolved work and recover accepted changes.
- **THEN** Slow/lost settings saves, navigation and role/tenant changes cannot duplicate intent, clear another draft, reveal stale facts or falsely report Saved.

### Requirement: US27-C30 — Verify database boundaries and attributable policy history

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a security and financial operations owner, I want to verify database boundaries and attributable policy history, so that scope changes, privileged paths and retirement cannot corrupt or expose financial analysis. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C30.

#### Scenario: US27-C30 AC27-C30-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: verify database boundaries and attributable policy history.
- **THEN** Actual anon/authenticated/owner/service/definer paths enforce qualified grants, effective old/new-row rules, immutable scope and append-only history; no caller can forge policy/bands.

#### Scenario: US27-C30 AC27-C30-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: verify database boundaries and attributable policy history.
- **THEN** Duplicate source-effect identities cannot produce two active episodes; invalid units/ranges/state and scope-changing mutations fail without partial history.

#### Scenario: US27-C30 AC27-C30-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: verify database boundaries and attributable policy history.
- **THEN** Retiring a Party, policy or user preserves legally required financial/policy/work evidence and lawful attribution.

#### Scenario: US27-C30 AC27-C30-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: verify database boundaries and attributable policy history.
- **THEN** Audits independently identify policy/source/cutoff/cause/actor/outcome without copying protected records; export/erasure/backup handling follows owners rather than perpetual retention.

### Requirement: US27-C31 — Roll out and stop ratings safely at measured workloads

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a tenant operations administrator, I want to roll out and stop ratings safely at measured workloads, so that large tenants and legacy consumers cannot break ordinary work or ignore tenant choice. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C31.

#### Scenario: US27-C31 AC27-C31-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: roll out and stop ratings safely at measured workloads.
- **THEN** Enabled/disabled, small/large/skewed multi-tenant workloads meet declared numeric freshness/query/job/memory/retry budgets and maintain fair progress without blocking ordinary facts.

#### Scenario: US27-C31 AC27-C31-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: roll out and stop ratings safely at measured workloads.
- **THEN** No disabled scope schedules rating calculations; an exceeded persisted deadline yields visible stale state and prevents new unsupported inference.

#### Scenario: US27-C31 AC27-C31-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: roll out and stop ratings safely at measured workloads.
- **THEN** Legacy-zero/import fixtures do not opt tenants in or populate meaningful public/missionary scores; every reached old reader/writer honors current generation.

#### Scenario: US27-C31 AC27-C31-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: roll out and stop ratings safely at measured workloads.
- **THEN** Mixed versions, restore, tenant migration, operational stop and roll-forward retain tenant choice, policy/history and financial truth without resurrected effects.

### Requirement: US27-C32 — Use a deterministic source-linked brief with qualified documents and exports

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a staff member preparing a meeting, I want to use a deterministic source-linked brief with qualified documents and exports, so that I can prepare accurately without AI, copied transcripts or a new document product. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C32.

#### Scenario: US27-C32 AC27-C32-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: use a deterministic source-linked brief with qualified documents and exports.
- **THEN** With AI and optional document-product services absent, staff can prepare a meeting from the complete useful permitted structured screen and follow source links.

#### Scenario: US27-C32 AC27-C32-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: use a deterministic source-linked brief with qualified documents and exports.
- **THEN** A brief distinguishes legal giving, recognition, commitments, actual exchanges, outreach and next work, showing source dates and incomplete/unknown coverage honestly.

#### Scenario: US27-C32 AC27-C32-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: use a deterministic source-linked brief with qualified documents and exports.
- **THEN** Private interaction or finance data cannot appear via summary, cached brief, linked document or export; ratings Off removes current bands without removing independent financial facts.

#### Scenario: US27-C32 AC27-C32-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: use a deterministic source-linked brief with qualified documents and exports.
- **THEN** One encounter referenced in brief/Request/CRM/Support remains source-owned; document/export access and expiry are rechecked and do not create public sharing or contact authority.

### Requirement: US27-C33 — Track a church and its contacts, giving and ministry relationships distinctly

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a church relations staff member, I want to track a church and its contacts, giving and ministry relationships distinctly, so that leadership changes and shared mailboxes do not rewrite history or grant member access. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C33.

#### Scenario: US27-C33 AC27-C33-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: track a church and its contacts, giving and ministry relationships distinctly.
- **THEN** Church, staff, missionaries and contact capacities remain distinct identities; sending does not imply financial support or personal donor access.

#### Scenario: US27-C33 AC27-C33-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: track a church and its contacts, giving and ministry relationships distinctly.
- **THEN** Changing a pastor/committee representative preserves the actual historical participants and old response attribution, while current work can be deliberately updated.

#### Scenario: US27-C33 AC27-C33-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: track a church and its contacts, giving and ministry relationships distinctly.
- **THEN** Church gift, member remittance and personal member gift do not merge into one giving or recognition total; shared mailbox/committee access grants no member solicitation.

#### Scenario: US27-C33 AC27-C33-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: track a church and its contacts, giving and ministry relationships distinctly.
- **THEN** Known budget/renewal/visit context and nonfinancial Requests can guide deliberate next work without automatic annual reminders, event booking or a separate church CRM.

### Requirement: US27-C34 — Use clear native owner, stage, Request and forecast/outcome views

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a development manager, I want to use clear native owner, stage, Request and forecast/outcome views, so that I can understand current work without a misleading combined total or dependence on broader analytics. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C34.

#### Scenario: US27-C34 AC27-C34-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: use clear native owner, stage, Request and forecast/outcome views.
- **THEN** Owner/stage/Request and honest forecast/outcome views remain usable without P33 or ratings, with financial tuple/coverage clearly stated.

#### Scenario: US27-C34 AC27-C34-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: use clear native owner, stage, Request and forecast/outcome views.
- **THEN** Overlapping appeal/request attribution and unlike currency/entity/period/lens results never add into a fabricated conserved total.

#### Scenario: US27-C34 AC27-C34-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: use clear native owner, stage, Request and forecast/outcome views.
- **THEN** Reconstruction from the same qualified source tuple produces the same permitted result; cache/replay respects current scope and revocation.

#### Scenario: US27-C34 AC27-C34-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: use clear native owner, stage, Request and forecast/outcome views.
- **THEN** An unqualified campaign-temporal basis remains visibly unavailable without guessed live/frozen attribution; independently qualified direct giving and nonfinancial facts still load.

### Requirement: US27-C35 — Understand and operate shared context and financial details in the familiar interface

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a staff member or missionary using mobile or assistive technology, I want to understand and operate shared context and financial details in the familiar interface, so that I can complete work clearly on different devices, languages and connection qualities. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C35.

#### Scenario: US27-C35 AC27-C35-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: understand and operate shared context and financial details in the familiar interface.
- **THEN** Users can identify responsibility, audience, actual exchange versus outreach, next action and how to change/correct it without coaching, duplicate entry or extra mandatory confirmation.

#### Scenario: US27-C35 AC27-C35-02
