- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: understand and operate shared context and financial details in the familiar interface.
- **THEN** The composed flow works with keyboard, screen reader, touch/mobile keyboard, reflow/zoom, visible focus, light/dark contrast and reduced motion; list/board actions have non-drag alternatives.

#### Scenario: US27-C35 AC27-C35-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: understand and operate shared context and financial details in the familiar interface.
- **THEN** Long/multiscript names, RTL, localized dates and exact amounts remain readable without altering source meaning or requiring new name/address input.

#### Scenario: US27-C35 AC27-C35-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: understand and operate shared context and financial details in the familiar interface.
- **THEN** Poor connections preserve permitted drafts and truthful pending/unavailable states, keep ordinary facts usable, and never silently publish or expose stale context.

### Requirement: US27-C36 — Require traceable source, privacy, accessibility, workload and user-comprehension evidence

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a release owner, I want to require traceable source, privacy, accessibility, workload and user-comprehension evidence, so that we activate qualified behavior and respond through accountable operators rather than claiming untested readiness. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-C36.

#### Scenario: US27-C36 AC27-C36-01

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: require traceable source, privacy, accessibility, workload and user-comprehension evidence.
- **THEN** Every old D26/D27/D28 requirement and acceptance clause has an explicit successor mapping; source, glossary, formal owner/OpenSpec, work, tests and release meaning remain consistent.

#### Scenario: US27-C36 AC27-C36-02

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: require traceable source, privacy, accessibility, workload and user-comprehension evidence.
- **THEN** Real-role/source/concurrency/egress/migration/native-independence proofs and actual composed accessibility checks are recorded separately from prior synthetic/document checks.

#### Scenario: US27-C36 AC27-C36-03

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: require traceable source, privacy, accessibility, workload and user-comprehension evidence.
- **THEN** Representative-user observations demonstrate correct sharing, voluntary participation, exchange and financial-band interpretation; any observed unsafe interpretation stops expansion until retested.

#### Scenario: US27-C36 AC27-C36-04

- **WHEN** the actor exercises this capability: require traceable source, privacy, accessibility, workload and user-comprehension evidence.
- **THEN** Release evidence assigns actual operators to every monitor, records exact numeric admitted workloads and deadlines, and demonstrates the recovery response; no unresolved mandatory gate is represented as a pass.

### Requirement: US27-A01 — Start with reviewed changes and opt into automatic changes for a bounded scope

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a Tenant administrator, I want to start with reviewed changes and opt into automatic changes for a bounded scope, so that I can choose the amount of automation without changing other responsibilities. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A01.

#### Scenario: US27-A01 AC27-A01-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A01.

- **GIVEN** a new Tenant and two independently declared assignment-policy scopes;
- **WHEN** an authorized administrator reads the saved mode, enables automatic changes for one scope, then returns that scope to Review changes;
- **THEN** New tenant uses reviewed existing-assignment transfers; tenant can explicitly enable Auto for a declared bounded scope and safely return to Review. Other scopes keep their own stated mode.

### Requirement: US27-A02 — Route a genuinely eligible new responsibility without enabling automatic replacement

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a development staff member, I want to route a genuinely eligible new responsibility without enabling automatic replacement, so that I can start work without an unnecessary automation setup. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A01, REQ27-A02, REQ27-A04.

#### Scenario: US27-A02 AC27-A01-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-A01, REQ27-A02.

- **GIVEN** qualified initial-routing criteria and ordinary staff transfer authority, with automatic replacement disabled;
- **WHEN** staff route a genuinely eligible new or unassigned responsibility and perform individual and finite reviewed-group transfers;
- **THEN** Native rule-based initial assignment and ordinary manual/bulk edits work without configuring a territory engine or automatic existing-assignment transfers.

#### Scenario: US27-A02 AC27-A04-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A04.

- **GIVEN** one protected manual assignment, one intentional vacancy and one imported responsibility with unknown management provenance;
- **WHEN** initial routing, automatic replacement, rule edits and delayed retries encounter those responsibilities;
- **THEN** A manually assigned record, deliberately unassigned role and imported unknown-provenance record are not moved or refilled by initial routing, Auto, rule edit or replay.

### Requirement: US27-A03 — Keep native assignment usable when optional products are unavailable

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a development staff member, I want to keep native assignment usable when optional products are unavailable, so that I can continue ordinary ministry work without a workflow or directory dependency. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A01, REQ27-A02, REQ27-A09.

#### Scenario: US27-A03 AC27-A01-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-A01, REQ27-A02, REQ27-A09.

- **GIVEN** native assignment prerequisites are qualified while Studio, giving ratings, optional reporting or directory services are unavailable;
- **WHEN** an authorized actor or bounded native executor performs an independently safe assignment operation;
- **THEN** Studio, ratings, optional reports and directory services being unavailable do not block independently safe native assignment; no rule uses unauthorized external authority.

### Requirement: US27-A04 — Configure understandable supported criteria, eligible targets and deterministic priority

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a Tenant administrator, I want to configure understandable supported criteria, eligible targets and deterministic priority, so that I can know which rule governs an assignment and avoid arbitrary replacements. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A02, REQ27-A09.

#### Scenario: US27-A04 AC27-A02-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A02.

- **GIVEN** ordered overlapping rules, including a known-false predicate, an unknown higher-priority predicate and a first-true rule whose target is ineligible;
- **WHEN** the source evaluates each case against current authorized input;
- **THEN** Ordered overlapping rules produce the first proved matching rule; unknown earlier predicates and ineligible selected targets do not silently fall through.

#### Scenario: US27-A04 AC27-A02-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-A02, REQ27-A09.

- **GIVEN** candidate staff differ in Tenant, role eligibility, current access and supported target-resolution basis;
- **WHEN** staff search/select or a rule resolves a receiving person, including an ambiguous multi-candidate result;
- **THEN** Target choices obey current staff/role/tenant/source eligibility and qualified deterministic resolution; multiple candidates are not resolved by database order.

### Requirement: US27-A05 — See no-match, unknown-input and ineligible-target outcomes separately

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a development staff member, I want to see no-match, unknown-input and ineligible-target outcomes separately, so that I can retain valid accountability while resolving the actual problem. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A02, REQ27-A07.

#### Scenario: US27-A05 AC27-A02-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A02.

- **GIVEN** ordered overlapping rules, including a known-false predicate, an unknown higher-priority predicate and a first-true rule whose target is ineligible;
- **WHEN** the source evaluates each case against current authorized input;
- **THEN** Ordered overlapping rules produce the first proved matching rule; unknown earlier predicates and ineligible selected targets do not silently fall through.

#### Scenario: US27-A05 AC27-A07-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A07.

- **GIVEN** a current assignment that is eligible, or a separate case with missing or ineligible current responsibility;
- **WHEN** evaluation finds no rule, an unavailable required input or a missing or ineligible selected target;
- **THEN** No match or missing target preserves eligible current responsibility and exposes a permitted reason/repair action; missing current responsibility is not called healthy.

#### Scenario: US27-A05 AC27-A02-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-A02, REQ27-A09.

- **GIVEN** candidate staff differ in Tenant, role eligibility, current access and supported target-resolution basis;
- **WHEN** staff search/select or a rule resolves a receiving person, including an ambiguous multi-candidate result;
- **THEN** Target choices obey current staff/role/tenant/source eligibility and qualified deterministic resolution; multiple candidates are not resolved by database order.

### Requirement: US27-A06 — Prevent cyclic or irrelevant rule evaluations

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an assignment-policy maintainer, I want to prevent cyclic or irrelevant rule evaluations, so that I can avoid repeated transfers and unnecessary work. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A02, REQ27-A15.

#### Scenario: US27-A06 AC27-A02-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-A02, REQ27-A15.

- **GIVEN** an attempted output-dependent policy, duplicate events and irrelevant source-field changes;
- **WHEN** configuration is validated and admitted source events are processed repeatedly;
- **THEN** Assignment outputs cannot trigger cyclic routing; duplicate irrelevant source updates do not produce new transfers/proposals/tasks.

### Requirement: US27-A07 — Transfer only one named role-qualified responsibility

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a development staff member, I want to transfer only one named role-qualified responsibility, so that I can preserve colleagues, other roles and the authoritative primary. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A03, REQ27-A10.

#### Scenario: US27-A07 AC27-A03-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A03, REQ27-A10.

- **GIVEN** one staff person holds two roles for one Party and multiple staff legitimately share a role;
- **WHEN** one named responsibility is transferred, a duplicate copy or cross-Tenant reference is attempted, and a target already holding a compatible assignment is encountered;
- **THEN** Same staff/Party can hold distinct roles; replacing one does not erase the other. Duplicate same-role source identities and cross-tenant references fail at admitted database paths.

#### Scenario: US27-A07 AC27-A03-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-A03, REQ27-A10, REQ27-A11.

- **GIVEN** a current primary and several assignments, plus a separate case losing its final eligible assignee;
- **WHEN** independent source connections concurrently change primary, replace an assignment or remove responsibility;
- **THEN** Concurrent primary/replacement/removal produces one valid source result or conflict; primary remains a valid assignee, or the source's honest unassigned/repair state is used.

#### Scenario: US27-A07 AC27-A03-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-A03, REQ27-A09.

- **GIVEN** legacy missionary assignment, role membership, a generic Owner display and saved portfolio views coexist with canonical assignment edges;
- **WHEN** every supported assignment writer and read projection identifies or changes staff responsibility;
- **THEN** No writer or read model treats legacy missionary ID, membership role, display Owner or a saved portfolio view as P9 assignment authority.

### Requirement: US27-A08 — Change or remove primary responsibility through the exact source operation

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an assignment manager, I want to change or remove primary responsibility through the exact source operation, so that I can avoid contradictory primaries or a fabricated successor. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A03, REQ27-A07, REQ27-A10, REQ27-A11.

#### Scenario: US27-A08 AC27-A03-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-A03, REQ27-A10, REQ27-A11.

- **GIVEN** a current primary and several assignments, plus a separate case losing its final eligible assignee;
- **WHEN** independent source connections concurrently change primary, replace an assignment or remove responsibility;
- **THEN** Concurrent primary/replacement/removal produces one valid source result or conflict; primary remains a valid assignee, or the source's honest unassigned/repair state is used.

#### Scenario: US27-A08 AC27-A07-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-A07, REQ27-A08, REQ27-A09.

- **GIVEN** a staff member has active responsibility and outstanding work or handoff;
- **WHEN** the identity or permission owner deactivates the person or revokes the applicable authority while transfer review is incomplete;
- **THEN** Staff departure/revocation acts immediately despite pending review/handoff; no successor is inferred from directory fields and no ineligible assignee appears as healthy current coverage.

### Requirement: US27-A09 — Protect a chosen person or an intentional vacancy from rules

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a development staff member, I want to protect a chosen person or an intentional vacancy from rules, so that I can retain deliberate relationship responsibility until I explicitly change control. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A04.

#### Scenario: US27-A09 AC27-A04-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A04.

- **GIVEN** one protected manual assignment, one intentional vacancy and one imported responsibility with unknown management provenance;
- **WHEN** initial routing, automatic replacement, rule edits and delayed retries encounter those responsibilities;
- **THEN** A manually assigned record, deliberately unassigned role and imported unknown-provenance record are not moved or refilled by initial routing, Auto, rule edit or replay.

#### Scenario: US27-A09 AC27-A04-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-A04, REQ27-A11.

- **GIVEN** a person is currently assigned and an automatic transfer has been evaluated or queued;
- **WHEN** authorized staff select Keep this assignment without changing the current person and the queued operation subsequently attempts acceptance;
- **THEN** Keep current person creates durable protected intent despite an unchanged assignee ID; concurrent/queued automation cannot erase it.

### Requirement: US27-A10 — Keep the current person as a protected manual choice

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a development staff member, I want to keep the current person as a protected manual choice, so that I can stop automatic replacement even when the assignee name stays unchanged. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A04, REQ27-A11.

#### Scenario: US27-A10 AC27-A04-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-A04, REQ27-A11.

- **GIVEN** a person is currently assigned and an automatic transfer has been evaluated or queued;
- **WHEN** authorized staff select Keep this assignment without changing the current person and the queued operation subsequently attempts acceptance;
- **THEN** Keep current person creates durable protected intent despite an unchanged assignee ID; concurrent/queued automation cannot erase it.

#### Scenario: US27-A10 AC27-A04-04

**Requirements:** REQ27-A04, REQ27-A11, REQ27-A12.

- **GIVEN** manual Keep, intentional unassignment, transfer, policy edit/disable and revocation can race with automatic acceptance;
- **WHEN** independent database connections force each relevant competing commit order;
- **THEN** Race manual Keep/unassign/transfer against Auto; race policy edit/disable and revocation against commit using independent connections. Only an outcome valid under the proved serialization order survives.

### Requirement: US27-A11 — Return a protected responsibility to rules with its current effect explained

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a development staff member, I want to return a protected responsibility to rules with its current effect explained, so that I can deliberately resume the chosen policy without guessing what will change. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A04, REQ27-A07.

#### Scenario: US27-A11 AC27-A04-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-A04, REQ27-A07.

- **GIVEN** a protected manual assignment and a visible current rule result;
- **WHEN** authorized staff choose Use rules again under Review changes, under Apply changes automatically and with no qualified match;
- **THEN** Use rules again clearly releases protection: Review produces a current proposal; Auto can apply the displayed current eligible result. No match does not silently clear valid responsibility.

#### Scenario: US27-A11 AC27-A07-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A07.

- **GIVEN** a current assignment that is eligible, or a separate case with missing or ineligible current responsibility;
- **WHEN** evaluation finds no rule, an unavailable required input or a missing or ineligible selected target;
- **THEN** No match or missing target preserves eligible current responsibility and exposes a permitted reason/repair action; missing current responsibility is not called healthy.

### Requirement: US27-A12 — Save inert drafts and activate policy prospectively

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a Tenant administrator, I want to save inert drafts and activate policy prospectively, so that I can avoid unexpectedly moving current assignments while maintaining the rules. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A01, REQ27-A05, REQ27-A14.

#### Scenario: US27-A12 AC27-A05-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A05, REQ27-A14.

- **GIVEN** editable rule drafts, an active policy and existing matching assignments;
- **WHEN** staff save a draft, activate or edit policy, change mode and separately review application to current assignments;
- **THEN** Draft save, activation, current-population application and mode change have the exact disclosed effects; enabling does not silently move all current matches.

#### Scenario: US27-A12 AC27-A01-04

**Requirements:** REQ27-A01, REQ27-A14.

- **GIVEN** an authorized edited settings draft and an independently changing saved policy;
- **WHEN** a background refresh returns a newer version or settings retrieval fails;
- **THEN** Concurrent settings refresh preserves an edited authorized draft and shows conflict; unreadable settings never render as the saved default or authorize Auto.

#### Scenario: US27-A12 AC27-A05-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-A05, REQ27-A11, REQ27-A12.

- **GIVEN** one source event is admitted before activation of policy P2 and another after activation;
- **WHEN** their workers are delayed or reordered, including updates with earlier business occurrence dates and separate explicit current-application or Use rules again operations;
- **THEN** Delay workers for source events committed before and after policy activation: the earlier event cannot execute the retired policy or be rebound to the new policy; the later qualified event may use the current policy after revalidation. Explicit current-application/Use rules again is distinct. Backdated business dates and transport arrival cannot change the authoritative source order or invent historical responsibility.

### Requirement: US27-A13 — Review current matches and apply one finite selected set

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an assignment manager, I want to review current matches and apply one finite selected set, so that I can update existing responsibilities without an unseen population sweep. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A05, REQ27-A06.

#### Scenario: US27-A13 AC27-A05-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A05, REQ27-A14.

- **GIVEN** editable rule drafts, an active policy and existing matching assignments;
- **WHEN** staff save a draft, activate or edit policy, change mode and separately review application to current assignments;
- **THEN** Draft save, activation, current-population application and mode change have the exact disclosed effects; enabling does not silently move all current matches.

#### Scenario: US27-A13 AC27-A05-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-A05, REQ27-A06.

- **GIVEN** a reviewed finite group with captured identities, expected revisions and intended destinations;
- **WHEN** the filter, rule, target or matching population changes before acceptance or retry;
- **THEN** A reviewed group contains only its finite original identities and intended results; filter/rule/target changes cannot substitute records or enlarge scope before apply.

#### Scenario: US27-A13 AC27-A06-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A06, REQ27-A11, REQ27-A15, REQ27-A16.

- **GIVEN** a finite reviewed group contains successful, conflicting, failed and unattempted members;
- **WHEN** execution times out, is canceled, resumes or receives duplicate delivery after partial acceptance;
- **THEN** Bulk partial success, cancel, retry and timeout expose exact permitted member results, preserve committed changes and resume without duplicates or unseen expansion.

### Requirement: US27-A14 — Compare actual and proposed responsibility and supersede stale proposals

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a development staff member, I want to compare actual and proposed responsibility and supersede stale proposals, so that I can make a current deliberate transfer rather than apply an outdated suggestion. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A05, REQ27-A06, REQ27-A14.

#### Scenario: US27-A14 AC27-A05-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-A05, REQ27-A06.

- **GIVEN** a reviewed finite group with captured identities, expected revisions and intended destinations;
- **WHEN** the filter, rule, target or matching population changes before acceptance or retry;
- **THEN** A reviewed group contains only its finite original identities and intended results; filter/rule/target changes cannot substitute records or enlarge scope before apply.

#### Scenario: US27-A14 AC27-A01-04

**Requirements:** REQ27-A01, REQ27-A14.

- **GIVEN** an authorized edited settings draft and an independently changing saved policy;
- **WHEN** a background refresh returns a newer version or settings retrieval fails;
- **THEN** Concurrent settings refresh preserves an edited authorized draft and shows conflict; unreadable settings never render as the saved default or authorize Auto.

#### Scenario: US27-A14 AC27-A14-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A14, REQ27-A18.

- **GIVEN** representative staff and administrators use the qualified complete assignment interface;
- **WHEN** they identify current/proposed responsibility, policy scope and manual protection and perform Apply, Keep, Use rules again and disable without facilitator correction;
- **THEN** Representative staff correctly identify current versus proposed assignee, role, manual protection, Auto scope and effects of Apply/Keep/Use rules again/disable without facilitator correction. Any critical meaning/action error blocks pilot acceptance.

### Requirement: US27-A15 — Recover partial group results and cancel only unaccepted members

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a development staff member, I want to recover partial group results and cancel only unaccepted members, so that I can finish authorized changes without repeating successful transfers. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A06, REQ27-A11, REQ27-A15, REQ27-A16.

#### Scenario: US27-A15 AC27-A05-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-A05, REQ27-A06.

- **GIVEN** a reviewed finite group with captured identities, expected revisions and intended destinations;
- **WHEN** the filter, rule, target or matching population changes before acceptance or retry;
- **THEN** A reviewed group contains only its finite original identities and intended results; filter/rule/target changes cannot substitute records or enlarge scope before apply.

#### Scenario: US27-A15 AC27-A06-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A06, REQ27-A11, REQ27-A15, REQ27-A16.

- **GIVEN** a finite reviewed group contains successful, conflicting, failed and unattempted members;
- **WHEN** execution times out, is canceled, resumes or receives duplicate delivery after partial acceptance;
- **THEN** Bulk partial success, cancel, retry and timeout expose exact permitted member results, preserve committed changes and resume without duplicates or unseen expansion.

### Requirement: US27-A16 — Disable and later re-enable automatic replacement prospectively

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a Tenant administrator, I want to disable and later re-enable automatic replacement prospectively, so that I can stop pending automatic changes without reversing completed work or replaying a backlog. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A05, REQ27-A11, REQ27-A12.

#### Scenario: US27-A16 AC27-A01-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A01.

- **GIVEN** a new Tenant and two independently declared assignment-policy scopes;
- **WHEN** an authorized administrator reads the saved mode, enables automatic changes for one scope, then returns that scope to Review changes;
- **THEN** New tenant uses reviewed existing-assignment transfers; tenant can explicitly enable Auto for a declared bounded scope and safely return to Review. Other scopes keep their own stated mode.

#### Scenario: US27-A16 AC27-A11-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-A11, REQ27-A12, REQ27-A17.

- **GIVEN** queued decisions and callbacks from earlier policy generations exist, including a restored snapshot;
- **WHEN** automatic changes are disabled and re-enabled, and stale, duplicate or out-of-order work is delivered;
- **THEN** Disable/re-enable, stale queued events, duplicate/out-of-order callbacks and replay after restore cannot apply old desired-owner decisions or revive old grants/tasks.

#### Scenario: US27-A16 AC27-A05-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-A05, REQ27-A11, REQ27-A12.

- **GIVEN** one source event is admitted before activation of policy P2 and another after activation;
- **WHEN** their workers are delayed or reordered, including updates with earlier business occurrence dates and separate explicit current-application or Use rules again operations;
- **THEN** Delay workers for source events committed before and after policy activation: the earlier event cannot execute the retired policy or be rebound to the new policy; the later qualified event may use the current policy after revalidation. Explicit current-application/Use rules again is distinct. Backdated business dates and transport arrival cannot change the authoritative source order or invent historical responsibility.

#### Scenario: US27-A16 AC27-A12-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A12, REQ27-A17.

- **GIVEN** a bounded pilot has accepted new assignments and protected manual choices under the new model;
- **WHEN** operators disable automatic acceptance, roll back compatible components and restore retained state;
- **THEN** Pilot disable/kill switch, mixed-version rollback and restore after new writes retain history/manual protections, fence stale work and preserve independent obligations.

### Requirement: US27-A17 — Order source events and policy changes by authoritative admission

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an assignment source owner, I want to order source events and policy changes by authoritative admission, so that I can prevent worker timing or backdated business dates from changing policy meaning. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A05, REQ27-A11, REQ27-A12.

#### Scenario: US27-A17 AC27-A04-04

**Requirements:** REQ27-A04, REQ27-A11, REQ27-A12.

- **GIVEN** manual Keep, intentional unassignment, transfer, policy edit/disable and revocation can race with automatic acceptance;
- **WHEN** independent database connections force each relevant competing commit order;
- **THEN** Race manual Keep/unassign/transfer against Auto; race policy edit/disable and revocation against commit using independent connections. Only an outcome valid under the proved serialization order survives.

#### Scenario: US27-A17 AC27-A11-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-A11, REQ27-A12, REQ27-A17.

- **GIVEN** queued decisions and callbacks from earlier policy generations exist, including a restored snapshot;
- **WHEN** automatic changes are disabled and re-enabled, and stale, duplicate or out-of-order work is delivered;
- **THEN** Disable/re-enable, stale queued events, duplicate/out-of-order callbacks and replay after restore cannot apply old desired-owner decisions or revive old grants/tasks.

#### Scenario: US27-A17 AC27-A05-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-A05, REQ27-A11, REQ27-A12.

- **GIVEN** one source event is admitted before activation of policy P2 and another after activation;
- **WHEN** their workers are delayed or reordered, including updates with earlier business occurrence dates and separate explicit current-application or Use rules again operations;
- **THEN** Delay workers for source events committed before and after policy activation: the earlier event cannot execute the retired policy or be rebound to the new policy; the later qualified event may use the current policy after revalidation. Explicit current-application/Use rules again is distinct. Backdated business dates and transport arrival cannot change the authoritative source order or invent historical responsibility.

### Requirement: US27-A18 — Retain independent partnership, Request and task responsibility during a representative transfer

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a development operations lead, I want to retain independent partnership, Request and task responsibility during a representative transfer, so that I can keep genuine obligations with their actual lawful owners. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A07, REQ27-A08, REQ27-A09.

#### Scenario: US27-A18 AC27-A08-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A08, REQ27-A09.

- **GIVEN** a Party has independently owned partnership work, Requests, tasks, access and financial obligations;
- **WHEN** a representative transfer is accepted or a transfer proposal is created;
- **THEN** Representative transfer does not move partnership/Request/task ownership, grant access, start cultivation, send donor messages or generate a task for every proposal.

#### Scenario: US27-A18 AC27-A07-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-A07, REQ27-A08.

- **GIVEN** affected work has a qualified owner condition requiring acceptance before transfer, or a different condition allowing acknowledgment afterward;
- **WHEN** the proposed representative change is attempted with and without the exact prerequisite or durable owned handoff disposition;
- **THEN** Where affected required work would lose eligible responsibility, require lawful source disposition/durable owned handoff. Source-required pre-transfer acceptance gates assignment commit; subsequent acknowledgment remains visibly outstanding after lawful assignment plus durable intent. Neither acknowledgment nor handoff completion is fabricated.

#### Scenario: US27-A18 AC27-A07-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-A07, REQ27-A08, REQ27-A09.

- **GIVEN** a staff member has active responsibility and outstanding work or handoff;
- **WHEN** the identity or permission owner deactivates the person or revokes the applicable authority while transfer review is incomplete;
- **THEN** Staff departure/revocation acts immediately despite pending review/handoff; no successor is inferred from directory fields and no ineligible assignee appears as healthy current coverage.

### Requirement: US27-A19 — See whether required handoff acceptance precedes transfer or acknowledgment remains outstanding

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a receiving staff member, I want to see whether required handoff acceptance precedes transfer or acknowledgment remains outstanding, so that I can accept actual source work without a false completion claim or universal ceremony. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A07, REQ27-A08.

#### Scenario: US27-A19 AC27-A07-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-A07, REQ27-A08.

- **GIVEN** affected work has a qualified owner condition requiring acceptance before transfer, or a different condition allowing acknowledgment afterward;
- **WHEN** the proposed representative change is attempted with and without the exact prerequisite or durable owned handoff disposition;
- **THEN** Where affected required work would lose eligible responsibility, require lawful source disposition/durable owned handoff. Source-required pre-transfer acceptance gates assignment commit; subsequent acknowledgment remains visibly outstanding after lawful assignment plus durable intent. Neither acknowledgment nor handoff completion is fabricated.

### Requirement: US27-A20 — Revoke departed staff access immediately while responsibility repair continues

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a security administrator, I want to revoke departed staff access immediately while responsibility repair continues, so that I can stop unauthorized access without abandoning source obligations. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A07, REQ27-A08, REQ27-A09.

#### Scenario: US27-A20 AC27-A09-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A09, REQ27-A10, REQ27-A11.

- **GIVEN** real authenticated principals, an automatic executor with a qualified human owner and all admitted mutation entry points;
- **WHEN** API, direct database role, RPC, import, worker and privileged-service callers forge authority fields or execute after their authority is revoked;
- **THEN** API, direct admitted DB role, RPC, import, worker and service-role tests reject forged tenant/actor/role/primary/policy IDs and unauthorized current-state changes. Revoked NHI owner authority blocks automatic execution despite historical policy approval.

#### Scenario: US27-A20 AC27-A07-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-A07, REQ27-A08.

- **GIVEN** affected work has a qualified owner condition requiring acceptance before transfer, or a different condition allowing acknowledgment afterward;
- **WHEN** the proposed representative change is attempted with and without the exact prerequisite or durable owned handoff disposition;
- **THEN** Where affected required work would lose eligible responsibility, require lawful source disposition/durable owned handoff. Source-required pre-transfer acceptance gates assignment commit; subsequent acknowledgment remains visibly outstanding after lawful assignment plus durable intent. Neither acknowledgment nor handoff completion is fabricated.

#### Scenario: US27-A20 AC27-A07-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-A07, REQ27-A08, REQ27-A09.

- **GIVEN** a staff member has active responsibility and outstanding work or handoff;
- **WHEN** the identity or permission owner deactivates the person or revokes the applicable authority while transfer review is incomplete;
- **THEN** Staff departure/revocation acts immediately despite pending review/handoff; no successor is inferred from directory fields and no ineligible assignee appears as healthy current coverage.

### Requirement: US27-A21 — Enforce separate current policy, transfer and recipient powers through every entry point

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a Tenant security owner, I want to enforce separate current policy, transfer and recipient powers through every entry point, so that I can prevent forged or privileged requests from bypassing exact scope. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A09, REQ27-A10, REQ27-A11.

#### Scenario: US27-A21 AC27-A09-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A09, REQ27-A10, REQ27-A11.

- **GIVEN** real authenticated principals, an automatic executor with a qualified human owner and all admitted mutation entry points;
- **WHEN** API, direct database role, RPC, import, worker and privileged-service callers forge authority fields or execute after their authority is revoked;
- **THEN** API, direct admitted DB role, RPC, import, worker and service-role tests reject forged tenant/actor/role/primary/policy IDs and unauthorized current-state changes. Revoked NHI owner authority blocks automatic execution despite historical policy approval.

#### Scenario: US27-A21 AC27-A09-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-A09, REQ27-A10.

- **GIVEN** a caller may reach an existing row but may not produce every possible resulting assignment state;
- **WHEN** direct admitted-role and service-path updates attempt forbidden Tenant, subject, recipient, role, control or scope changes;
- **THEN** A permitted old-row update cannot create an unauthorized new tenant, subject, recipient, role, control state or scope; effective USING/WITH CHECK, grants and bypass paths are proved.

#### Scenario: US27-A21 AC27-A09-04

**Requirements:** REQ27-A09, REQ27-A11, REQ27-A13.

- **GIVEN** a permitted preview, resumable draft or operation receipt exists;
- **WHEN** Tenant context changes, permissions are revoked or the session expires before apply, result recovery, export or download;
- **THEN** Tenant switch, revoked permission and expired session between preview and apply/download narrow or reject current output; cached drafts/receipts cannot leak or replay across tenants.

### Requirement: US27-A22 — Run assignment changes only under current bounded execution authority

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an automation operator, I want to run assignment changes only under current bounded execution authority, so that I can preserve human approval lineage without impersonation or stale grants. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A09, REQ27-A11.

#### Scenario: US27-A22 AC27-A09-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A09, REQ27-A10, REQ27-A11.

- **GIVEN** real authenticated principals, an automatic executor with a qualified human owner and all admitted mutation entry points;
- **WHEN** API, direct database role, RPC, import, worker and privileged-service callers forge authority fields or execute after their authority is revoked;
- **THEN** API, direct admitted DB role, RPC, import, worker and service-role tests reject forged tenant/actor/role/primary/policy IDs and unauthorized current-state changes. Revoked NHI owner authority blocks automatic execution despite historical policy approval.

#### Scenario: US27-A22 AC27-A04-04

**Requirements:** REQ27-A04, REQ27-A11, REQ27-A12.

- **GIVEN** manual Keep, intentional unassignment, transfer, policy edit/disable and revocation can race with automatic acceptance;
- **WHEN** independent database connections force each relevant competing commit order;
- **THEN** Race manual Keep/unassign/transfer against Auto; race policy edit/disable and revocation against commit using independent connections. Only an outcome valid under the proved serialization order survives.

#### Scenario: US27-A22 AC27-A11-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-A11, REQ27-A12, REQ27-A17.

- **GIVEN** queued decisions and callbacks from earlier policy generations exist, including a restored snapshot;
- **WHEN** automatic changes are disabled and re-enabled, and stale, duplicate or out-of-order work is delivered;
- **THEN** Disable/re-enable, stale queued events, duplicate/out-of-order callbacks and replay after restore cannot apply old desired-owner decisions or revive old grants/tasks.

### Requirement: US27-A23 — Receive permission-safe candidates, reasons, totals and history

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized assignment reader, I want to receive permission-safe candidates, reasons, totals and history, so that I can understand work without exposing private people, source facts or restricted duties. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A09, REQ27-A13.

#### Scenario: US27-A23 AC27-A09-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-A09, REQ27-A13.

- **GIVEN** some candidate staff, work, matching inputs and history are unavailable to the current viewer;
- **WHEN** the viewer searches, filters, counts, orders, previews, explains, exports or consumes cached/derived assignment data;
- **THEN** Target search, preview counts/differences, reasons, sorting, history, exports, caches, AI/briefs and notifications reveal no unauthorized people, work or rule inputs.

#### Scenario: US27-A23 AC27-A09-04

**Requirements:** REQ27-A09, REQ27-A11, REQ27-A13.

- **GIVEN** a permitted preview, resumable draft or operation receipt exists;
- **WHEN** Tenant context changes, permissions are revoked or the session expires before apply, result recovery, export or download;
- **THEN** Tenant switch, revoked permission and expired session between preview and apply/download narrow or reject current output; cached drafts/receipts cannot leak or replay across tenants.

#### Scenario: US27-A23 AC27-A10-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-A10, REQ27-A13, REQ27-A16.

- **GIVEN** source history and diagnostics include assignment reasons and references to protected information;
- **WHEN** logs, notifications, lawful exports, retention/redaction and backup recovery are exercised;
- **THEN** Logs/notifications omit sensitive rule input and work content; lawful retention/redaction/export/backup recovery does not resurrect deleted protected text.

### Requirement: US27-A24 — Retain safe context and drafts across changes in access or Tenant

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a development staff member, I want to retain safe context and drafts across changes in access or Tenant, so that I can recover appropriately without leaking or replaying stale information. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A11, REQ27-A13, REQ27-A14.

#### Scenario: US27-A24 AC27-A01-04

**Requirements:** REQ27-A01, REQ27-A14.

- **GIVEN** an authorized edited settings draft and an independently changing saved policy;
- **WHEN** a background refresh returns a newer version or settings retrieval fails;
- **THEN** Concurrent settings refresh preserves an edited authorized draft and shows conflict; unreadable settings never render as the saved default or authorize Auto.

#### Scenario: US27-A24 AC27-A09-04

**Requirements:** REQ27-A09, REQ27-A11, REQ27-A13.

- **GIVEN** a permitted preview, resumable draft or operation receipt exists;
- **WHEN** Tenant context changes, permissions are revoked or the session expires before apply, result recovery, export or download;
- **THEN** Tenant switch, revoked permission and expired session between preview and apply/download narrow or reject current output; cached drafts/receipts cannot leak or replay across tenants.

#### Scenario: US27-A24 AC27-A11-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-A11, REQ27-A13, REQ27-A14.

- **GIVEN** a mobile or throttled connection and an authorized in-progress edit or transfer;
- **WHEN** requests slow, responses arrive late or are lost, navigation/reconnect occurs and access is subsequently removed;
- **THEN** Throttled/mobile and lost-response journeys keep essential context/actions usable, preserve permitted edits and reconcile without duplicate transfer; access loss clears disallowed local data.

### Requirement: US27-A25 — Commit the assignment, control, primary, history and required intent coherently

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an assignment source owner, I want to commit the assignment, control, primary, history and required intent coherently, so that I can avoid partial responsibility changes when a save fails. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A10, REQ27-A11, REQ27-A16.

#### Scenario: US27-A25 AC27-A09-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-A09, REQ27-A10.

- **GIVEN** a caller may reach an existing row but may not produce every possible resulting assignment state;
- **WHEN** direct admitted-role and service-path updates attempt forbidden Tenant, subject, recipient, role, control or scope changes;
- **THEN** A permitted old-row update cannot create an unauthorized new tenant, subject, recipient, role, control state or scope; effective USING/WITH CHECK, grants and bypass paths are proved.

#### Scenario: US27-A25 AC27-A10-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A10, REQ27-A11, REQ27-A16.

- **GIVEN** a valid transfer includes required assignment, control, primary, history, receipt and durable effect intent;
- **WHEN** failure is injected before authoritative commit, then in a separate case after commit but before projection publication;
- **THEN** Fault injection before authoritative commit proves assignment/control/primary/history/receipt/effect intent all-or-none; after-commit projection failure preserves source truth.

### Requirement: US27-A26 — Recover a lost response using the same operation and inspect historical results honestly

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a development staff member, I want to recover a lost response using the same operation and inspect historical results honestly, so that I can avoid duplicate transfers or overwriting later choices. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A11, REQ27-A12, REQ27-A16.

#### Scenario: US27-A26 AC27-A10-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A10, REQ27-A11, REQ27-A16.

- **GIVEN** a valid transfer includes required assignment, control, primary, history, receipt and durable effect intent;
- **WHEN** failure is injected before authoritative commit, then in a separate case after commit but before projection publication;
- **THEN** Fault injection before authoritative commit proves assignment/control/primary/history/receipt/effect intent all-or-none; after-commit projection failure preserves source truth.

#### Scenario: US27-A26 AC27-A11-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A11, REQ27-A12.

- **GIVEN** a transfer was accepted but its response was lost, followed by a later valid assignment or protection change;
- **WHEN** the original intent is retried and a distinct later legitimate transfer is subsequently requested;
- **THEN** Retrying a lost response returns the same current-authorized operation result and creates one business effect; a later legitimate transfer remains possible.

#### Scenario: US27-A26 AC27-A06-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A06, REQ27-A11, REQ27-A15, REQ27-A16.

- **GIVEN** a finite reviewed group contains successful, conflicting, failed and unattempted members;
- **WHEN** execution times out, is canceled, resumes or receives duplicate delivery after partial acceptance;
- **THEN** Bulk partial success, cancel, retry and timeout expose exact permitted member results, preserve committed changes and resume without duplicates or unseen expansion.

### Requirement: US27-A27 — Correct responsibility or return to a previous staff member as a new authorized transition

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an assignment manager, I want to correct responsibility or return to a previous staff member as a new authorized transition, so that I can preserve history and later restrictions instead of blindly undoing them. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A03, REQ27-A04, REQ27-A12.

#### Scenario: US27-A27 AC27-A03-04

**Requirements:** REQ27-A03, REQ27-A04, REQ27-A12.

- **GIVEN** ended assignments, previous manual protections and intervening later decisions exist;
- **WHEN** a permitted reader inspects history or authorized staff correct responsibility and later return to a previously assigned person;
- **THEN** Ended assignments and prior manual protections retain lawful actor/time/role history; correction and return are new transitions, not edits to historical identity.

#### Scenario: US27-A27 AC27-A11-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A11, REQ27-A12.

- **GIVEN** a transfer was accepted but its response was lost, followed by a later valid assignment or protection change;
- **WHEN** the original intent is retried and a distinct later legitimate transfer is subsequently requested;
- **THEN** Retrying a lost response returns the same current-authorized operation result and creates one business effect; a later legitimate transfer remains possible.

#### Scenario: US27-A27 AC27-A03-05

**Requirements:** REQ27-A03, REQ27-A04, REQ27-A12, REQ27-A17.

- **GIVEN** merged Parties or imported/legacy rows contain conflicting manual, unknown and rule-managed provenance;
- **WHEN** qualified source identity repair, migration or merge/Undo handles the affected assignment references;
- **THEN** Merge/import/legacy provenance conflicts preserve protected choices and source work; do not mark every surviving edge rule-managed or guess a primary.

### Requirement: US27-A28 — Repair merged or imported assignment references without guessing provenance

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a data steward, I want to repair merged or imported assignment references without guessing provenance, so that I can preserve protected choices, roles, financial history and actual remaining work. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A03, REQ27-A04, REQ27-A12, REQ27-A17.

#### Scenario: US27-A28 AC27-A03-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A03, REQ27-A10.

- **GIVEN** one staff person holds two roles for one Party and multiple staff legitimately share a role;
- **WHEN** one named responsibility is transferred, a duplicate copy or cross-Tenant reference is attempted, and a target already holding a compatible assignment is encountered;
- **THEN** Same staff/Party can hold distinct roles; replacing one does not erase the other. Duplicate same-role source identities and cross-tenant references fail at admitted database paths.

#### Scenario: US27-A28 AC27-A03-04

**Requirements:** REQ27-A03, REQ27-A04, REQ27-A12.

- **GIVEN** ended assignments, previous manual protections and intervening later decisions exist;
- **WHEN** a permitted reader inspects history or authorized staff correct responsibility and later return to a previously assigned person;
- **THEN** Ended assignments and prior manual protections retain lawful actor/time/role history; correction and return are new transitions, not edits to historical identity.

#### Scenario: US27-A28 AC27-A03-05

**Requirements:** REQ27-A03, REQ27-A04, REQ27-A12, REQ27-A17.

- **GIVEN** merged Parties or imported/legacy rows contain conflicting manual, unknown and rule-managed provenance;
- **WHEN** qualified source identity repair, migration or merge/Undo handles the affected assignment references;
- **THEN** Merge/import/legacy provenance conflicts preserve protected choices and source work; do not mark every surviving edge rule-managed or guess a primary.

#### Scenario: US27-A28 AC27-A17-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A17.

- **GIVEN** retained assignment data, old readers/writers, role collisions and unknown provenance have been inventoried;
- **WHEN** compatible constraints/readers are installed and resumable idempotent backfill and mixed-version transition are exercised;
- **THEN** Retained-data census and idempotent backfill preserve unknown/manual provenance and roles, cause no assignment/outward/access effects, and safely handle old/new readers and writers.

### Requirement: US27-A29 — Use clear accessible settings, review, transfer and recovery flows on mobile or slow connections

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a development staff member, I want to use clear accessible settings, review, transfer and recovery flows on mobile or slow connections, so that I can understand current responsibility and complete the intended action without workarounds. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A14, REQ27-A18.

#### Scenario: US27-A29 AC27-A14-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A14, REQ27-A18.

- **GIVEN** representative staff and administrators use the qualified complete assignment interface;
- **WHEN** they identify current/proposed responsibility, policy scope and manual protection and perform Apply, Keep, Use rules again and disable without facilitator correction;
- **THEN** Representative staff correctly identify current versus proposed assignee, role, manual protection, Auto scope and effects of Apply/Keep/Use rules again/disable without facilitator correction. Any critical meaning/action error blocks pilot acceptance.

#### Scenario: US27-A29 AC27-A14-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-A14.

- **GIVEN** the actual composed settings, ordered rules, preview and transfer/recovery interfaces are available;
- **WHEN** keyboard and screen-reader users complete individual and finite-group workflows, including conflict and partial success;
- **THEN** Keyboard/focus and screen-reader checks cover settings, ordering, previews, grouped apply, conflict, partial results and recovery; automated checks supplement manual verification.

#### Scenario: US27-A29 AC27-A14-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-A14.

- **GIVEN** actual interfaces contain long Unicode names, translated and RTL labels and light/dark themes;
- **WHEN** users zoom or work at applicable 320 CSS-pixel reflow and operate all controls by keyboard and touch;
- **THEN** At applicable 320 CSS px reflow/zoom, long Unicode/RTL/translated names and labels stay readable; target sizes and light/dark contrast meet WCAG 2.2 AA without drag-only or color-only operation.

#### Scenario: US27-A29 AC27-A11-03

**Requirements:** REQ27-A11, REQ27-A13, REQ27-A14.

- **GIVEN** a mobile or throttled connection and an authorized in-progress edit or transfer;
- **WHEN** requests slow, responses arrive late or are lost, navigation/reconnect occurs and access is subsequently removed;
- **THEN** Throttled/mobile and lost-response journeys keep essential context/actions usable, preserve permitted edits and reconcile without duplicate transfer; access loss clears disallowed local data.

### Requirement: US27-A30 — Inspect safe diagnostics, measure capacity and recover source failures

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an operations lead, I want to inspect safe diagnostics, measure capacity and recover source failures, so that I can operate a bounded reliable service without database edits or noisy duplicate tasks. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A13, REQ27-A15, REQ27-A16, REQ27-A18.

#### Scenario: US27-A30 AC27-A10-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A10, REQ27-A11, REQ27-A16.

- **GIVEN** a valid transfer includes required assignment, control, primary, history, receipt and durable effect intent;
- **WHEN** failure is injected before authoritative commit, then in a separate case after commit but before projection publication;
- **THEN** Fault injection before authoritative commit proves assignment/control/primary/history/receipt/effect intent all-or-none; after-commit projection failure preserves source truth.

#### Scenario: US27-A30 AC27-A06-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A06, REQ27-A11, REQ27-A15, REQ27-A16.

- **GIVEN** a finite reviewed group contains successful, conflicting, failed and unattempted members;
- **WHEN** execution times out, is canceled, resumes or receives duplicate delivery after partial acceptance;
- **THEN** Bulk partial success, cancel, retry and timeout expose exact permitted member results, preserve committed changes and resume without duplicates or unseen expansion.

#### Scenario: US27-A30 AC27-A10-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-A10, REQ27-A13, REQ27-A16.

- **GIVEN** source history and diagnostics include assignment reasons and references to protected information;
- **WHEN** logs, notifications, lawful exports, retention/redaction and backup recovery are exercised;
- **THEN** Logs/notifications omit sensitive rule input and work content; lawful retention/redaction/export/backup recovery does not resurrect deleted protected text.

#### Scenario: US27-A30 AC27-A15-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A15.

- **GIVEN** production-shaped source data and real authorization represent the proposed deployment envelope;
- **WHEN** the qualification team measures policy evaluation, proposal paging, preview aggregation, concurrent editing and finite-group application;
- **THEN** Publish a measured envelope for tenant/role/rule counts, overlap, largest portfolio, concurrent editing/bulk, p95/error/freshness and indexed query plans using real source authorization; incomplete preview coverage is disclosed.

#### Scenario: US27-A30 AC27-A18-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A18.

- **GIVEN** the ratified decision, glossary, formal owners and this specification are versioned;
- **WHEN** an independent reviewer follows every D30 requirement and acceptance outcome through design, future delivery work and release evidence;
- **THEN** Trace Q30 → D30 → glossary → exact owner/OpenSpec amendments → design/tasks/issues → source/security/concurrency/UI tests → release evidence, distinguishing selected/reviewed/ratified/implemented/qualified states.

### Requirement: US27-A31 — Migrate, pilot, disable and restore assignment features without side effects

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a release operator, I want to migrate, pilot, disable and restore assignment features without side effects, so that I can preserve accepted history and protections across old and new components. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A12, REQ27-A17.

#### Scenario: US27-A31 AC27-A11-02

**Requirements:** REQ27-A11, REQ27-A12, REQ27-A17.

- **GIVEN** queued decisions and callbacks from earlier policy generations exist, including a restored snapshot;
- **WHEN** automatic changes are disabled and re-enabled, and stale, duplicate or out-of-order work is delivered;
- **THEN** Disable/re-enable, stale queued events, duplicate/out-of-order callbacks and replay after restore cannot apply old desired-owner decisions or revive old grants/tasks.

#### Scenario: US27-A31 AC27-A03-05

**Requirements:** REQ27-A03, REQ27-A04, REQ27-A12, REQ27-A17.

- **GIVEN** merged Parties or imported/legacy rows contain conflicting manual, unknown and rule-managed provenance;
- **WHEN** qualified source identity repair, migration or merge/Undo handles the affected assignment references;
- **THEN** Merge/import/legacy provenance conflicts preserve protected choices and source work; do not mark every surviving edge rule-managed or guess a primary.

#### Scenario: US27-A31 AC27-A17-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A17.

- **GIVEN** retained assignment data, old readers/writers, role collisions and unknown provenance have been inventoried;
- **WHEN** compatible constraints/readers are installed and resumable idempotent backfill and mixed-version transition are exercised;
- **THEN** Retained-data census and idempotent backfill preserve unknown/manual provenance and roles, cause no assignment/outward/access effects, and safely handle old/new readers and writers.

#### Scenario: US27-A31 AC27-A12-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A12, REQ27-A17.

- **GIVEN** a bounded pilot has accepted new assignments and protected manual choices under the new model;
- **WHEN** operators disable automatic acceptance, roll back compatible components and restore retained state;
- **THEN** Pilot disable/kill switch, mixed-version rollback and restore after new writes retain history/manual protections, fence stale work and preserve independent obligations.

### Requirement: US27-A32 — Trace every accepted assignment rule and proof outcome to the formal owners and release evidence

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an implementation reviewer, I want to trace every accepted assignment rule and proof outcome to the formal owners and release evidence, so that I can verify complete scope without confusing specification publication with a working feature. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-A18.

#### Scenario: US27-A32 AC27-A18-01

**Requirements:** REQ27-A18.

- **GIVEN** the ratified decision, glossary, formal owners and this specification are versioned;
- **WHEN** an independent reviewer follows every D30 requirement and acceptance outcome through design, future delivery work and release evidence;
- **THEN** Trace Q30 → D30 → glossary → exact owner/OpenSpec amendments → design/tasks/issues → source/security/concurrency/UI tests → release evidence, distinguishing selected/reviewed/ratified/implemented/qualified states.

### Requirement: US27-Q01 — One authoritative command boundary

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized operator, I want one authoritative command boundary, so that each fact has one accountable source. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-Q01.

#### Scenario: US27-Q01 AC27-Q01-01

- **GIVEN** a staff actor uses either a list, detail, board or later qualified projection.

- **WHEN** the same business change is submitted.

- **THEN** the same owner validation and durable outcome apply and there is one authoritative history.

#### Scenario: US27-Q01 AC27-Q01-02

- **GIVEN** a dependent source is unavailable or not qualified.

- **WHEN** a consumer attempts a source-owned effect.

- **THEN** the dependent action reports unavailable with safe recovery, makes no substitute fact and leaves unrelated valid work usable.

### Requirement: US27-Q02 — Current authority across all paths

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized operator, I want current authority across all paths, so that my work never exposes or changes another scope. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-Q02.

#### Scenario: US27-Q02 AC27-Q02-01

- **GIVEN** two tenants contain similar identities and a staff actor supplies a foreign ID or forged attribution.

- **WHEN** any list, command, job or receipt is requested.

- **THEN** no foreign value or effect is returned, counts do not disclose it and accepted audit attribution comes only from the authenticated source.

#### Scenario: US27-Q02 AC27-Q02-02

- **GIVEN** a user loses access after submit but before job execution or receipt recovery.

- **WHEN** the delayed path runs.

- **THEN** current policy denies the forbidden effect or disclosure while preserving lawful already accepted work.

### Requirement: US27-Q03 — Database constraints and effective RLS

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a delivery owner, I want database constraints and effective RLS, so that invalid and unauthorized data cannot bypass the interface. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-Q03.

#### Scenario: US27-Q03 AC27-Q03-01

- **GIVEN** an authenticated user can edit a permitted record.

- **WHEN** they attempt to change tenant, author, scope or a reference to an ineligible row through table, RPC, upsert or view.

- **THEN** each bypass is rejected atomically by the actual deployed policies/constraints or qualified source boundary.

#### Scenario: US27-Q03 AC27-Q03-02

- **GIVEN** a privileged job or definer runs.

- **WHEN** it attempts an out-of-scope write or disclosure.

- **THEN** the actual capability ceiling denies it and the test verifies grants, policies and function configuration rather than SQL strings alone.

### Requirement: US27-Q04 — Durable effects and truthful retries

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized operator, I want durable effects and truthful retries, so that recovery preserves one truthful business effect. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-Q04.

#### Scenario: US27-Q04 AC27-Q04-01

- **GIVEN** the source commits but the response is lost.

- **WHEN** the actor retries the same operation.

- **THEN** one business effect/history entry/required work intent exists and the permitted original result is recovered.

#### Scenario: US27-Q04 AC27-Q04-02

- **GIVEN** source success precedes failed task or notification projection.

- **WHEN** recovery executes twice.

- **THEN** source success remains true, one required effect is visible after recovery and no extra task/message is created.

### Requirement: US27-Q05 — Concurrent intent and temporal correctness

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized operator, I want concurrent intent and temporal correctness, so that another edit cannot silently erase my intended change. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-Q05.

#### Scenario: US27-Q05 AC27-Q05-01

- **GIVEN** two real database sessions edit the same source revision.

- **WHEN** their commits are deliberately interleaved.

- **THEN** at most the permitted serialized intent wins and the other gets a recoverable conflict without losing authorized draft input.

#### Scenario: US27-Q05 AC27-Q05-02

- **GIVEN** a backdated or corrected encounter, gift or decision arrives.

- **WHEN** current projections recompute.

- **THEN** history preserves its original and corrected meaning and recorded time does not masquerade as occurrence time.

### Requirement: US27-Q06 — Complete permission-safe projections

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized operator, I want complete permission-safe projections, so that the same privacy and coverage apply everywhere. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-Q06.

#### Scenario: US27-Q06 AC27-Q06-01

- **GIVEN** two viewers have different source permissions and share a cached filter.

- **WHEN** both open list, counts, brief, export and history.

- **THEN** each receives only their current permitted facts and no cache or aggregate expands disclosure.

#### Scenario: US27-Q06 AC27-Q06-02

- **GIVEN** only one page is loaded or financial coverage is missing.

- **WHEN** a total or coverage label renders.

- **THEN** the complete qualified total is used or the limitation is explicit; it never reports the page sum as complete or unknown as zero.

### Requirement: US27-Q07 — Operational follow-through without coercion

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized operator, I want operational follow-through without coercion, so that necessary work remains visible without coercive reporting. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-Q07.

#### Scenario: US27-Q07 AC27-Q07-01

- **GIVEN** an active partnership completes or cancels its last valid next task.

- **WHEN** the source outcome is rendered.

- **THEN** a valid replacement or owned actionable exception is visible and the record is not falsely healthy.

#### Scenario: US27-Q07 AC27-Q07-02

- **GIVEN** a missionary adds an eligible voluntary update without an active staff enrollment.

- **WHEN** the update is accepted.

- **THEN** no enrollment, compulsory task, quota, coaching obligation or performance penalty is created.

### Requirement: US27-Q08 — Native integration independence

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized operator, I want native integration independence, so that ordinary development works without optional products. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-Q08.

#### Scenario: US27-Q08 AC27-Q08-01

- **GIVEN** optional Workflow Studio and Web Studio/Eve products are unavailable.

- **WHEN** staff complete assignment, admission, Request, response and follow-up.

- **THEN** the qualified native workflow succeeds without opening a diagram or enabling optional products.

#### Scenario: US27-Q08 AC27-Q08-02

- **GIVEN** a later configured workflow requests the same source-purpose follow-up.

- **WHEN** native and configured work both retry.

- **THEN** one intended task/message exists; a separately authorized different purpose remains independent.

### Requirement: US27-Q09 — Clear composed interface and accessible alternatives

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized operator, I want a clear composed interface and accessible alternatives, so that I can understand and complete every supported flow. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-Q09.

#### Scenario: US27-Q09 AC27-Q09-01

- **GIVEN** a keyboard or assistive-technology user performs the ordinary and failure journeys.

- **WHEN** they change a stage, review a Request, manage an appeal and recover a conflict.

- **THEN** every control and result is understandable without drag/color alone, focus is usable and authorized input is retained.

#### Scenario: US27-Q09 AC27-Q09-02

- **GIVEN** a small-screen or slow-connection user saves.

- **WHEN** the network delays, fails or returns an ambiguous response.

- **THEN** pending state is truthful, repeated taps cannot duplicate effects and the next recovery action is clear.

### Requirement: US27-Q10 — International and sensitive context

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized operator, I want international and sensitive context, so that international and sensitive context stays accurate and private. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-Q10.

#### Scenario: US27-Q10 AC27-Q10-01

- **GIVEN** a record uses long Unicode/RTL names, zero- or three-decimal currency and date-only history.

- **WHEN** it is edited, viewed and exported through allowed paths.

- **THEN** identity and exact quantities survive with readable labels and no fabricated address, date or currency conversion.

#### Scenario: US27-Q10 AC27-Q10-02

- **GIVEN** a source becomes restricted or reaches its governed retention action.

- **WHEN** briefs, files, exports and cached recovery are requested.

- **THEN** current restriction propagates to all derivatives while lawful audit and correction follow the owner policy.

### Requirement: US27-Q11 — Migration and safe activation

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a delivery owner, I want migration and safe activation, so that retained work survives upgrades and failures. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-Q11.

#### Scenario: US27-Q11 AC27-Q11-01

- **GIVEN** legacy rows contain unknown role provenance, unmapped scope and protected notes.

- **WHEN** migration resumes after a simulated crash.

- **THEN** each retained source maps once or remains an owned explicit exception with no transfer, disclosure, duplicate effect or invented fact.

#### Scenario: US27-Q11 AC27-Q11-02

- **GIVEN** new-version writes have occurred.

- **WHEN** the feature is disabled and old jobs attempt execution.

- **THEN** incompatible writers are fenced, accepted history and handling obligations remain accessible through current authority and a tested safe recovery path exists.

### Requirement: US27-Q12 — Measured capacity and bounded work

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a delivery owner, I want measured capacity and bounded work, so that admitted workloads have proved operational limits. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-Q12.

#### Scenario: US27-Q12 AC27-Q12-01

- **GIVEN** a release profile omits a numeric bound, source coverage or supported-version measurement.

- **WHEN** activation readiness is evaluated.

- **THEN** the affected profile fails qualification with the missing evidence named.

#### Scenario: US27-Q12 AC27-Q12-02

- **GIVEN** the declared maximum tenant and an overloaded tenant run concurrently.

- **WHEN** representative reads, writes and recovery execute.

- **THEN** measured budgets hold for admitted tenants or overload is visibly bounded without lost accepted work, unauthorized fallback or starvation.

### Requirement: US27-Q13 — Separate business evidence and operational signals

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized operator, I want separate business evidence and operational signals, so that I can explain and safely recover what happened. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-Q13.

#### Scenario: US27-Q13 AC27-Q13-01

- **GIVEN** a command partially completes downstream or a batch has mixed results.

- **WHEN** an authorized operator investigates.

- **THEN** the operator can distinguish accepted source effects, pending/rejected work and safe next actions without reading private narrative logs.

#### Scenario: US27-Q13 AC27-Q13-02

- **GIVEN** a declared monitor breaches its threshold.

- **WHEN** its runbook is exercised.

- **THEN** the assigned owner receives the signal and performs the named containment/reconciliation response preserving source history.

### Requirement: US27-Q14 — Confirmed test seam and independently verifiable stories

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a delivery owner, I want a confirmed test seam and independently verifiable stories, so that each delivered outcome has meaningful proof. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-Q14.

#### Scenario: US27-Q14 AC27-Q14-01

- **GIVEN** an acceptance run accidentally uses demo auth, fake source storage or a missing required owner.

- **WHEN** suite preflight runs.

- **THEN** it fails and cannot be reported as real integration evidence.

#### Scenario: US27-Q14 AC27-Q14-02

- **GIVEN** an implementation claims a story complete.

- **WHEN** its mapped tests and source/provider qualification are reviewed.

- **THEN** the external outcome, persistence and relevant denial/failure/race cases are proved on the admitted version; documentary checks alone do not close it.

### Requirement: US27-Q15 — Financial owner gates E7 and C-01

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As an authorized operator, I want financial owner gates E7 and C-01, so that unresolved source policy cannot turn into invented financial truth. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-Q15.

#### Scenario: US27-Q15 AC27-Q15-01

- **GIVEN** E7 attribution meaning is unresolved.

- **WHEN** an affected appeal asks for received-toward-goal.

- **THEN** the measure is explicitly unavailable, no live/frozen assumption is made, and independent work remains usable.

#### Scenario: US27-Q15 AC27-Q15-02

- **GIVEN** a reviewed Request or finance-intake record is used as authority to correct a posted gift before C-01 resolution.

- **WHEN** the dependent command is proposed.

- **THEN** that lane remains blocked by the financial owner, without manufacturing correction approval.

### Requirement: US27-Q16 — Truthful release evidence and staff pilot

The system SHALL provide this observable outcome: As a delivery owner, I want truthful release evidence and a staff pilot, so that release readiness is supported by actual user and runtime evidence. The complete normative contracts are REQ27-Q16.

#### Scenario: US27-Q16 AC27-Q16-01

- **GIVEN** pilot participants mistake a planning response or physical gift report for received money, private notes for shared content or an assignment for access.

- **WHEN** the pilot is reviewed.

- **THEN** expansion stops, the flow/copy is corrected and the affected journey is retested.

#### Scenario: US27-Q16 AC27-Q16-02

- **GIVEN** only specification formatting, trace and OpenSpec validation have passed.

- **WHEN** the work is reported.

- **THEN** the report says specified/published and keeps implementation, provider, runtime, usability and activation proof outstanding.
